 121 | vec3 oct_to_vec3(vec2 e) {
 122 | 	vec3 v = vec3(e.xy, 1.0 - abs(e.x) - abs(e.y));
 123 | 	float t = max(-v.z, 0.0);
 124 | 	v.xy += t * -sign(v.xy);
 125 | 	return normalize(v);
 126 | }
 127 | 
 128 | 
 129 | vec3 oct_to_vec3_with_border(vec2 uv, float border_size) {
 130 | 	
 131 | 	uv = (uv - 0.5) * (2.0 / border_size);
 132 | 	
 133 | 	
 134 | 	vec2 mask = step(vec2(1.0), abs(uv));
 135 | 	uv = 2.0 * clamp(uv, -1.0, 1.0) - uv;
 136 | 	uv = mix(uv, -uv, mask.yx);
 137 | 	return oct_to_vec3(uv);
 138 | }
 139 | 
 140 | vec2 oct_wrap(vec2 v) {
 141 | 	vec2 signVal;
 142 | 	signVal.x = v.x >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : -1.0;
 143 | 	signVal.y = v.y >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : -1.0;
 144 | 	return (1.0 - abs(v.yx)) * signVal;
 145 | }
 146 | 
 147 | vec2 vec3_to_oct(vec3 n) {
 148 | 	
 149 | 	n /= (abs(n.x) + abs(n.y) + abs(n.z));
 150 | 	n.xy = (n.z >= 0.0) ? n.xy : oct_wrap(n.xy);
 151 | 	n.xy = n.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;
 152 | 	return n.xy;
 153 | }
 154 | 
 155 | 
 156 | 
 157 | vec2 vec3_to_oct_with_border(vec3 n, vec2 border_size) {
 158 | 	vec2 uv = vec3_to_oct(n);
 159 | 	return uv * border_size.y + border_size.x;
 160 | }
 161 | 
 162 | float vec3_to_oct_lod(vec3 n_ddx, vec3 n_ddy, float pixel_size) {
 163 | 	
 164 | 	
 165 | 	float pixel_size_sqr = 4.0 * pixel_size * pixel_size;
 166 | 	float ddx = dot(n_ddx, n_ddx) / pixel_size_sqr;
 167 | 	float ddy = dot(n_ddy, n_ddy) / pixel_size_sqr;
 168 | 	float dd_sqr = max(ddx, ddy);
 169 | 	return 0.25 * log2(dd_sqr + 1e-6f);
 170 | }

 816 | vec4 normal_roughness_compatibility(vec4 p_normal_roughness) {
 817 | 	float roughness = p_normal_roughness.w;
 818 | 	if (roughness > 0.5) {
 819 | 		roughness = 1.0 - roughness;
 820 | 	}
 821 | 	roughness /= (127.0 / 255.0);
 822 | 	return vec4(normalize(p_normal_roughness.xyz * 2.0 - 1.0) * 0.5 + 0.5, roughness);
 823 | }

 829 | }

 884 | #ifdef USE_LIGHTMAP
 885 | 
 886 | float w0(float a) {
 887 | 	return (1.0 / 6.0) * (a * (a * (-a + 3.0) - 3.0) + 1.0);
 888 | }
 889 | 
 890 | float w1(float a) {
 891 | 	return (1.0 / 6.0) * (a * a * (3.0 * a - 6.0) + 4.0);
 892 | }
 893 | 
 894 | float w2(float a) {
 895 | 	return (1.0 / 6.0) * (a * (a * (-3.0 * a + 3.0) + 3.0) + 1.0);
 896 | }
 897 | 
 898 | float w3(float a) {
 899 | 	return (1.0 / 6.0) * (a * a * a);
 900 | }
 901 | 
 902 | 
 903 | float g0(float a) {
 904 | 	return w0(a) + w1(a);
 905 | }
 906 | 
 907 | float g1(float a) {
 908 | 	return w2(a) + w3(a);
 909 | }
 910 | 
 911 | 
 912 | float h0(float a) {
 913 | 	return -1.0 + w1(a) / (w0(a) + w1(a));
 914 | }
 915 | 
 916 | float h1(float a) {
 917 | 	return 1.0 + w3(a) / (w2(a) + w3(a));
 918 | }
 919 | 
 920 | vec4 textureArray_bicubic(texture2DArray tex, vec3 uv, vec2 texture_size) {
 921 | 	vec2 texel_size = vec2(1.0) / texture_size;
 922 | 
 923 | 	uv.xy = uv.xy * texture_size + vec2(0.5);
 924 | 
 925 | 	vec2 iuv = floor(uv.xy);
 926 | 	vec2 fuv = fract(uv.xy);
 927 | 
 928 | 	float g0x = g0(fuv.x);
 929 | 	float g1x = g1(fuv.x);
 930 | 	float h0x = h0(fuv.x);
 931 | 	float h1x = h1(fuv.x);
 932 | 	float h0y = h0(fuv.y);
 933 | 	float h1y = h1(fuv.y);
 934 | 
 935 | 	vec2 p0 = (vec2(iuv.x + h0x, iuv.y + h0y) - vec2(0.5)) * texel_size;
 936 | 	vec2 p1 = (vec2(iuv.x + h1x, iuv.y + h0y) - vec2(0.5)) * texel_size;
 937 | 	vec2 p2 = (vec2(iuv.x + h0x, iuv.y + h1y) - vec2(0.5)) * texel_size;
 938 | 	vec2 p3 = (vec2(iuv.x + h1x, iuv.y + h1y) - vec2(0.5)) * texel_size;
 939 | 
 940 | 	return (g0(fuv.y) * (g0x * texture(sampler2DArray(tex, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), vec3(p0, uv.z)) + g1x * texture(sampler2DArray(tex, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), vec3(p1, uv.z)))) +
 941 | 			(g1(fuv.y) * (g0x * texture(sampler2DArray(tex, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), vec3(p2, uv.z)) + g1x * texture(sampler2DArray(tex, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), vec3(p3, uv.z))));
 942 | }
 943 | #endif 
 944 | 
 945 | #ifdef USE_MULTIVIEW
 946 | #extension GL_EXT_multiview : enable

 948 | vec3 multiview_uv(vec2 uv) {
 949 | 	return vec3(uv, ViewIndex);
 950 | }
 951 | ivec3 multiview_uv(ivec2 uv) {
 952 | 	return ivec3(uv, int(ViewIndex));
 953 | }
 954 | layout(location = 11) in vec4 combined_projected;
 955 | #else 
 956 | #define ViewIndex 0
 957 | vec2 multiview_uv(vec2 uv) {
 958 | 	return uv;
 959 | }
 960 | ivec2 multiview_uv(ivec2 uv) {
 961 | 	return uv;
 962 | }
 963 | #endif

 991 | /* clang-format off */
 992 | layout(set = MATERIAL_UNIFORM_SET, binding = 0, std140) uniform MaterialUniforms {
 993 | highp float m_surface_detail_strength;
 994 | highp float m_surface_detail_fade_m;
 995 | highp vec4 m_grass_tint;
 996 | highp float m_grass_scale;
 997 | highp float m_turf_patch_strength;
 998 | highp float m_turf_patch_freq;
 999 | highp vec4 m_turf_dry_tint;
1000 | highp vec4 m_turf_lush_tint;
1001 | highp vec2 m_turf_dry_band;
1002 | highp vec2 m_turf_lush_band;
1003 | highp vec4 m_tint;

1008 | layout(set = 3, binding = 1) uniform highp texture2D m_dirt_texture;
1009 | layout(set = 3, binding = 2) uniform highp texture2D m_grass_texture;
1010 | layout(location=15) in highp vec3 m_world_position;
1011 | 
1012 | vec3 m_cloudreach_detail_normal(vec3 m_at, vec3 m_normal_world, float m_height_value)
1013 | 	{
1014 | 		vec3 m_n=normalize(m_normal_world);
1015 | 		vec3 m_dx=dFdx(m_at);
1016 | 		vec3 m_dy=dFdy(m_at);
1017 | 		vec3 m_rx=cross(m_dy, m_n);
1018 | 		vec3 m_ry=cross(m_n, m_dx);
1019 | 		float m_determinant=dot(m_dx, m_rx);
1020 | 		vec3 m_gradient=((sign(m_determinant) * ((dFdx(m_height_value) * m_rx) + (dFdy(m_height_value) * m_ry))) / max(abs(m_determinant), 0.000001));
1021 | 		float m_fade=(1.0 - smoothstep((material.m_surface_detail_fade_m * 0.25), material.m_surface_detail_fade_m, max(length(m_dx), length(m_dy))));
1022 | return normalize((m_n - ((m_gradient * material.m_surface_detail_strength) * m_fade)));	}
1023 | 
1024 | float m_cloudreach_turf_patch(vec2 m_xz)
1025 | 	{
1026 | 		vec2 m_p=(m_xz * material.m_turf_patch_freq);
1027 | 		float m_a=(sin((m_p.x + (sin((m_p.y * 1.37000000476837)) * 1.70000004768372))) * sin(((m_p.y * 0.91000002622604) + (sin((m_p.x * 1.12999999523163)) * 1.29999995231628))));
1028 | 		float m_b=(sin((((m_p.x * 2.30999994277954) + 1.89999997615814) + (sin((m_p.y * 2.70000004768372)) * 1.10000002384186))) * sin((((m_p.y * 2.0699999332428) + 0.69999998807907) + (sin((m_p.x * 1.89999997615814)) * 1.20000004768372))));
1029 | return ((m_a * 0.72000002861023) + (m_b * 0.28000000119209));	}
1030 | 
1031 | vec3 m_cloudreach_turf(vec3 m_at)
1032 | 	{
1033 | 		vec3 m_fine=texture(sampler2D(m_grass_texture, SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_ANISOTROPIC_REPEAT), (m_at.xz * material.m_grass_scale)).rgb;
1034 | 		vec3 m_turned=texture(sampler2D(m_grass_texture, SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_ANISOTROPIC_REPEAT), (((mat2(0.80000001192093,0.60000002384186,-0.60000002384186,0.80000001192093) * m_at.xz) * material.m_grass_scale) * 0.70999997854233)).rgb;
1035 | 		vec3 m_texture_value=mix(m_fine, m_turned, 0.34999999403954);
1036 | 		m_texture_value=mix(vec3(0.50999999046326,0.50999999046326,0.50999999046326), m_texture_value, 0.51999998092651);
1037 | 		float m_broad=(sin(((m_at.x * 0.02899999916553) + sin((m_at.z * 0.04100000113249)))) * 0.04500000178814);
1038 | 		vec3 m_tint=material.m_grass_tint.rgb;
1039 | 		if ((material.m_turf_patch_strength > 0.0))
1040 | 		{
1041 | 					{
1042 | 			float m_field=m_cloudreach_turf_patch(m_at.xz);
1043 | 			float m_dry=smoothstep(material.m_turf_dry_band.x, material.m_turf_dry_band.y, m_field);
1044 | 			float m_lush=smoothstep(material.m_turf_lush_band.x, material.m_turf_lush_band.y, -m_field);
1045 | 			material.m_tint=mix(material.m_tint, material.m_turf_dry_tint.rgb, (m_dry * material.m_turf_patch_strength));
1046 | 			material.m_tint=mix(material.m_tint, material.m_turf_lush_tint.rgb, (m_lush * material.m_turf_patch_strength));
1047 | 		}
1048 | ;
1049 | 		}
1050 | return ((m_texture_value * material.m_tint) * (1.0 + m_broad));	}
1051 |

1089 | #ifdef ALPHA_HASH_USED
1090 | 
1091 | float hash_2d(vec2 p) {
1092 | 	return fract(1.0e4 * sin(17.0 * p.x + 0.1 * p.y) *
1093 | 			(0.1 + abs(sin(13.0 * p.y + p.x))));
1094 | }
1095 | 
1096 | float hash_3d(vec3 p) {
1097 | 	return hash_2d(vec2(hash_2d(p.xy), p.z));
1098 | }
1099 | 
1100 | half compute_alpha_hash_threshold(vec3 pos, float hash_scale) {
1101 | 	vec3 dx = dFdx(pos);
1102 | 	vec3 dy = dFdy(pos);
1103 | 
1104 | 	float delta_max_sqr = max(length(dx), length(dy));
1105 | 	float pix_scale = 1.0 / (hash_scale * delta_max_sqr);
1106 | 
1107 | 	vec2 pix_scales =
1108 | 			vec2(exp2(floor(log2(pix_scale))), exp2(ceil(log2(pix_scale))));
1109 | 
1110 | 	vec2 a_thresh = vec2(hash_3d(floor(pix_scales.x * pos.xyz)),
1111 | 			hash_3d(floor(pix_scales.y * pos.xyz)));
1112 | 
1113 | 	float lerp_factor = fract(log2(pix_scale));
1114 | 
1115 | 	float a_interp = (1.0 - lerp_factor) * a_thresh.x + lerp_factor * a_thresh.y;
1116 | 
1117 | 	float min_lerp = min(lerp_factor, 1.0 - lerp_factor);
1118 | 
1119 | 	vec3 cases = vec3(a_interp * a_interp / (2.0 * min_lerp * (1.0 - min_lerp)),
1120 | 			(a_interp - 0.5 * min_lerp) / (1.0 - min_lerp),
1121 | 			1.0 - ((1.0 - a_interp) * (1.0 - a_interp) / (2.0 * min_lerp * (1.0 - min_lerp))));
1122 | 
1123 | 	float alpha_hash_threshold =
1124 | 			(a_interp < (1.0 - min_lerp)) ? ((a_interp < min_lerp) ? cases.x : cases.y) : cases.z;
1125 | 
1126 | 	return half(clamp(alpha_hash_threshold, 0.00001, 1.0));
1127 | }
1128 | 
1129 | #endif 
1130 | 
1131 | #ifdef ALPHA_ANTIALIASING_EDGE_USED
1132 | 
1133 | half calc_mip_level(vec2 texture_coord) {
1134 | 	vec2 dx = dFdx(texture_coord);
1135 | 	vec2 dy = dFdy(texture_coord);
1136 | 	float delta_max_sqr = max(dot(dx, dx), dot(dy, dy));
1137 | 	return max(half(0.0), half(0.5) * half(log2(delta_max_sqr)));
1138 | }
1139 | 
1140 | half compute_alpha_antialiasing_edge(half input_alpha, vec2 texture_coord, half alpha_edge) {
1141 | 	input_alpha *= half(1.0) + calc_mip_level(texture_coord) * half(0.25);
1142 | 	input_alpha = (input_alpha - alpha_edge) / max(fwidth(input_alpha), half(0.0001)) + half(0.5);
1143 | 	return clamp(input_alpha, half(0.0), half(1.0));
1144 | }
1145 | 
1146 | #endif

1160 | 
1161 | #define M_PI 3.14159265359
1162 | #define M_TAU 6.28318530718
1163 | 
1164 | float acos_approx(float p_x) {
1165 | 	float x = abs(p_x);
1166 | 	float res = -0.156583f * x + (M_PI / 2.0);
1167 | 	res *= sqrt(1.0f - x);
1168 | 	return (p_x >= 0) ? res : M_PI - res;
1169 | }
1170 | 
1171 | vec3 fetch_ltc_lod(vec2 uv, vec4 texture_rect, float lod, float max_mipmap, texture2D area_light_atlas, sampler texture_sampler) {
1172 | 	float low = min(max(floor(lod), 0.0), max_mipmap - 1.0);
1173 | 	float high = min(max(floor(lod + 1.0), 1.0), max_mipmap);
1174 | 	vec2 sample_pos = texture_rect.xy + clamp(uv, 0.0, 1.0) * texture_rect.zw; 
1175 | 	vec4 sample_col_low = textureLod(sampler2D(area_light_atlas, texture_sampler), sample_pos, low);
1176 | 	vec4 sample_col_high = textureLod(sampler2D(area_light_atlas, texture_sampler), sample_pos, high);
1177 | 
1178 | 	float blend = high - clamp(lod, high - 1.0, high);
1179 | 	vec4 sample_col = mix(sample_col_high, sample_col_low, blend);
1180 | 	return sample_col.rgb * sample_col.a; 
1181 | }
1182 | 
1183 | vec3 integrate_edge_hill(vec3 p0, vec3 p1) {
1184 | 	
1185 | 	
1186 | 	float cosTheta = dot(p0, p1);
1187 | 
1188 | 	float x = cosTheta;
1189 | 	float y = abs(x);
1190 | 	float a = 5.42031 + (3.12829 + 0.0902326 * y) * y;
1191 | 	float b = 3.45068 + (4.18814 + y) * y;
1192 | 	float theta_sintheta = a / b;
1193 | 
1194 | 	if (x < 0.0) {
1195 | 		theta_sintheta = M_PI * inversesqrt(1.0 - x * x) - theta_sintheta; 
1196 | 	}
1197 | 	return theta_sintheta * cross(p0, p1);
1198 | }
1199 | 
1200 | float integrate_edge(vec3 p_proj0, vec3 p_proj1, vec3 p0, vec3 p1) {
1201 | 	float epsilon = 0.00001;
1202 | 	bool opposite_sides = dot(p_proj0, p_proj1) < -1.0 + epsilon;
1203 | 	if (opposite_sides) {
1204 | 		
1205 | 		vec3 half_point_t = p0 + normalize(p1 - p0) * dot(p0, normalize(p0 - p1));
1206 | 		vec3 half_point = normalize(half_point_t);
1207 | 
1208 | 		
1209 | 		float theta_sintheta = 0.0;
1210 | 		{
1211 | 			float cosTheta = dot(p_proj0, half_point);
1212 | 
1213 | 			float x = cosTheta;
1214 | 			float y = abs(x);
1215 | 			float a = 5.42031 + (3.12829 + 0.0902326 * y) * y;
1216 | 			float b = 3.45068 + (4.18814 + y) * y;
1217 | 			theta_sintheta = a / b;
1218 | 			if (x < 0.0) {
1219 | 				theta_sintheta = M_PI * inversesqrt(1.0 - x * x) - theta_sintheta; 
1220 | 			}
1221 | 		}
1222 | 		float aa = (theta_sintheta * cross(p_proj0, half_point)).y;
1223 | 		{
1224 | 			float cosTheta = dot(half_point, p_proj1);
1225 | 
1226 | 			float x = cosTheta;
1227 | 			float y = abs(x);
1228 | 			float a = 5.42031 + (3.12829 + 0.0902326 * y) * y;
1229 | 			float b = 3.45068 + (4.18814 + y) * y;
1230 | 			theta_sintheta = a / b;
1231 | 			if (x < 0.0) {
1232 | 				theta_sintheta = M_PI * inversesqrt(1.0 - x * x) - theta_sintheta; 
1233 | 			}
1234 | 		}
1235 | 		float bb = (theta_sintheta * cross(half_point, p_proj1)).y;
1236 | 		return aa + bb;
1237 | 	} else {
1238 | 		
1239 | 		float cosTheta = dot(p_proj0, p_proj1);
1240 | 
1241 | 		float x = cosTheta;
1242 | 		float y = abs(x);
1243 | 		float a = 5.42031 + (3.12829 + 0.0902326 * y) * y;
1244 | 		float b = 3.45068 + (4.18814 + y) * y;
1245 | 		float theta_sintheta = a / b;
1246 | 
1247 | 		if (x < 0.0) {
1248 | 			theta_sintheta = M_PI * inversesqrt(1.0 - x * x) - theta_sintheta; 
1249 | 		}
1250 | 		return (theta_sintheta * cross(p_proj0, p_proj1)).y;
1251 | 	}
1252 | }
1253 | 
1254 | vec3 fetch_ltc_filtered_texture_with_form_factor(vec4 texture_rect, vec3 L[4], float max_mipmap, texture2D area_light_atlas, sampler texture_sampler) {
1255 | 	vec3 L0 = normalize(L[0]);
1256 | 	vec3 L1 = normalize(L[1]);
1257 | 	vec3 L2 = normalize(L[2]);
1258 | 	vec3 L3 = normalize(L[3]);
1259 | 
1260 | 	vec3 F = vec3(0.0); 
1261 | 	F += integrate_edge_hill(L0, L1);
1262 | 	F += integrate_edge_hill(L1, L2);
1263 | 	F += integrate_edge_hill(L2, L3);
1264 | 	F += integrate_edge_hill(L3, L0);
1265 | 
1266 | 	vec2 uv;
1267 | 	float lod = 0.0;
1268 | 
1269 | 	if (dot(F, F) < 1e-16) {
1270 | 		uv = vec2(0.5);
1271 | 		lod = max_mipmap;
1272 | 	} else {
1273 | 		vec3 lx = L[1] - L[0];
1274 | 		vec3 ly = L[3] - L[0];
1275 | 		vec3 ln = cross(lx, ly);
1276 | 
1277 | 		float dist_x_area = dot(L[0], ln);
1278 | 		float d = dist_x_area / dot(F, ln);
1279 | 		vec3 isec = d * F;
1280 | 		vec3 li = isec - L[0]; 
1281 | 
1282 | 		float dot_lxy = dot(lx, ly);
1283 | 		float inv_dot_lxlx = 1.0 / dot(lx, lx);
1284 | 		vec3 ly_ = vec3(ly - lx * dot_lxy * inv_dot_lxlx); 
1285 | 		uv.y = dot(vec3(li), ly_) / dot(ly_, ly_);
1286 | 		uv.x = dot(vec3(li), lx) * inv_dot_lxlx - dot_lxy * inv_dot_lxlx * uv.y;
1287 | 
1288 | 		lod = abs(dist_x_area) / pow(dot(ln, ln), 0.75);
1289 | 		lod = log(2048.0 * lod) / log(3.0);
1290 | 	}
1291 | 	return fetch_ltc_lod(vec2(1.0) - uv, texture_rect, lod, max_mipmap, area_light_atlas, texture_sampler);
1292 | }
1293 | 
1294 | 
1295 | float quad_solid_angle(vec3 L[4]) {
1296 | 	
1297 | 	
1298 | 	vec3 c1 = cross(L[0], L[1]);
1299 | 	vec3 c2 = cross(L[1], L[2]);
1300 | 	vec3 c3 = cross(L[2], L[3]);
1301 | 	vec3 c4 = cross(L[3], L[0]);
1302 | 	vec3 n0 = normalize(c1);
1303 | 	vec3 n1 = normalize(c2);
1304 | 	vec3 n2 = normalize(c3);
1305 | 	vec3 n3 = normalize(c4);
1306 | 	float g0 = acos(clamp(dot(-n0, n1), -1.0, 1.0));
1307 | 	float g1 = acos(clamp(dot(-n1, n2), -1.0, 1.0));
1308 | 	float g2 = acos(clamp(dot(-n2, n3), -1.0, 1.0));
1309 | 	float g3 = acos(clamp(dot(-n3, n0), -1.0, 1.0));
1310 | 
1311 | 	float angle_sum = g0 + g1 + g2 + g3;
1312 | 
1313 | 	return clamp(angle_sum - M_TAU, 0.0, M_TAU);
1314 | }
1315 | 
1316 | void clip_quad_to_horizon(inout vec3 L[5], out int vertex_count) {
1317 | 	
1318 | 	int config = 0;
1319 | 	if (L[0].y > 0.0) {
1320 | 		config += 1;
1321 | 	}
1322 | 	if (L[1].y > 0.0) {
1323 | 		config += 2;
1324 | 	}
1325 | 	if (L[2].y > 0.0) {
1326 | 		config += 4;
1327 | 	}
1328 | 	if (L[3].y > 0.0) {
1329 | 		config += 8;
1330 | 	}
1331 | 
1332 | 	
1333 | 	vertex_count = 0;
1334 | 
1335 | 	if (config == 0) {
1336 | 		
1337 | 	} else if (config == 1) { 
1338 | 		vertex_count = 3;
1339 | 		L[1] = -L[1].y * L[0] + L[0].y * L[1];
1340 | 		L[2] = -L[3].y * L[0] + L[0].y * L[3];
1341 | 	} else if (config == 2) { 
1342 | 		vertex_count = 3;
1343 | 		L[0] = -L[0].y * L[1] + L[1].y * L[0];
1344 | 		L[2] = -L[2].y * L[1] + L[1].y * L[2];
1345 | 	} else if (config == 3) { 
1346 | 		vertex_count = 4;
1347 | 		L[2] = -L[2].y * L[1] + L[1].y * L[2];
1348 | 		L[3] = -L[3].y * L[0] + L[0].y * L[3];
1349 | 	} else if (config == 4) { 
1350 | 		vertex_count = 3;
1351 | 		L[0] = -L[3].y * L[2] + L[2].y * L[3];
1352 | 		L[1] = -L[1].y * L[2] + L[2].y * L[1];
1353 | 	} else if (config == 5) { 
1354 | 		vertex_count = 0;
1355 | 	} else if (config == 6) { 
1356 | 		vertex_count = 4;
1357 | 		L[0] = -L[0].y * L[1] + L[1].y * L[0];
1358 | 		L[3] = -L[3].y * L[2] + L[2].y * L[3];
1359 | 	} else if (config == 7) { 
1360 | 		vertex_count = 5;
1361 | 		L[4] = -L[3].y * L[0] + L[0].y * L[3];
1362 | 		L[3] = -L[3].y * L[2] + L[2].y * L[3];
1363 | 	} else if (config == 8) { 
1364 | 		vertex_count = 3;
1365 | 		L[0] = -L[0].y * L[3] + L[3].y * L[0];
1366 | 		L[1] = -L[2].y * L[3] + L[3].y * L[2];
1367 | 		L[2] = L[3];
1368 | 	} else if (config == 9) { 
1369 | 		vertex_count = 4;
1370 | 		L[1] = -L[1].y * L[0] + L[0].y * L[1];
1371 | 		L[2] = -L[2].y * L[3] + L[3].y * L[2];
1372 | 	} else if (config == 10) { 
1373 | 		vertex_count = 0;
1374 | 	} else if (config == 11) { 
1375 | 		vertex_count = 5;
1376 | 		L[4] = L[3];
1377 | 		L[3] = -L[2].y * L[3] + L[3].y * L[2];
1378 | 		L[2] = -L[2].y * L[1] + L[1].y * L[2];
1379 | 	} else if (config == 12) { 
1380 | 		vertex_count = 4;
1381 | 		L[1] = -L[1].y * L[2] + L[2].y * L[1];
1382 | 		L[0] = -L[0].y * L[3] + L[3].y * L[0];
1383 | 	} else if (config == 13) { 
1384 | 		vertex_count = 5;
1385 | 		L[4] = L[3];
1386 | 		L[3] = L[2];
1387 | 		L[2] = -L[1].y * L[2] + L[2].y * L[1];
1388 | 		L[1] = -L[1].y * L[0] + L[0].y * L[1];
1389 | 	} else if (config == 14) { 
1390 | 		vertex_count = 5;
1391 | 		L[4] = -L[0].y * L[3] + L[3].y * L[0];
1392 | 		L[0] = -L[0].y * L[1] + L[1].y * L[0];
1393 | 	} else if (config == 15) { 
1394 | 		vertex_count = 4;
1395 | 	}
1396 | 
1397 | 	if (vertex_count == 3) {
1398 | 		L[3] = L[0];
1399 | 	}
1400 | 	if (vertex_count == 4) {
1401 | 		L[4] = L[0];
1402 | 	}
1403 | }
1404 | 
1405 | float ltc_integrate_clipped_quad(vec3 L[5], vec3 L_proj[5], int vertices_above_horizon) {
1406 | 	float I;
1407 | 	I = integrate_edge(L_proj[0], L_proj[1], L[0], L[1]);
1408 | 	I += integrate_edge(L_proj[1], L_proj[2], L[1], L[2]);
1409 | 	I += integrate_edge(L_proj[2], L_proj[3], L[2], L[3]);
1410 | 	if (vertices_above_horizon >= 4) {
1411 | 		I += integrate_edge(L_proj[3], L_proj[4], L[3], L[4]);
1412 | 	}
1413 | 	if (vertices_above_horizon == 5) {
1414 | 		I += integrate_edge(L_proj[4], L_proj[0], L[4], L[0]);
1415 | 	}
1416 | 	return abs(I);
1417 | }
1418 | 
1419 | void ltc_evaluate(vec3 normal, vec3 eye_vec, mat3 M_inv, vec3 points[4], vec4 texture_rect, float max_mipmap, texture2D area_light_atlas, sampler texture_sampler, out float integral, out vec3 tex_color) {
1420 | 	
1421 | 	tex_color = vec3(1.0);
1422 | 	
1423 | 	vec3 x, z;
1424 | 	z = -normalize(eye_vec - normal * dot(eye_vec, normal)); 
1425 | 	x = cross(normal, z);
1426 | 
1427 | 	
1428 | 	M_inv = M_inv * transpose(mat3(x, normal, z));
1429 | 
1430 | 	vec3 L[5];
1431 | 	L[0] = M_inv * points[0];
1432 | 	L[1] = M_inv * points[1];
1433 | 	L[2] = M_inv * points[2];
1434 | 	L[3] = M_inv * points[3];
1435 | 
1436 | 	vec3 L_unclipped[4];
1437 | 	L_unclipped[0] = L[0];
1438 | 	L_unclipped[1] = L[1];
1439 | 	L_unclipped[2] = L[2];
1440 | 	L_unclipped[3] = L[3];
1441 | 
1442 | 	int n;
1443 | 	clip_quad_to_horizon(L, n);
1444 | 	if (n == 0) {
1445 | 		integral = 0.0;
1446 | 		return;
1447 | 	}
1448 | 
1449 | 	
1450 | 	vec3 L_proj[5];
1451 | 	L_proj[0] = normalize(L[0]);
1452 | 	L_proj[1] = normalize(L[1]);
1453 | 	L_proj[2] = normalize(L[2]);
1454 | 	L_proj[3] = normalize(L[3]);
1455 | 	L_proj[4] = normalize(L[4]);
1456 | 
1457 | 	if (texture_rect != vec4(0.0)) {
1458 | 		tex_color = vec3(fetch_ltc_filtered_texture_with_form_factor(texture_rect, L_unclipped, max_mipmap, area_light_atlas, texture_sampler));
1459 | 	}
1460 | 
1461 | 	
1462 | 	vec3 pnorm = normalize(cross(L_proj[0] - L_proj[1], L_proj[2] - L_proj[1]));
1463 | 	if (abs(dot(pnorm, L_proj[0])) < 1e-10) {
1464 | 		
1465 | 		
1466 | 		vec3 r10 = points[0] - points[1];
1467 | 		vec3 r12 = points[2] - points[1];
1468 | 		float alpha = -dot(points[1], r10) / dot(r10, r10);
1469 | 		float beta = -dot(points[1], r12) / dot(r12, r12);
1470 | 		if (0.0 < alpha && alpha < 1.0 && 0.0 < beta && beta < 1.0) { 
1471 | 			integral = 1.0;
1472 | 			return;
1473 | 		} else {
1474 | 			integral = 0.0;
1475 | 			return;
1476 | 		}
1477 | 	}
1478 | 
1479 | 	float I = ltc_integrate_clipped_quad(L, L_proj, n);
1480 | 	integral = I / (2.0 * M_PI);
1481 | }
1482 | 
1483 | void ltc_evaluate_specular(vec3 normal, vec3 eye_vec, float roughness, vec3 points[4], vec4 texture_rect, float max_mipmap, texture2D area_light_atlas, sampler texture_sampler, sampler2D ltc_lut1, sampler2D ltc_lut2, out float ltc_specular, out vec2 fresnel, out vec3 ltc_specular_tex_color) {
1484 | 	float theta = acos_approx(dot(normal, eye_vec));
1485 | 	const float LTC_LUT_SIZE = float(64.0);
1486 | 	vec2 lut_pos = vec2(max(roughness, float(0.02)), theta / float(0.5 * M_PI));
1487 | 	vec2 lut_uv = vec2(lut_pos * (float(63.0) / LTC_LUT_SIZE) + vec2(float(0.5) / LTC_LUT_SIZE)); 
1488 | 	vec4 M_brdf_abcd = texture(ltc_lut1, lut_uv);
1489 | 	vec3 M_brdf_e_mag_fres = texture(ltc_lut2, lut_uv).xyz;
1490 | 	float scale = 1.0 / (M_brdf_abcd.x * M_brdf_e_mag_fres.x - M_brdf_abcd.y * M_brdf_abcd.w);
1491 | 
1492 | 	mat3 M_inv = mat3(
1493 | 			vec3(0, 0, 1.0 / M_brdf_abcd.z),
1494 | 			vec3(-M_brdf_abcd.w * scale, M_brdf_abcd.x * scale, 0),
1495 | 			vec3(-M_brdf_e_mag_fres.x * scale, M_brdf_abcd.y * scale, 0));
1496 | 
1497 | 	ltc_evaluate(normal, eye_vec, M_inv, points, texture_rect, max_mipmap, area_light_atlas, texture_sampler, ltc_specular, ltc_specular_tex_color);
1498 | 	fresnel = vec2(M_brdf_e_mag_fres.yz);
1499 | }
1500 | 
1501 | void ltc_evaluate_diff(vec3 normal, vec3 points[4], vec4 texture_rect, float max_mipmap, texture2D area_light_atlas, sampler texture_sampler, out float integral, out vec3 tex_color) {
1502 | 	
1503 | 	tex_color = vec3(1.0);
1504 | 	
1505 | 	vec3 x, z;
1506 | 	vec3 eye_vec = abs(normal.z) < 0.7 ? vec3(0.0, 0.0, -1.0) : vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0);
1507 | 	z = -normalize(eye_vec - normal * dot(eye_vec, normal)); 
1508 | 	x = cross(normal, z);
1509 | 
1510 | 	
1511 | 	mat3 M_inv = transpose(mat3(x, normal, z));
1512 | 
1513 | 	vec3 L[5];
1514 | 	L[0] = M_inv * points[0];
1515 | 	L[1] = M_inv * points[1];
1516 | 	L[2] = M_inv * points[2];
1517 | 	L[3] = M_inv * points[3];
1518 | 
1519 | 	vec3 L_unclipped[4];
1520 | 	L_unclipped[0] = L[0];
1521 | 	L_unclipped[1] = L[1];
1522 | 	L_unclipped[2] = L[2];
1523 | 	L_unclipped[3] = L[3];
1524 | 
1525 | 	int n;
1526 | 	clip_quad_to_horizon(L, n);
1527 | 	if (n == 0) {
1528 | 		integral = 0.0;
1529 | 		return;
1530 | 	}
1531 | 
1532 | 	
1533 | 	vec3 L_proj[5];
1534 | 	L_proj[0] = normalize(L[0]);
1535 | 	L_proj[1] = normalize(L[1]);
1536 | 	L_proj[2] = normalize(L[2]);
1537 | 	L_proj[3] = normalize(L[3]);
1538 | 	L_proj[4] = normalize(L[4]);
1539 | 
1540 | 	
1541 | 	vec3 pnorm = normalize(cross(L_proj[0] - L_proj[1], L_proj[2] - L_proj[1]));
1542 | 	if (abs(dot(pnorm, L_proj[0])) < 1e-10) {
1543 | 		
1544 | 		
1545 | 		integral = 0.0;
1546 | 		return;
1547 | 	}
1548 | 
1549 | 	if (texture_rect != vec4(0.0)) {
1550 | 		tex_color = fetch_ltc_filtered_texture_with_form_factor(texture_rect, L_unclipped, max_mipmap, area_light_atlas, texture_sampler);
1551 | 	}
1552 | 
1553 | 	float I = ltc_integrate_clipped_quad(L, L_proj, n);
1554 | 	integral = I; 
1555 | }
1556 | 
1557 | 
1558 | 
1559 | 
1560 | #ifdef UBERSHADER
1561 | 
1562 | #define SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION [[dont_unroll]]
1563 | #else
1564 | 
1565 | #define SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
1566 | #endif
1567 | 
1568 | half D_GGX(half NoH, half roughness, hvec3 n, hvec3 h) {
1569 | 	half a = NoH * roughness;
1570 | #ifdef EXPLICIT_FP16
1571 | 	hvec3 NxH = cross(n, h);
1572 | 	half k = roughness / (dot(NxH, NxH) + a * a);
1573 | #else
1574 | 	float k = roughness / (1.0 - NoH * NoH + a * a);
1575 | #endif
1576 | 	half d = k * k * half(1.0 / M_PI);
1577 | 	return saturateHalf(d);
1578 | }
1579 | 
1580 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
1581 | #ifdef SSS_MODE_SKIN
1582 | hvec3 SSS_skin(half NdotL, half transmittance_depth, half transmittance_z, half transmittance_boost, hvec4 transmittance_color, hvec3 light_color) {
1583 | 	half scale = half(8.25) / transmittance_depth;
1584 | 	half d = scale * abs(transmittance_z);
1585 | 	float dd = float(-d * d);
1586 | 	hvec3 profile = hvec3(vec3(0.233, 0.455, 0.649) * exp(dd / 0.0064) +
1587 | 			vec3(0.1, 0.336, 0.344) * exp(dd / 0.0484) +
1588 | 			vec3(0.118, 0.198, 0.0) * exp(dd / 0.187) +
1589 | 			vec3(0.113, 0.007, 0.007) * exp(dd / 0.567) +
1590 | 			vec3(0.358, 0.004, 0.0) * exp(dd / 1.99) +
1591 | 			vec3(0.078, 0.0, 0.0) * exp(dd / 7.41));
1592 | 
1593 | 	return profile * transmittance_color.a * light_color * clamp(transmittance_boost - NdotL, half(0.0), half(1.0)) * half(1.0 / M_PI);
1594 | }
1595 | #else
1596 | hvec3 SSS(half NdotL, half transmittance_depth, half transmittance_z, half transmittance_boost, hvec4 transmittance_color, hvec3 light_color) {
1597 | 	half scale = half(8.25) / transmittance_depth;
1598 | 	half d = scale * abs(transmittance_z);
1599 | 	half dd = -d * d;
1600 | 	return exp(dd) * transmittance_color.rgb * transmittance_color.a * light_color * clamp(transmittance_boost - NdotL, half(0.0), half(1.0)) * half(1.0 / M_PI);
1601 | }
1602 | #endif
1603 | #endif
1604 | 
1605 | 
1606 | half V_GGX(half NdotL, half NdotV, half alpha) {
1607 | 	half v = half(0.5) / mix(half(2.0) * NdotL * NdotV, NdotL + NdotV, alpha);
1608 | 	return saturateHalf(v);
1609 | }
1610 | 
1611 | half D_GGX_anisotropic(half cos_theta_m, half alpha_x, half alpha_y, half cos_phi, half sin_phi) {
1612 | 	half alpha2 = alpha_x * alpha_y;
1613 | 	vec3 v = vec3(alpha_y * cos_phi, alpha_x * sin_phi, alpha2 * cos_theta_m);
1614 | 	float v2 = dot(v, v);
1615 | 	half w2 = half(float(alpha2) / v2);
1616 | 	return alpha2 * w2 * w2 * half(1.0 / M_PI);
1617 | }
1618 | 
1619 | half V_GGX_anisotropic(half alpha_x, half alpha_y, half TdotV, half TdotL, half BdotV, half BdotL, half NdotV, half NdotL) {
1620 | 	half Lambda_V = NdotL * length(hvec3(alpha_x * TdotV, alpha_y * BdotV, NdotV));
1621 | 	half Lambda_L = NdotV * length(hvec3(alpha_x * TdotL, alpha_y * BdotL, NdotL));
1622 | 	half v = half(0.5) / (Lambda_V + Lambda_L);
1623 | 	return saturateHalf(v);
1624 | }
1625 | 
1626 | half SchlickFresnel(half u) {
1627 | 	half m = half(1.0) - u;
1628 | 	half m2 = m * m;
1629 | 	return m2 * m2 * m; 
1630 | }
1631 | 
1632 | hvec3 F0(half metallic, half specular, hvec3 albedo) {
1633 | 	half dielectric = half(0.16) * specular * specular;
1634 | 	
1635 | 	
1636 | 	return mix(hvec3(dielectric), albedo, hvec3(metallic));
1637 | }
1638 | 
1639 | half V_Kelemen(half LdotH) {
1640 | 	
1641 | 	return saturateHalf(half(0.25) / (LdotH * LdotH + half(1e-4)));
1642 | }
1643 | 
1644 | hvec3 f0_Clear_Coat_To_Surface(hvec3 f0) {
1645 | 	
1646 | 	
1647 | 	
1648 | 	return clamp(f0 * (f0 * (half(0.941892) - half(0.263008) * f0) + half(0.346479)) - half(0.0285998), half(0.0), half(1.0));
1649 | }
1650 | 
1651 | void light_compute(hvec3 N, hvec3 L, hvec3 V, half A, hvec3 light_color, bool is_directional, half attenuation, hvec3 f0, half roughness, half metallic, half specular_amount, hvec3 albedo, inout half alpha, vec2 screen_uv, hvec3 energy_compensation,
1652 | #ifdef LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED
1653 | 		hvec3 backlight,
1654 | #endif
1655 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
1656 | 		hvec4 transmittance_color,
1657 | 		half transmittance_depth,
1658 | 		half transmittance_boost,
1659 | 		half transmittance_z,
1660 | #endif
1661 | #ifdef LIGHT_RIM_USED
1662 | 		half rim, half rim_tint,
1663 | #endif
1664 | #ifdef LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED
1665 | 		half clearcoat, half clearcoat_roughness, hvec3 vertex_normal,
1666 | #endif
1667 | #ifdef LIGHT_ANISOTROPY_USED
1668 | 		hvec3 B, hvec3 T, half anisotropy,
1669 | #endif
1670 | 		inout hvec3 diffuse_light, inout hvec3 specular_light) {
1671 | #if defined(LIGHT_CODE_USED)
1672 | 	
1673 | 	mat4 inv_view_matrix = transpose(mat4(scene_data_block.data.inv_view_matrix[0],
1674 | 			scene_data_block.data.inv_view_matrix[1],
1675 | 			scene_data_block.data.inv_view_matrix[2],
1676 | 			vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)));
1677 | 	mat4 read_view_matrix = transpose(mat4(scene_data_block.data.view_matrix[0],
1678 | 			scene_data_block.data.view_matrix[1],
1679 | 			scene_data_block.data.view_matrix[2],
1680 | 			vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)));
1681 | 
1682 | #ifdef USING_MOBILE_RENDERER
1683 | 	uint instance_index = draw_call.instance_index;
1684 | #else
1685 | 	uint instance_index = instance_index_interp;
1686 | #endif
1687 | 
1688 | 	mat4 read_model_matrix = transpose(mat4(instances.data[instance_index].transform[0],
1689 | 			instances.data[instance_index].transform[1],
1690 | 			instances.data[instance_index].transform[2],
1691 | 			vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)));
1692 | 
1693 | #undef projection_matrix
1694 | #define projection_matrix scene_data_block.data.projection_matrix
1695 | #undef inv_projection_matrix
1696 | #define inv_projection_matrix scene_data_block.data.inv_projection_matrix
1697 | 
1698 | 	vec2 read_viewport_size = scene_data_block.data.viewport_size;
1699 | 
1700 | #ifdef LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED
1701 | 	vec3 backlight_highp = vec3(backlight);
1702 | #endif
1703 | 	float roughness_highp = float(roughness);
1704 | 	float metallic_highp = float(metallic);
1705 | 	vec3 albedo_highp = vec3(albedo);
1706 | 	float alpha_highp = float(alpha);
1707 | 	vec3 normal_highp = vec3(N);
1708 | 	vec3 light_highp = vec3(L);
1709 | 	vec3 view_highp = vec3(V);
1710 | 	float specular_amount_highp = float(specular_amount);
1711 | 	vec3 light_color_highp = vec3(light_color);
1712 | 	float attenuation_highp = float(attenuation);
1713 | 	vec3 diffuse_light_highp = vec3(diffuse_light);
1714 | 	vec3 specular_light_highp = vec3(specular_light);
1715 | 	bool is_area = false;
1716 | 	float area_diffuse = 1.0;
1717 | 	float area_specular = 1.0;
1718 | 	vec3 area_diffuse_tex_color = vec3(1.0);
1719 | 	vec3 area_specular_tex_color = vec3(1.0);
1720 | 
1721 | 
1722 | 	alpha = half(alpha_highp);
1723 | 	diffuse_light = hvec3(diffuse_light_highp);
1724 | 	specular_light = hvec3(specular_light_highp);
1725 | #else 
1726 | 	half NdotL = min(A + dot(N, L), half(1.0));
1727 | 	half cNdotV = max(dot(N, V), half(1e-4));
1728 | 
1729 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
1730 | 	{
1731 | #ifdef SSS_MODE_SKIN
1732 | 		diffuse_light += SSS_skin(NdotL, transmittance_depth, transmittance_z, transmittance_boost, transmittance_color, light_color);
1733 | #else
1734 | 		diffuse_light += SSS(NdotL, transmittance_depth, transmittance_z, transmittance_boost, transmittance_color, light_color);
1735 | #endif
1736 | 	}
1737 | #endif 
1738 | 
1739 | #if defined(LIGHT_RIM_USED)
1740 | 	
1741 | 	half rim_light = pow(max(half(1e-4), half(1.0) - cNdotV), max(half(0.0), (half(1.0) - roughness) * half(16.0)));
1742 | 	diffuse_light += rim_light * rim * mix(hvec3(1.0), albedo, rim_tint) * light_color;
1743 | #endif
1744 | 
1745 | 	half cc_attenuation = half(1.0);
1746 | 
1747 | 	
1748 | 	if (is_directional || attenuation > HALF_FLT_MIN) {
1749 | 		half cNdotL = max(NdotL, half(0.0));
1750 | #if defined(DIFFUSE_BURLEY) || defined(SPECULAR_SCHLICK_GGX) || defined(LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED)
1751 | 		hvec3 H = normalize(V + L);
1752 | 		half cLdotH = clamp(A + dot(L, H), half(0.0), half(1.0));
1753 | #endif
1754 | 
1755 | #if defined(LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED)
1756 | 		
1757 | 		half ccNdotL = clamp(A + dot(vertex_normal, L), half(0.0), half(1.0));
1758 | 		half ccNdotH = clamp(A + dot(vertex_normal, H), half(0.0), half(1.0));
1759 | 		half cLdotH5 = SchlickFresnel(cLdotH);
1760 | 
1761 | 		half Dr = D_GGX(ccNdotH, half(mix(half(0.001), half(0.1), clearcoat_roughness)), vertex_normal, H);
1762 | 		half Gr = V_Kelemen(cLdotH);
1763 | 		half Fr = mix(half(0.04), half(1.0), cLdotH5) * clearcoat;
1764 | 		cc_attenuation = half(1.0) - Fr;
1765 | 		half clearcoat_specular_brdf_NL = Dr * Gr * Fr * ccNdotL;
1766 | 		specular_light += clearcoat_specular_brdf_NL * light_color * attenuation * specular_amount;
1767 | #endif 
1768 | 
1769 | 		if (metallic < half(1.0)) {
1770 | 			half diffuse_brdf_NL; 
1771 | 
1772 | #if defined(DIFFUSE_LAMBERT_WRAP)
1773 | 			
1774 | 			
1775 | 			half op_roughness = half(1.0) + roughness;
1776 | 			diffuse_brdf_NL = max(half(0.0), (NdotL + roughness) / (op_roughness * op_roughness)) * half(1.0 / M_PI);
1777 | #elif defined(DIFFUSE_TOON)
1778 | 
1779 | 			diffuse_brdf_NL = smoothstep(-roughness, max(roughness, half(0.01)), NdotL) * half(1.0 / M_PI);
1780 | 
1781 | #elif defined(DIFFUSE_BURLEY)
1782 | 			{
1783 | 				half FD90_minus_1 = half(2.0) * cLdotH * cLdotH * roughness - half(0.5);
1784 | 				half FdV = half(1.0) + FD90_minus_1 * SchlickFresnel(cNdotV);
1785 | 				half FdL = half(1.0) + FD90_minus_1 * SchlickFresnel(cNdotL);
1786 | 				diffuse_brdf_NL = half(1.0 / M_PI) * FdV * FdL * cNdotL;
1787 | 			}
1788 | #else
1789 | 			
1790 | 			diffuse_brdf_NL = cNdotL * half(1.0 / M_PI);
1791 | #endif
1792 | 
1793 | 			diffuse_light += light_color * diffuse_brdf_NL * attenuation * cc_attenuation;
1794 | 
1795 | #if defined(LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED)
1796 | 			diffuse_light += light_color * (hvec3(1.0 / M_PI) - diffuse_brdf_NL) * backlight * attenuation;
1797 | #endif
1798 | 		}
1799 | 
1800 | 		if (roughness > half(0.0)) {
1801 | #if defined(SPECULAR_SCHLICK_GGX)
1802 | 			half cNdotH = clamp(A + dot(N, H), half(0.0), half(1.0));
1803 | #endif
1804 | 			
1805 | 			
1806 | #if defined(SPECULAR_TOON)
1807 | 			hvec3 R = normalize(-reflect(L, N));
1808 | 			half RdotV = dot(R, V);
1809 | 			half mid = half(1.0) - roughness;
1810 | 			mid *= mid;
1811 | 			half intensity = smoothstep(mid - roughness * half(0.5), mid + roughness * half(0.5), RdotV) * mid;
1812 | 			diffuse_light += light_color * intensity * attenuation * specular_amount; 
1813 | 
1814 | #elif defined(SPECULAR_DISABLED)
1815 | 			
1816 | 
1817 | #elif defined(SPECULAR_SCHLICK_GGX)
1818 | 			
1819 | 			half alpha_ggx = roughness * roughness;
1820 | #if defined(LIGHT_ANISOTROPY_USED)
1821 | 			half aspect = sqrt(half(1.0) - anisotropy * half(0.9));
1822 | 			half ax = alpha_ggx / aspect;
1823 | 			half ay = alpha_ggx * aspect;
1824 | 			half XdotH = dot(T, H);
1825 | 			half YdotH = dot(B, H);
1826 | 			half D = D_GGX_anisotropic(cNdotH, ax, ay, XdotH, YdotH);
1827 | 			half G = V_GGX_anisotropic(ax, ay, dot(T, V), dot(T, L), dot(B, V), dot(B, L), cNdotV, cNdotL);
1828 | #else 
1829 | 			half D = D_GGX(cNdotH, alpha_ggx, N, H);
1830 | 			half G = V_GGX(cNdotL, cNdotV, alpha_ggx);
1831 | #endif 
1832 | 	   
1833 | #if !defined(LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED)
1834 | 			half cLdotH5 = SchlickFresnel(cLdotH);
1835 | #endif
1836 | 			
1837 | 			
1838 | 			half f90 = clamp(dot(f0, hvec3(50.0 * 0.33)), metallic, half(1.0));
1839 | 			hvec3 F = f0 + (f90 - f0) * cLdotH5;
1840 | 			hvec3 specular_brdf_NL = energy_compensation * cNdotL * D * F * G;
1841 | 			specular_light += specular_brdf_NL * light_color * attenuation * cc_attenuation * specular_amount;
1842 | #endif
1843 | 		}
1844 | 
1845 | #ifdef USE_SHADOW_TO_OPACITY
1846 | 		alpha = min(alpha, clamp(half(1.0 - attenuation), half(0.0), half(1.0)));
1847 | #endif
1848 | 	}
1849 | #endif 
1850 | }
1851 | 
1852 | #ifndef SHADOWS_DISABLED
1853 | 
1854 | 
1855 | 
1856 | float quick_hash(vec2 pos) {
1857 | 	const vec3 magic = vec3(0.06711056f, 0.00583715f, 52.9829189f);
1858 | 	return fract(magic.z * fract(dot(pos, magic.xy)));
1859 | }
1860 | 
1861 | half sample_directional_pcf_shadow(texture2D shadow, vec2 shadow_pixel_size, vec4 coord, float taa_frame_count) {
1862 | 	vec2 pos = coord.xy;
1863 | 	float depth = coord.z;
1864 | 
1865 | 	
1866 | 	if (sc_directional_soft_shadow_samples() == 0) {
1867 | 		return half(textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow, shadow_sampler), vec4(pos, depth, 1.0)));
1868 | 	}
1869 | 
1870 | 	mat2 disk_rotation;
1871 | 	{
1872 | 		float r = quick_hash(gl_FragCoord.xy + vec2(taa_frame_count * 5.588238)) * 2.0 * M_PI;
1873 | 		float sr = sin(r);
1874 | 		float cr = cos(r);
1875 | 		disk_rotation = mat2(vec2(cr, -sr), vec2(sr, cr));
1876 | 	}
1877 | 
1878 | 	float avg = 0.0;
1879 | 
1880 | 	SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
1881 | 	for (uint i = 0; i < sc_directional_soft_shadow_samples(); i++) {
1882 | 		avg += textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow, shadow_sampler), vec4(pos + shadow_pixel_size * (disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.directional_soft_shadow_kernel[i].xy), depth, 1.0));
1883 | 	}
1884 | 
1885 | 	return half(avg * (1.0 / float(sc_directional_soft_shadow_samples())));
1886 | }
1887 | 
1888 | half sample_pcf_shadow(texture2D shadow, vec2 shadow_pixel_size, vec3 coord, float taa_frame_count) {
1889 | 	vec2 pos = coord.xy;
1890 | 	float depth = coord.z;
1891 | 
1892 | 	
1893 | 	if (sc_soft_shadow_samples() == 0) {
1894 | 		return half(textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow, shadow_sampler), vec4(pos, depth, 1.0)));
1895 | 	}
1896 | 
1897 | 	mat2 disk_rotation;
1898 | 	{
1899 | 		float r = quick_hash(gl_FragCoord.xy + vec2(taa_frame_count * 5.588238)) * 2.0 * M_PI;
1900 | 		float sr = sin(r);
1901 | 		float cr = cos(r);
1902 | 		disk_rotation = mat2(vec2(cr, -sr), vec2(sr, cr));
1903 | 	}
1904 | 
1905 | 	float avg = 0.0;
1906 | 
1907 | 	SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
1908 | 	for (uint i = 0; i < sc_soft_shadow_samples(); i++) {
1909 | 		avg += textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow, shadow_sampler), vec4(pos + shadow_pixel_size * (disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.soft_shadow_kernel[i].xy), depth, 1.0));
1910 | 	}
1911 | 
1912 | 	return half(avg * (1.0 / float(sc_soft_shadow_samples())));
1913 | }
1914 | 
1915 | half sample_omni_pcf_shadow(texture2D shadow, float blur_scale, vec2 coord, vec4 uv_rect, vec2 flip_offset, float depth, float taa_frame_count) {
1916 | 	
1917 | 	if (sc_soft_shadow_samples() == 0) {
1918 | 		vec2 pos = coord * 0.5 + 0.5;
1919 | 		pos = uv_rect.xy + pos * uv_rect.zw;
1920 | 		return half(textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow, shadow_sampler), vec4(pos, depth, 1.0)));
1921 | 	}
1922 | 
1923 | 	mat2 disk_rotation;
1924 | 	{
1925 | 		float r = quick_hash(gl_FragCoord.xy + vec2(taa_frame_count * 5.588238)) * 2.0 * M_PI;
1926 | 		float sr = sin(r);
1927 | 		float cr = cos(r);
1928 | 		disk_rotation = mat2(vec2(cr, -sr), vec2(sr, cr));
1929 | 	}
1930 | 
1931 | 	float avg = 0.0;
1932 | 	vec2 offset_scale = blur_scale * 2.0 * scene_data_block.data.shadow_atlas_pixel_size / uv_rect.zw;
1933 | 
1934 | 	SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
1935 | 	for (uint i = 0; i < sc_soft_shadow_samples(); i++) {
1936 | 		vec2 offset = offset_scale * (disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.soft_shadow_kernel[i].xy);
1937 | 		vec2 sample_coord = coord + offset;
1938 | 
1939 | 		float sample_coord_length_squared = dot(sample_coord, sample_coord);
1940 | 		bool do_flip = sample_coord_length_squared > 1.0;
1941 | 
1942 | 		if (do_flip) {
1943 | 			float len = sqrt(sample_coord_length_squared);
1944 | 			sample_coord = sample_coord * (2.0 / len - 1.0);
1945 | 		}
1946 | 
1947 | 		sample_coord = sample_coord * 0.5 + 0.5;
1948 | 		sample_coord = uv_rect.xy + sample_coord * uv_rect.zw;
1949 | 
1950 | 		if (do_flip) {
1951 | 			sample_coord += flip_offset;
1952 | 		}
1953 | 		avg += textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow, shadow_sampler), vec4(sample_coord, depth, 1.0));
1954 | 	}
1955 | 
1956 | 	return half(avg * (1.0 / float(sc_soft_shadow_samples())));
1957 | }
1958 | 
1959 | half sample_directional_soft_shadow(texture2D shadow, vec3 pssm_coord, vec2 tex_scale, float taa_frame_count) {
1960 | 	
1961 | 	float blocker_count = 0.0;
1962 | 	float blocker_average = 0.0;
1963 | 
1964 | 	mat2 disk_rotation;
1965 | 	{
1966 | 		float r = quick_hash(gl_FragCoord.xy + vec2(taa_frame_count * 5.588238)) * 2.0 * M_PI;
1967 | 		float sr = sin(r);
1968 | 		float cr = cos(r);
1969 | 		disk_rotation = mat2(vec2(cr, -sr), vec2(sr, cr));
1970 | 	}
1971 | 
1972 | 	SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
1973 | 	for (uint i = 0; i < sc_directional_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
1974 | 		vec2 suv = pssm_coord.xy + (disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.directional_penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy) * tex_scale;
1975 | 		float d = textureLod(sampler2D(shadow, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), suv, 0.0).r;
1976 | 		if (d > pssm_coord.z) {
1977 | 			blocker_average += d;
1978 | 			blocker_count += 1.0;
1979 | 		}
1980 | 	}
1981 | 
1982 | 	if (blocker_count > 0.0) {
1983 | 		
1984 | 		blocker_average /= blocker_count;
1985 | 		float penumbra = (-pssm_coord.z + blocker_average) / (1.0 - blocker_average);
1986 | 		tex_scale *= penumbra;
1987 | 
1988 | 		float s = 0.0;
1989 | 
1990 | 		SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
1991 | 		for (uint i = 0; i < sc_directional_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
1992 | 			vec2 suv = pssm_coord.xy + (disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.directional_penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy) * tex_scale;
1993 | 			s += textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow, shadow_sampler), vec4(suv, pssm_coord.z, 1.0));
1994 | 		}
1995 | 
1996 | 		return half(s / float(sc_directional_penumbra_shadow_samples()));
1997 | 
1998 | 	} else {
1999 | 		
2000 | 		return half(1.0);
2001 | 	}
2002 | }
2003 | 
2004 | #endif 
2005 | 
2006 | half get_omni_attenuation(float distance, float inv_range, float decay) {
2007 | 	float nd = distance * inv_range;
2008 | 	nd *= nd;
2009 | 	nd *= nd; 
2010 | 	nd = max(1.0 - nd, 0.0);
2011 | 	nd *= nd; 
2012 | 	return half(nd * pow(max(distance, 0.0001), -decay));
2013 | }
2014 | 
2015 | void light_process_omni(uint idx, vec3 vertex, hvec3 eye_vec, hvec3 normal, vec3 vertex_ddx, vec3 vertex_ddy, hvec3 f0, half roughness, half metallic, float taa_frame_count, hvec3 albedo, inout half alpha, vec2 screen_uv, hvec3 energy_compensation,
2016 | #ifdef LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED
2017 | 		hvec3 backlight,
2018 | #endif
2019 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
2020 | 		hvec4 transmittance_color,
2021 | 		half transmittance_depth,
2022 | 		half transmittance_boost,
2023 | #endif
2024 | #ifdef LIGHT_RIM_USED
2025 | 		half rim, half rim_tint,
2026 | #endif
2027 | #ifdef LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED
2028 | 		half clearcoat, half clearcoat_roughness, hvec3 vertex_normal,
2029 | #endif
2030 | #ifdef LIGHT_ANISOTROPY_USED
2031 | 		hvec3 binormal, hvec3 tangent, half anisotropy,
2032 | #endif
2033 | 		inout hvec3 diffuse_light, inout hvec3 specular_light) {
2034 | 
2035 | 	
2036 | 	vec3 light_rel_vec = omni_lights.data[idx].position - vertex;
2037 | 	float light_length = length(light_rel_vec);
2038 | 	half omni_attenuation = get_omni_attenuation(light_length, omni_lights.data[idx].inv_radius, omni_lights.data[idx].attenuation);
2039 | 
2040 | 	
2041 | 	half size = half(0.0);
2042 | 	if (sc_use_light_soft_shadows() && omni_lights.data[idx].size > 0.0) {
2043 | 		half t = half(omni_lights.data[idx].size / max(0.001, light_length));
2044 | 		size = half(1.0) / sqrt(half(1.0) + t * t);
2045 | 		size = max(half(1.0) - size, half(0.0));
2046 | 	}
2047 | 
2048 | 	half shadow = half(1.0);
2049 | #ifndef SHADOWS_DISABLED
2050 | 	
2051 | 	if (omni_attenuation > HALF_FLT_MIN && omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity > 0.001) {
2052 | 		
2053 | 		vec2 texel_size = scene_data_block.data.shadow_atlas_pixel_size;
2054 | 		vec4 base_uv_rect = omni_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect;
2055 | 		base_uv_rect.xy += texel_size;
2056 | 		base_uv_rect.zw -= texel_size * 2.0;
2057 | 
2058 | 		
2059 | 		vec2 flip_offset = omni_lights.data[idx].direction.xy;
2060 | 
2061 | 		vec3 local_vert = (omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex, 1.0)).xyz;
2062 | 
2063 | 		float shadow_len = length(local_vert); 
2064 | 		vec3 shadow_dir = normalize(local_vert);
2065 | 
2066 | 		vec3 local_normal = normalize(mat3(omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix) * vec3(normal));
2067 | 		vec3 normal_bias = local_normal * omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_normal_bias * (1.0 - abs(dot(local_normal, shadow_dir)));
2068 | 
2069 | 		if (sc_use_light_soft_shadows() && omni_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size > 0.0) {
2070 | 			
2071 | 
2072 | 			
2073 | 
2074 | 			float blocker_count = 0.0;
2075 | 			float blocker_average = 0.0;
2076 | 
2077 | 			mat2 disk_rotation;
2078 | 			{
2079 | 				float r = quick_hash(gl_FragCoord.xy + vec2(taa_frame_count * 5.588238)) * 2.0 * M_PI;
2080 | 				float sr = sin(r);
2081 | 				float cr = cos(r);
2082 | 				disk_rotation = mat2(vec2(cr, -sr), vec2(sr, cr));
2083 | 			}
2084 | 
2085 | 			vec3 basis_normal = shadow_dir;
2086 | 			vec3 v0 = abs(basis_normal.z) < 0.999 ? vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0) : vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);
2087 | 			vec3 tangent = normalize(cross(v0, basis_normal));
2088 | 			vec3 bitangent = normalize(cross(tangent, basis_normal));
2089 | 			float z_norm = 1.0 - shadow_len * omni_lights.data[idx].inv_radius;
2090 | 
2091 | 			tangent *= omni_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size * omni_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_scale;
2092 | 			bitangent *= omni_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size * omni_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_scale;
2093 | 
2094 | 			SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
2095 | 			for (uint i = 0; i < sc_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
2096 | 				vec2 disk = disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy;
2097 | 
2098 | 				vec3 pos = local_vert + tangent * disk.x + bitangent * disk.y;
2099 | 
2100 | 				pos = normalize(pos);
2101 | 
2102 | 				vec4 uv_rect = base_uv_rect;
2103 | 
2104 | 				if (pos.z >= 0.0) {
2105 | 					uv_rect.xy += flip_offset;
2106 | 				}
2107 | 
2108 | 				pos.z = 1.0 + abs(pos.z);
2109 | 				pos.xy /= pos.z;
2110 | 
2111 | 				pos.xy = pos.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;
2112 | 				pos.xy = uv_rect.xy + pos.xy * uv_rect.zw;
2113 | 
2114 | 				float d = textureLod(sampler2D(shadow_atlas, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), pos.xy, 0.0).r;
2115 | 				if (d > z_norm) {
2116 | 					blocker_average += d;
2117 | 					blocker_count += 1.0;
2118 | 				}
2119 | 			}
2120 | 
2121 | 			if (blocker_count > 0.0) {
2122 | 				
2123 | 				blocker_average /= blocker_count;
2124 | 				float penumbra = (-z_norm + blocker_average) / (1.0 - blocker_average);
2125 | 				tangent *= penumbra;
2126 | 				bitangent *= penumbra;
2127 | 
2128 | 				z_norm += omni_lights.data[idx].inv_radius * omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_bias;
2129 | 
2130 | 				shadow = half(0.0);
2131 | 
2132 | 				SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
2133 | 				for (uint i = 0; i < sc_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
2134 | 					vec2 disk = disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy;
2135 | 					vec3 pos = local_vert + tangent * disk.x + bitangent * disk.y;
2136 | 
2137 | 					pos = normalize(pos);
2138 | 					pos = normalize(pos + normal_bias);
2139 | 
2140 | 					vec4 uv_rect = base_uv_rect;
2141 | 
2142 | 					if (pos.z >= 0.0) {
2143 | 						uv_rect.xy += flip_offset;
2144 | 					}
2145 | 
2146 | 					pos.z = 1.0 + abs(pos.z);
2147 | 					pos.xy /= pos.z;
2148 | 
2149 | 					pos.xy = pos.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;
2150 | 					pos.xy = uv_rect.xy + pos.xy * uv_rect.zw;
2151 | 					shadow += half(textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow_atlas, shadow_sampler), vec4(pos.xy, z_norm, 1.0)));
2152 | 				}
2153 | 
2154 | 				shadow /= half(sc_penumbra_shadow_samples());
2155 | 				shadow = mix(half(1.0), shadow, half(omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity));
2156 | 
2157 | 			} else {
2158 | 				
2159 | 				shadow = half(1.0);
2160 | 			}
2161 | 		} else {
2162 | 			vec4 uv_rect = base_uv_rect;
2163 | 
2164 | 			vec3 shadow_sample = normalize(shadow_dir + normal_bias);
2165 | 			if (shadow_sample.z >= 0.0) {
2166 | 				uv_rect.xy += flip_offset;
2167 | 				flip_offset *= -1.0;
2168 | 			}
2169 | 
2170 | 			shadow_sample.z = 1.0 + abs(shadow_sample.z);
2171 | 			vec2 pos = shadow_sample.xy / shadow_sample.z;
2172 | 			float depth = shadow_len - omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_bias;
2173 | 			depth *= omni_lights.data[idx].inv_radius;
2174 | 			depth = 1.0 - depth;
2175 | 			shadow = mix(half(1.0), sample_omni_pcf_shadow(shadow_atlas, omni_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_scale / shadow_sample.z, pos, uv_rect, flip_offset, depth, taa_frame_count), half(omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity));
2176 | 		}
2177 | 	}
2178 | #endif
2179 | 
2180 | 	vec3 color = omni_lights.data[idx].color;
2181 | 
2182 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
2183 | 	half transmittance_z = transmittance_depth; 
2184 | 	transmittance_color.a *= omni_attenuation;
2185 | #ifndef SHADOWS_DISABLED
2186 | 	if (omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity > 0.001) {
2187 | 		
2188 | 		vec2 texel_size = scene_data_block.data.shadow_atlas_pixel_size;
2189 | 		vec4 uv_rect = omni_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect;
2190 | 		uv_rect.xy += texel_size;
2191 | 		uv_rect.zw -= texel_size * 2.0;
2192 | 
2193 | 		
2194 | 		vec2 flip_offset = omni_lights.data[idx].direction.xy;
2195 | 
2196 | 		vec3 local_vert = (omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex - normal * omni_lights.data[idx].transmittance_bias, 1.0)).xyz;
2197 | 
2198 | 		float shadow_len = length(local_vert); 
2199 | 		vec3 shadow_sample = normalize(local_vert);
2200 | 
2201 | 		if (shadow_sample.z >= 0.0) {
2202 | 			uv_rect.xy += flip_offset;
2203 | 			flip_offset *= -1.0;
2204 | 		}
2205 | 
2206 | 		shadow_sample.z = 1.0 + abs(shadow_sample.z);
2207 | 		vec2 pos = shadow_sample.xy / shadow_sample.z;
2208 | 		float depth = shadow_len * omni_lights.data[idx].inv_radius;

2215 | 	}
2216 | #endif 
2217 | #endif 
2218 | 
2219 | 	if (sc_use_light_projector() && omni_lights.data[idx].projector_rect != vec4(0.0)) {

2227 | 		}

2230 |

2234 | 
2235 |

2238 | 
2239 | 		if (sc_projector_use_mipmaps()) {

2241 | 			vec2 proj_uv_ddy;
2242 | 			{
2243 | 				vec3 local_v_ddx = (omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex + vertex_ddx, 1.0)).xyz;
2244 | 				local_v_ddx = normalize(local_v_ddx);
2245 | 
2246 | 				if (local_v_ddx.z >= 0.0) {
2247 | 					local_v_ddx.z += 1.0;
2248 | 				} else {
2249 | 					local_v_ddx.z = 1.0 - local_v_ddx.z;
2250 | 				}
2251 |

2259 | 
2260 | 				if (local_v_ddy.z >= 0.0) {

2299 | #endif

2304 | vec2 normal_to_panorama(vec3 n) {
2305 | 	n = normalize(n);
2306 | 	vec2 panorama_coords = vec2(atan(n.x, n.z), acos(-n.y));
2307 | 
2308 | 	if (panorama_coords.x < 0.0) {
2309 | 		panorama_coords.x += M_PI * 2.0;
2310 | 	}
2311 | 
2312 | 	panorama_coords /= vec2(M_PI * 2.0, M_PI);
2313 | 	return panorama_coords;
2314 | }
2315 | 
2316 | void light_process_spot(uint idx, vec3 vertex, hvec3 eye_vec, hvec3 normal, vec3 vertex_ddx, vec3 vertex_ddy, hvec3 f0, half roughness, half metallic, float taa_frame_count, hvec3 albedo, inout half alpha, vec2 screen_uv, hvec3 energy_compensation,
2317 | #ifdef LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED
2318 | 		hvec3 backlight,
2319 | #endif
2320 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
2321 | 		hvec4 transmittance_color,
2322 | 		half transmittance_depth,
2323 | 		half transmittance_boost,
2324 | #endif
2325 | #ifdef LIGHT_RIM_USED
2326 | 		half rim, half rim_tint,
2327 | #endif
2328 | #ifdef LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED
2329 | 		half clearcoat, half clearcoat_roughness, hvec3 vertex_normal,
2330 | #endif
2331 | #ifdef LIGHT_ANISOTROPY_USED
2332 | 		hvec3 binormal, hvec3 tangent, half anisotropy,
2333 | #endif
2334 | 		inout hvec3 diffuse_light,
2335 | 		inout hvec3 specular_light) {
2336 | 
2337 | 	
2338 | 	vec3 light_rel_vec = spot_lights.data[idx].position - vertex;
2339 | 	float light_length = length(light_rel_vec);
2340 | 	hvec3 light_rel_vec_norm = hvec3(light_rel_vec / light_length);
2341 | 	half spot_attenuation = get_omni_attenuation(light_length, spot_lights.data[idx].inv_radius, spot_lights.data[idx].attenuation);
2342 | 	vec3 spot_dir = spot_lights.data[idx].direction;
2343 | 	float cone_angle = spot_lights.data[idx].cone_angle;
2344 | 	float scos = max(dot(-vec3(light_rel_vec_norm), spot_dir), cone_angle);
2345 | 
2346 | 	
2347 | 	float spot_rim = max(1e-4, (1.0 - scos) / (1.0 - cone_angle));
2348 | 	spot_attenuation *= half(1.0 - pow(spot_rim, spot_lights.data[idx].cone_attenuation));
2349 | 
2350 | 	
2351 | 	half size = half(0.0);
2352 | 	if (sc_use_light_soft_shadows() && spot_lights.data[idx].size > 0.0) {
2353 | 		half t = half(spot_lights.data[idx].size / max(0.001, light_length));
2354 | 		size = half(1.0) / sqrt(half(1.0) + t * t);
2355 | 		size = max(half(1.0) - size, half(0.0));
2356 | 	}
2357 | 
2358 | 	half shadow = half(1.0);
2359 | #ifndef SHADOWS_DISABLED
2360 | 	
2361 | 	if (spot_attenuation > HALF_FLT_MIN && spot_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity > 0.001) {
2362 | 		vec3 normal_bias = vec3(normal) * light_length * spot_lights.data[idx].shadow_normal_bias * (1.0 - abs(dot(normal, light_rel_vec_norm)));
2363 | 
2364 | 		
2365 | 		vec4 v = vec4(vertex + normal_bias, 1.0);
2366 | 
2367 | 		vec4 splane = (spot_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * v);
2368 | 		splane.z += spot_lights.data[idx].shadow_bias;
2369 | 		splane /= splane.w;
2370 | 
2371 | 		if (sc_use_light_soft_shadows() && spot_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size > 0.0) {
2372 | 			
2373 | 
2374 | 			
2375 | 			float z_norm = dot(spot_dir, -light_rel_vec) * spot_lights.data[idx].inv_radius;
2376 | 
2377 | 			vec2 shadow_uv = splane.xy * spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.zw + spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.xy;
2378 | 
2379 | 			float blocker_count = 0.0;
2380 | 			float blocker_average = 0.0;
2381 | 
2382 | 			mat2 disk_rotation;
2383 | 			{
2384 | 				float r = quick_hash(gl_FragCoord.xy + vec2(taa_frame_count * 5.588238)) * 2.0 * M_PI;
2385 | 				float sr = sin(r);
2386 | 				float cr = cos(r);
2387 | 				disk_rotation = mat2(vec2(cr, -sr), vec2(sr, cr));
2388 | 			}
2389 | 
2390 | 			float uv_size = spot_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size * z_norm * spot_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_scale;
2391 | 			vec2 clamp_max = spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.xy + spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.zw;
2392 | 
2393 | 			SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
2394 | 			for (uint i = 0; i < sc_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
2395 | 				vec2 suv = shadow_uv + (disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy) * uv_size;
2396 | 				suv = clamp(suv, spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.xy, clamp_max);
2397 | 				float d = textureLod(sampler2D(shadow_atlas, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), suv, 0.0).r;
2398 | 				if (d > splane.z) {
2399 | 					blocker_average += d;
2400 | 					blocker_count += 1.0;
2401 | 				}
2402 | 			}
2403 | 
2404 | 			if (blocker_count > 0.0) {
2405 | 				
2406 | 				blocker_average /= blocker_count;
2407 | 				float penumbra = (-z_norm + blocker_average) / (1.0 - blocker_average);
2408 | 				uv_size *= penumbra;
2409 | 
2410 | 				shadow = half(0.0);
2411 | 
2412 | 				SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
2413 | 				for (uint i = 0; i < sc_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
2414 | 					vec2 suv = shadow_uv + (disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy) * uv_size;
2415 | 					suv = clamp(suv, spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.xy, clamp_max);
2416 | 					shadow += half(textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow_atlas, shadow_sampler), vec4(suv, splane.z, 1.0)));
2417 | 				}
2418 | 
2419 | 				shadow /= half(sc_penumbra_shadow_samples());
2420 | 				shadow = mix(half(1.0), shadow, half(spot_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity));
2421 | 
2422 | 			} else {
2423 | 				
2424 | 				shadow = half(1.0);
2425 | 			}
2426 | 		} else {
2427 | 			
2428 | 			vec3 shadow_uv = vec3(splane.xy * spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.zw + spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.xy, splane.z);
2429 | 			shadow = mix(half(1.0), sample_pcf_shadow(shadow_atlas, spot_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_scale * scene_data_block.data.shadow_atlas_pixel_size, shadow_uv, taa_frame_count), half(spot_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity));
2430 | 		}
2431 | 	}
2432 | #endif 
2433 | 
2434 | 	vec3 color = spot_lights.data[idx].color;
2435 | 
2436 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
2437 | 	half transmittance_z = transmittance_depth;
2438 | 	transmittance_color.a *= spot_attenuation;
2439 | #ifndef SHADOWS_DISABLED
2440 | 	if (spot_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity > 0.001) {
2441 | 		vec4 splane = (spot_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex - vec3(normal) * spot_lights.data[idx].transmittance_bias, 1.0));
2442 | 		splane /= splane.w;
2443 | 
2444 | 		vec3 shadow_uv = vec3(splane.xy * spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.zw + spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.xy, splane.z);
2445 | 		float shadow_z = textureLod(sampler2D(shadow_atlas, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), shadow_uv.xy, 0.0).r;
2446 | 
2447 | 		shadow_z = shadow_z * 2.0 - 1.0;
2448 | 		float z_far = 1.0 / spot_lights.data[idx].inv_radius;
2449 | 		float z_near = 0.01;
2450 | 		shadow_z = 2.0 * z_near * z_far / (z_far + z_near - shadow_z * (z_far - z_near));
2451 | 
2452 | 		
2453 | 		float z = dot(spot_dir, -light_rel_vec);
2454 | 		transmittance_z = half(z - shadow_z);
2455 | 	}
2456 | #endif 
2457 | #endif 
2458 | 
2459 | 	if (sc_use_light_projector() && spot_lights.data[idx].projector_rect != vec4(0.0)) {
2460 | 		vec4 splane = (spot_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex, 1.0));
2461 | 		splane /= splane.w;
2462 | 
2463 | 		vec2 proj_uv = splane.xy * spot_lights.data[idx].projector_rect.zw;
2464 | 
2465 | 		if (sc_projector_use_mipmaps()) {
2466 | 			
2467 | 			vec4 splane_ddx = (spot_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex + vertex_ddx, 1.0));
2468 | 			splane_ddx /= splane_ddx.w;
2469 | 			vec2 proj_uv_ddx = splane_ddx.xy * spot_lights.data[idx].projector_rect.zw - proj_uv;
2470 | 
2471 | 			vec4 splane_ddy = (spot_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex + vertex_ddy, 1.0));
2472 | 			splane_ddy /= splane_ddy.w;
2473 | 			vec2 proj_uv_ddy = splane_ddy.xy * spot_lights.data[idx].projector_rect.zw - proj_uv;
2474 | 
2475 | 			vec4 proj = textureGrad(sampler2D(decal_atlas_srgb, light_projector_sampler), proj_uv + spot_lights.data[idx].projector_rect.xy, proj_uv_ddx, proj_uv_ddy);
2476 | 			color *= proj.rgb * proj.a;
2477 | 		} else {
2478 | 			vec4 proj = textureLod(sampler2D(decal_atlas_srgb, light_projector_sampler), proj_uv + spot_lights.data[idx].projector_rect.xy, 0.0);
2479 | 			color *= proj.rgb * proj.a;
2480 | 		}
2481 | 	}
2482 | 
2483 | 	light_compute(normal, hvec3(light_rel_vec_norm), eye_vec, size, hvec3(color), false, spot_attenuation * shadow, f0, roughness, metallic, half(spot_lights.data[idx].specular_amount), albedo, alpha, screen_uv, energy_compensation,
2484 | #ifdef LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED
2485 | 			backlight,
2486 | #endif
2487 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
2488 | 			transmittance_color,
2489 | 			transmittance_depth,
2490 | 			transmittance_boost,
2491 | 			transmittance_z,
2492 | #endif
2493 | #ifdef LIGHT_RIM_USED
2494 | 			rim * spot_attenuation, rim_tint,
2495 | #endif
2496 | #ifdef LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED
2497 | 			clearcoat, clearcoat_roughness, vertex_normal,
2498 | #endif
2499 | #ifdef LIGHT_ANISOTROPY_USED
2500 | 			binormal, tangent, anisotropy,
2501 | #endif
2502 | 
2503 | 			diffuse_light, specular_light);
2504 | }

2506 | 
2507 | void light_process_area(uint idx, vec3 vertex, hvec3 eye_vec, hvec3 normal, vec3 vertex_ddx, vec3 vertex_ddy, hvec3 f0, half roughness, half metallic, float taa_frame_count, hvec3 albedo, inout half alpha, vec2 screen_uv, hvec3 energy_compensation,
2508 | #ifdef LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED
2509 | 		hvec3 backlight,
2510 | #endif
2511 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
2512 | 		hvec4 transmittance_color,
2513 | 		half transmittance_depth,
2514 | 		half transmittance_boost,
2515 | #endif
2516 | #ifdef LIGHT_RIM_USED
2517 | 		half rim, half rim_tint,
2518 | #endif
2519 | #ifdef LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED
2520 | 		half clearcoat, half clearcoat_roughness, hvec3 vertex_normal,
2521 | #endif

2524 | #endif
2525 | 		inout hvec3 diffuse_light, inout hvec3 specular_light) {
2526 | 	half EPSILON = half(1e-7);
2527 | 	hvec3 area_width = hvec3(area_lights.data[idx].area_width);
2528 | 	hvec3 area_height = hvec3(area_lights.data[idx].area_height);
2529 | 
2530 | 	if (dot(area_width, area_width) < EPSILON || dot(area_height, area_height) < EPSILON) { 
2531 | 		return;
2532 | 	}
2533 | 
2534 | 	if (dot(area_lights.data[idx].direction, vertex - area_lights.data[idx].position) <= 0) {
2535 | 		return; 
2536 | 	}
2537 | 
2538 | 	half a_len = length(area_width);
2539 | 	half b_len = length(area_height);
2540 | 	half a_half_len = a_len / half(2.0);
2541 | 	half b_half_len = b_len / half(2.0);
2542 | 	hvec3 light_center = hvec3(area_lights.data[idx].position);
2543 | 	hvec3 light_to_vert = hvec3(vertex) - light_center;
2544 | 	hvec3 area_a_dir = normalize(area_width);
2545 | 	hvec3 area_b_dir = normalize(area_height);
2546 | 	hvec3 area_direction = hvec3(area_lights.data[idx].direction);
2547 | 	hvec3 pos_local_to_light = hvec3(dot(light_to_vert, area_a_dir), dot(light_to_vert, area_b_dir), dot(light_to_vert, -area_direction));
2548 | 
2549 | 	hvec3 closest_point_local_to_light = hvec3(clamp(pos_local_to_light.x, -a_half_len, a_half_len), clamp(pos_local_to_light.y, -b_half_len, b_half_len), 0);
2550 | 	half dist = length(closest_point_local_to_light - pos_local_to_light);
2551 | 
2552 | 	half light_length = max(half(0.0), dist);
2553 | 	half light_attenuation_raw = get_omni_attenuation(float(light_length), area_lights.data[idx].inv_radius, area_lights.data[idx].attenuation);
2554 | 	half light_attenuation_ltc = light_attenuation_raw * half(light_length * light_length); 
2555 | 	half shadow = half(1.0);
2556 | 
2557 | #ifndef SHADOWS_DISABLED
2558 | 	
2559 | 	if (light_attenuation_raw > HALF_FLT_MIN && area_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity > 0.001) {
2560 | 		
2561 | 		vec2 texel_size = scene_data_block.data.shadow_atlas_pixel_size;
2562 | 		vec4 base_uv_rect = area_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect;
2563 | 		base_uv_rect.xy += texel_size;
2564 | 		base_uv_rect.zw -= texel_size * 2.0;
2565 | 
2566 | 		vec3 local_vert = (area_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex, 1.0)).xyz;
2567 | 
2568 | 		float shadow_len = length(local_vert); 
2569 | 		vec3 shadow_dir = normalize(local_vert);
2570 | 
2571 | 		vec3 local_normal = normalize(mat3(area_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix) * vec3(normal));
2572 | 		vec3 normal_bias = local_normal * area_lights.data[idx].shadow_normal_bias * (1.0 - abs(dot(local_normal, shadow_dir)));
2573 | 
2574 | 		float inv_center_range = area_lights.data[idx].cone_attenuation;
2575 | 
2576 | 		if (sc_use_light_soft_shadows() && area_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size > 0.0) {
2577 | 			
2578 | 
2579 | 			
2580 | 
2581 | 			float blocker_count = 0.0;
2582 | 			float blocker_average = 0.0;
2583 | 
2584 | 			mat2 disk_rotation;
2585 | 			{
2586 | 				float r = quick_hash(gl_FragCoord.xy + vec2(taa_frame_count * 5.588238)) * 2.0 * M_PI;
2587 | 				float sr = sin(r);
2588 | 				float cr = cos(r);
2589 | 				disk_rotation = mat2(vec2(cr, -sr), vec2(sr, cr));
2590 | 			}
2591 | 
2592 | 			vec3 basis_normal = shadow_dir;
2593 | 			vec3 v0 = abs(basis_normal.z) < 0.999 ? vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0) : vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);
2594 | 			vec3 tangent = normalize(cross(v0, basis_normal));
2595 | 			vec3 bitangent = normalize(cross(tangent, basis_normal));
2596 | 			float z_norm = 1.0 - shadow_len * inv_center_range;
2597 | 
2598 | 			tangent *= area_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size * area_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_scale;
2599 | 			bitangent *= area_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size * area_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_scale;
2600 | 
2601 | 			SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
2602 | 			for (uint i = 0; i < sc_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
2603 | 				vec2 disk = disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy;
2604 | 
2605 | 				vec3 pos = local_vert + tangent * disk.x + bitangent * disk.y;
2606 | 
2607 | 				pos = normalize(pos);
2608 | 
2609 | 				vec4 uv_rect = base_uv_rect;
2610 | 
2611 | 				pos.z = 1.0 + abs(pos.z);
2612 | 				pos.xy /= pos.z;
2613 | 
2614 | 				pos.xy = pos.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;
2615 | 				pos.xy = uv_rect.xy + pos.xy * uv_rect.zw;
2616 |

2618 | 				if (d > z_norm) {
2619 | 					blocker_average += d;
2620 | 					blocker_count += 1.0;
2621 | 				}
2622 | 			}
2623 | 
2624 | 			if (blocker_count > 0.0) {
2625 | 				
2626 | 				blocker_average /= blocker_count;
2627 | 				float penumbra = (-z_norm + blocker_average) / (1.0 - blocker_average);
2628 | 				tangent *= penumbra;
2629 | 				bitangent *= penumbra;
2630 | 
2631 | 				z_norm += inv_center_range * area_lights.data[idx].shadow_bias;
2632 | 
2633 | 				shadow = half(0.0);
2634 | 
2635 | 				SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
2636 | 				for (uint i = 0; i < sc_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
2637 | 					vec2 disk = disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy;
2638 | 					vec3 pos = local_vert + tangent * disk.x + bitangent * disk.y;
2639 | 
2640 | 					pos = normalize(pos);
2641 | 					pos = normalize(pos + normal_bias);
2642 | 
2643 | 					vec4 uv_rect = base_uv_rect;
2644 | 
2645 | 					pos.z = 1.0 + abs(pos.z);
2646 | 					pos.xy /= pos.z;
2647 | 
2648 | 					pos.xy = pos.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;
2649 | 					pos.xy = uv_rect.xy + pos.xy * uv_rect.zw;
2650 | 					shadow += half(textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow_atlas, shadow_sampler), vec4(pos.xy, z_norm, 1.0)));
2651 | 				}
2652 | 
2653 | 				shadow /= half(sc_penumbra_shadow_samples());

2655 | 
2656 | 			} else {
2657 |

2659 | 			}
2660 | 		} else {
2661 | 			vec4 uv_rect = base_uv_rect;

2664 |

2668 | 			depth *= inv_center_range;

2670 | 			shadow = mix(half(1.0), sample_omni_pcf_shadow(shadow_atlas, area_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_scale / shadow_sample.z, pos, uv_rect, vec2(0), depth, taa_frame_count), half(area_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity));
2671 | 		}
2672 | 	}
2673 | #endif
2674 | 	light_attenuation_ltc = light_attenuation_ltc * shadow;
2675 | 	half light_attenuation = light_attenuation_raw * shadow;
2676 | 	hvec3 color = hvec3(area_lights.data[idx].color);
2677 | 	float max_mipmap = area_lights.data[idx].cone_angle;
2678 |

2776 | 			
2777 | 			vec2 texel_size = scene_data_block.data.shadow_atlas_pixel_size;
2778 | 			vec4 uv_rect = area_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect;
2779 | 			uv_rect.xy += texel_size;
2780 | 			uv_rect.zw -= texel_size * 2.0;
2781 | 			vec3 local_vert = (area_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex - vec3(normal) * area_lights.data[idx].transmittance_bias, 1.0)).xyz;
2782 | 
2783 | 			float shadow_len = length(local_vert); 
2784 | 			vec3 shadow_sample = normalize(local_vert);
2785 | 
2786 | 			shadow_sample.z = 1.0 + abs(shadow_sample.z);
2787 | 			vec2 pos = shadow_sample.xy / shadow_sample.z;
2788 | 			float inv_center_range = area_lights.data[idx].cone_attenuation;
2789 | 			float depth = shadow_len * inv_center_range;
2790 | 			depth = 1.0 - depth;
2791 | 
2792 | 			pos = pos * 0.5 + 0.5;
2793 | 			pos = uv_rect.xy + pos * uv_rect.zw;
2794 | 			float shadow_z = textureLod(sampler2D(shadow_atlas, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), pos, 0.0).r;
2795 | 			transmittance_z = half((depth - shadow_z) / inv_center_range);
2796 | 		}
2797 | #endif
2798 | 		
2799 | #ifdef SSS_MODE_SKIN
2800 | 		diffuse_light += SSS_skin(half(ltc_diffuse), transmittance_depth, transmittance_z, transmittance_boost, transmittance_color, color * isotropic_light_color * area);
2801 | #else
2802 | 		diffuse_light += SSS(half(ltc_diffuse), transmittance_depth, transmittance_z, transmittance_boost, transmittance_color, color * isotropic_light_color * area);
2803 | #endif
2804 | 	}
2805 | #endif 
2806 | 
2807 | #if defined(LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED) || defined(LIGHT_RIM_USED) || defined(DIFFUSE_TOON) || defined(SPECULAR_TOON)
2808 | 	float solid_angle = quad_solid_angle(points);
2809 | #endif
2810 | 
2811 | #if defined(LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED)
2812 | 	vec3 cc_specular_tex_color = vec3(1.0);
2813 | 	float cc_specular_ltc = 0.0;
2814 | 	vec2 cc_fresnel;
2815 | 	ltc_evaluate_specular(vec3(vertex_normal), vec3(eye_vec), sqrt(mix(0.001, 0.1, float(clearcoat_roughness))), points, area_lights.data[idx].projector_rect, max_mipmap, area_light_atlas, SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP, ltc_lut1, ltc_lut2, cc_specular_ltc, cc_fresnel, cc_specular_tex_color);
2816 | 	half Fr = (half(0.04) * max(half(cc_fresnel.x), half(0.0)) + half(1.0 - 0.04) * max(half(cc_fresnel.y), half(0.0))) * clearcoat;
2817 | 	cc_attenuation = half(1.0) - Fr;
2818 | 	specular_light += half(cc_specular_ltc) * hvec3(cc_specular_tex_color) * Fr * color * light_attenuation_ltc * specular_amount;
2819 | #endif 
2820 | 
2821 | 	if (metallic < half(1.0)) {
2822 | #if defined(DIFFUSE_TOON)
2823 | 		float backface_ltc_diffuse = 0.0;
2824 | 		vec3 backface_ltc_tex_color_discard = vec3(1.0);
2825 | 		ltc_evaluate(vec3(-normal), vec3(eye_vec), mat3(1), points, vec4(0.0), max_mipmap, area_light_atlas, SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP, backface_ltc_diffuse, backface_ltc_tex_color_discard);
2826 | 		half NdotL = half((ltc_diffuse - backface_ltc_diffuse) / (max(solid_angle, 0.001) / M_PI));
2827 | 		half diffuse_brdf_NL = smoothstep(-roughness, max(roughness, half(0.01)), NdotL) * half(1.0 / M_PI);
2828 | 		diffuse_light += diffuse_brdf_NL * isotropic_light_color * color * area * light_attenuation * cc_attenuation;
2829 | #else

2831 | #endif 
2832 | 
2833 | #if defined(LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED)
2834 | 		
2835 | 		diffuse_light += color * max(half(solid_angle / M_PI) - half(ltc_diffuse), half(0.0)) * backlight * isotropic_light_color * light_attenuation_ltc;
2836 | #endif
2837 | 	}
2838 | 
2839 | #if defined(LIGHT_RIM_USED) 
2840 | 	half cNdotV = max(dot(normal, eye_vec), half(1e-4));
2841 | 	half rim_light = pow(max(half(1e-4), half(1.0) - cNdotV), max(half(0.0), (half(1.0) - roughness) * half(16.0)));
2842 | 	diffuse_light += rim_light * rim * mix(hvec3(1.0), albedo, rim_tint) * isotropic_light_color * color * half(solid_angle) * light_attenuation_ltc;
2843 | #endif
2844 | 
2845 | #if defined(SPECULAR_TOON)
2846 | 	
2847 | 	half mid = half(1.0) - roughness;
2848 | 	mid *= mid;

2850 | 	half RdotV = half(ltc_specular / (max(solid_angle, 0.001) / (M_PI)));
2851 | 	half intensity = smoothstep(mid - roughness * half(0.5), mid + roughness * half(0.5), RdotV) * mid; 
2852 | 	diffuse_light += intensity * hvec3(ltc_specular_tex_color) * color * area * light_attenuation * specular_amount; 
2853 | #elif defined(SPECULAR_DISABLED)
2854 | 	
2855 | #else
2856 | 	hvec3 spec = half(ltc_specular) * hvec3(ltc_specular_tex_color) * color * fresnel_color;
2857 | 	specular_light += spec * specular_amount * light_attenuation_ltc * cc_attenuation;
2858 | #endif 
2859 | 
2860 | #endif 
2861 | }

2863 | void reflection_process(uint ref_index, vec3 vertex, hvec3 ref_vec, hvec3 normal, half roughness, hvec3 ambient_light,

3006 | vec4 voxel_cone_trace(texture3D probe, vec3 cell_size, vec3 pos, vec3 direction, float tan_half_angle, float max_distance, float p_bias) {
3007 | 	float dist = p_bias;
3008 | 	vec4 color = vec4(0.0);
3009 | 
3010 | 	while (dist < max_distance && color.a < 0.95) {
3011 | 		float diameter = max(1.0, 2.0 * tan_half_angle * dist);
3012 | 		vec3 uvw_pos = (pos + dist * direction) * cell_size;
3013 | 		float half_diameter = diameter * 0.5;
3014 | 		
3015 | 		if (any(greaterThan(abs(uvw_pos - 0.5), vec3(0.5f + half_diameter * cell_size)))) {
3016 | 			break;
3017 | 		}
3018 | 		vec4 scolor = textureLod(sampler3D(probe, DEFAULT_SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP), uvw_pos, log2(diameter));
3019 | 		float a = (1.0 - color.a);
3020 | 		color += a * scolor;
3021 | 		dist += half_diameter;
3022 | 	}
3023 | 
3024 | 	return color;
3025 | }
3026 | 
3027 | vec4 voxel_cone_trace_45_degrees(texture3D probe, vec3 cell_size, vec3 pos, vec3 direction, float tan_half_angle, float max_distance, float p_bias) {
3028 | 	float dist = p_bias;
3029 | 	vec4 color = vec4(0.0);
3030 | 	float radius = max(0.5, tan_half_angle * dist);
3031 | 	float lod_level = log2(radius * 2.0);
3032 | 
3033 | 	while (dist < max_distance && color.a < 0.95) {
3034 | 		vec3 uvw_pos = (pos + dist * direction) * cell_size;
3035 | 
3036 | 		
3037 | 		if (any(greaterThan(abs(uvw_pos - 0.5), vec3(0.5f + radius * cell_size)))) {
3038 | 			break;
3039 | 		}
3040 | 		vec4 scolor = textureLod(sampler3D(probe, DEFAULT_SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP), uvw_pos, lod_level);
3041 | 		lod_level += 1.0;
3042 | 
3043 | 		float a = (1.0 - color.a);
3044 | 		scolor *= a;
3045 | 		color += scolor;
3046 | 		dist += radius;
3047 | 		radius = max(0.5, tan_half_angle * dist);
3048 | 	}
3049 | 
3050 | 	return color;
3051 | }

3053 | void voxel_gi_compute(uint index, vec3 position, vec3 normal, vec3 ref_vec, mat3 normal_xform, float roughness, vec3 ambient, vec3 environment, inout vec4 out_spec, inout vec4 out_diff) {
3054 | 	position = (voxel_gi_instances.data[index].xform * vec4(position, 1.0)).xyz;
3055 | 	ref_vec = normalize((voxel_gi_instances.data[index].xform * vec4(ref_vec, 0.0)).xyz);
3056 | 	normal = normalize((voxel_gi_instances.data[index].xform * vec4(normal, 0.0)).xyz);
3057 | 
3058 | 	position += normal * voxel_gi_instances.data[index].normal_bias;
3059 | 
3060 | 	
3061 | 	if (any(bvec2(any(lessThan(position, vec3(0.0))), any(greaterThan(position, voxel_gi_instances.data[index].bounds))))) {
3062 | 		return;
3063 | 	}
3064 | 
3065 | 	vec3 blendv = abs(position / voxel_gi_instances.data[index].bounds * 2.0 - 1.0);
3066 | 	float blend = clamp(1.0 - max(blendv.x, max(blendv.y, blendv.z)), 0.0, 1.0);
3067 | 	
3068 | 
3069 | 	float max_distance = length(voxel_gi_instances.data[index].bounds);
3070 | 	vec3 cell_size = 1.0 / voxel_gi_instances.data[index].bounds;
3071 | 
3072 | 	
3073 | 
3074 | #define MAX_CONE_DIRS 4
3075 | 
3076 | 	vec3 cone_dirs[MAX_CONE_DIRS] = vec3[](
3077 | 			vec3(0.707107, 0.0, 0.707107),
3078 | 			vec3(0.0, 0.707107, 0.707107),
3079 | 			vec3(-0.707107, 0.0, 0.707107),
3080 | 			vec3(0.0, -0.707107, 0.707107));
3081 | 
3082 | 	float cone_weights[MAX_CONE_DIRS] = float[](0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25);
3083 | 	float cone_angle_tan = 0.98269;
3084 | 
3085 | 	vec3 light = vec3(0.0);
3086 | 
3087 | 	for (int i = 0; i < MAX_CONE_DIRS; i++) {
3088 | 		vec3 dir = normalize((voxel_gi_instances.data[index].xform * vec4(normal_xform * cone_dirs[i], 0.0)).xyz);
3089 | 
3090 | 		vec4 cone_light = voxel_cone_trace_45_degrees(voxel_gi_textures[index], cell_size, position, dir, cone_angle_tan, max_distance, voxel_gi_instances.data[index].bias);
3091 | 
3092 | 		if (voxel_gi_instances.data[index].blend_ambient) {
3093 | 			cone_light.rgb = mix(ambient, cone_light.rgb, min(1.0, cone_light.a / 0.95));
3094 | 		}
3095 | 
3096 | 		light += cone_weights[i] * cone_light.rgb;
3097 | 	}
3098 | 
3099 | 	light *= voxel_gi_instances.data[index].dynamic_range * voxel_gi_instances.data[index].exposure_normalization;
3100 | 	out_diff += vec4(light * blend, blend);
3101 | 
3102 | 	
3103 | 	vec4 irr_light = voxel_cone_trace(voxel_gi_textures[index], cell_size, position, ref_vec, tan(roughness * 0.5 * M_PI * 0.99), max_distance, voxel_gi_instances.data[index].bias);
3104 | 	if (voxel_gi_instances.data[index].blend_ambient) {
3105 | 		irr_light.rgb = mix(environment, irr_light.rgb, min(1.0, irr_light.a / 0.95));
3106 | 	}
3107 | 	irr_light.rgb *= voxel_gi_instances.data[index].dynamic_range * voxel_gi_instances.data[index].exposure_normalization;
3108 | 	
3109 | 
3110 | 	out_spec += vec4(irr_light.rgb * blend, blend);
3111 | }

3113 | vec2 octahedron_wrap(vec2 v) {
3114 | 	vec2 signVal;
3115 | 	signVal.x = v.x >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : -1.0;
3116 | 	signVal.y = v.y >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : -1.0;
3117 | 	return (1.0 - abs(v.yx)) * signVal;
3118 | }
3119 | 
3120 | vec2 octahedron_encode(vec3 n) {
3121 | 	
3122 | 	n /= (abs(n.x) + abs(n.y) + abs(n.z));
3123 | 	n.xy = n.z >= 0.0 ? n.xy : octahedron_wrap(n.xy);
3124 | 	n.xy = n.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;
3125 | 	return n.xy;
3126 | }
