3128 | void sdfgi_process(uint cascade, vec3 cascade_pos, vec3 cam_pos, vec3 cam_normal, vec3 cam_specular_normal, bool use_specular, float roughness, out vec3 diffuse_light, out vec3 specular_light, out float blend) {
3129 | 	cascade_pos += cam_normal * sdfgi.normal_bias;
3130 | 
3131 | 	vec3 base_pos = floor(cascade_pos);
3132 | 	
3133 | 	ivec3 probe_base_pos = ivec3(base_pos);
3134 | 
3135 | 	vec4 diffuse_accum = vec4(0.0);
3136 | 	vec3 specular_accum;
3137 | 
3138 | 	ivec3 tex_pos = ivec3(probe_base_pos.xy, int(cascade));
3139 | 	tex_pos.x += probe_base_pos.z * sdfgi.probe_axis_size;
3140 | 	tex_pos.xy = tex_pos.xy * (SDFGI_OCT_SIZE + 2) + ivec2(1);
3141 | 
3142 | 	vec3 diffuse_posf = (vec3(tex_pos) + vec3(octahedron_encode(cam_normal) * float(SDFGI_OCT_SIZE), 0.0)) * sdfgi.lightprobe_tex_pixel_size;
3143 | 
3144 | 	vec3 specular_posf;
3145 | 
3146 | 	if (use_specular) {
3147 | 		specular_accum = vec3(0.0);
3148 | 		specular_posf = (vec3(tex_pos) + vec3(octahedron_encode(cam_specular_normal) * float(SDFGI_OCT_SIZE), 0.0)) * sdfgi.lightprobe_tex_pixel_size;
3149 | 	}
3150 | 
3151 | 	vec4 light_accum = vec4(0.0);
3152 | 	float weight_accum = 0.0;
3153 | 
3154 | 	for (uint j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
3155 | 		ivec3 offset = (ivec3(j) >> ivec3(0, 1, 2)) & ivec3(1, 1, 1);
3156 | 		ivec3 probe_posi = probe_base_pos;
3157 | 		probe_posi += offset;
3158 | 
3159 | 		
3160 | 
3161 | 		vec3 probe_pos = vec3(probe_posi);
3162 | 		vec3 probe_to_pos = cascade_pos - probe_pos;
3163 | 		vec3 probe_dir = normalize(-probe_to_pos);
3164 | 
3165 | 		vec3 trilinear = vec3(1.0) - abs(probe_to_pos);
3166 | 		float weight = trilinear.x * trilinear.y * trilinear.z * max(0.005, dot(cam_normal, probe_dir));
3167 | 
3168 | 		
3169 | 
3170 | 		if (sdfgi.use_occlusion) {
3171 | 			ivec3 occ_indexv = abs((sdfgi.cascades[cascade].probe_world_offset + probe_posi) & ivec3(1, 1, 1)) * ivec3(1, 2, 4);
3172 | 			vec4 occ_mask = mix(vec4(0.0), vec4(1.0), equal(ivec4(occ_indexv.x | occ_indexv.y), ivec4(0, 1, 2, 3)));
3173 | 
3174 | 			vec3 occ_pos = clamp(cascade_pos, probe_pos - sdfgi.occlusion_clamp, probe_pos + sdfgi.occlusion_clamp) * sdfgi.probe_to_uvw;
3175 | 			occ_pos.z += float(cascade);
3176 | 			if (occ_indexv.z != 0) { 
3177 | 				occ_pos.x += 1.0;
3178 | 			}
3179 | 
3180 | 			occ_pos *= sdfgi.occlusion_renormalize;
3181 | 			float occlusion = dot(textureLod(sampler3D(sdfgi_occlusion_cascades, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), occ_pos, 0.0), occ_mask);
3182 | 
3183 | 			weight *= max(occlusion, 0.01);
3184 | 		}
3185 | 
3186 | 		
3187 | 
3188 | 		vec3 diffuse;
3189 | 		vec3 pos_uvw = diffuse_posf;
3190 | 		pos_uvw.xy += vec2(offset.xy) * sdfgi.lightprobe_uv_offset.xy;
3191 | 		pos_uvw.x += float(offset.z) * sdfgi.lightprobe_uv_offset.z;
3192 | 		diffuse = textureLod(sampler2DArray(sdfgi_lightprobe_texture, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), pos_uvw, 0.0).rgb;
3193 | 
3194 | 		diffuse_accum += vec4(diffuse * weight * sdfgi.cascades[cascade].exposure_normalization, weight);
3195 | 
3196 | 		if (use_specular) {
3197 | 			vec3 specular = vec3(0.0);
3198 | 			vec3 pos_uvw = specular_posf;
3199 | 			pos_uvw.xy += vec2(offset.xy) * sdfgi.lightprobe_uv_offset.xy;
3200 | 			pos_uvw.x += float(offset.z) * sdfgi.lightprobe_uv_offset.z;
3201 | 			if (roughness < 0.99) {
3202 | 				specular = textureLod(sampler2DArray(sdfgi_lightprobe_texture, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), pos_uvw + vec3(0, 0, float(sdfgi.max_cascades)), 0.0).rgb;
3203 | 			}
3204 | 			if (roughness > 0.5) {
3205 | 				specular = mix(specular, textureLod(sampler2DArray(sdfgi_lightprobe_texture, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), pos_uvw, 0.0).rgb, (roughness - 0.5) * 2.0);
3206 | 			}
3207 | 
3208 | 			specular_accum += specular * weight * sdfgi.cascades[cascade].exposure_normalization;
3209 | 		}
3210 | 	}
3211 | 
3212 | 	if (diffuse_accum.a > 0.0) {
3213 | 		diffuse_accum.rgb /= diffuse_accum.a;
3214 | 	}
3215 | 
3216 | 	diffuse_light = diffuse_accum.rgb;
3217 | 
3218 | 	if (use_specular) {
3219 | 		if (diffuse_accum.a > 0.0) {
3220 | 			specular_accum /= diffuse_accum.a;
3221 | 		}
3222 | 
3223 | 		specular_light = specular_accum;
3224 | 	}
3225 | 
3226 | 	{
3227 | 		
3228 | 		float blend_from = (float(sdfgi.probe_axis_size - 1) / 2.0) - 2.5;
3229 | 		float blend_to = blend_from + 2.0;
3230 | 
3231 | 		vec3 inner_pos = cam_pos * sdfgi.cascades[cascade].to_probe;
3232 | 
3233 | 		float len = length(inner_pos);
3234 | 
3235 | 		inner_pos = abs(normalize(inner_pos));
3236 | 		len *= max(inner_pos.x, max(inner_pos.y, inner_pos.z));
3237 | 
3238 | 		if (len >= blend_from) {
3239 | 			blend = smoothstep(blend_from, blend_to, len);
3240 | 		} else {
3241 | 			blend = 0.0;
3242 | 		}
3243 | 	}
3244 | }
3245 | 
3246 | #endif 
3247 | 
3248 | #ifndef MODE_RENDER_DEPTH
3249 | 
3250 | vec4 volumetric_fog_process(vec2 screen_uv, float z) {
3251 | 	vec3 fog_pos = vec3(screen_uv, z * implementation_data.volumetric_fog_inv_length);
3252 | 	if (fog_pos.z < 0.0) {
3253 | 		return vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
3254 | 	} else if (fog_pos.z < 1.0) {
3255 | 		fog_pos.z = pow(fog_pos.z, implementation_data.volumetric_fog_detail_spread);
3256 | 	}
3257 | 
3258 | 	return texture(sampler3D(volumetric_fog_texture, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), fog_pos);
3259 | }
3260 | 
3261 | vec4 fog_process(vec3 vertex) {
3262 | 	vec3 fog_color = scene_data_block.data.fog_light_color;
3263 | 
3264 | 	if (scene_data_block.data.fog_aerial_perspective > 0.0) {
3265 | 		vec3 sky_fog_color = vec3(0.0);
3266 | 		vec3 cube_view = scene_data_block.data.radiance_inverse_xform * vertex;
3267 | 		
3268 | 		float mip_level = mix(1.0 / MAX_ROUGHNESS_LOD, 1.0, 1.0 - (abs(vertex.z) - scene_data_block.data.z_near) / (scene_data_block.data.z_far - scene_data_block.data.z_near));
3269 | #ifdef USE_RADIANCE_OCTMAP_ARRAY
3270 | 		float roughness_lod, blend;
3271 | 		blend = modf(mip_level * MAX_ROUGHNESS_LOD, roughness_lod);
3272 | 		float cube_lod = vec3_to_oct_lod(dFdx(cube_view), dFdy(cube_view), scene_data_block.data.radiance_pixel_size);
3273 | 		vec2 cube_uv = vec3_to_oct_with_border(cube_view, vec2(scene_data_block.data.radiance_border_size, 1.0 - scene_data_block.data.radiance_border_size * 2.0));
3274 | 		vec3 sky_sample_a = textureLod(sampler2DArray(radiance_octmap, DEFAULT_SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP), vec3(cube_uv, roughness_lod), cube_lod).rgb;
3275 | 		vec3 sky_sample_b = textureLod(sampler2DArray(radiance_octmap, DEFAULT_SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP), vec3(cube_uv, roughness_lod + 1), cube_lod).rgb;
3276 | 		sky_fog_color = mix(sky_sample_a, sky_sample_b, blend);
3277 | #else
3278 | 		float roughness_lod = mip_level * MAX_ROUGHNESS_LOD;
3279 | 		vec2 cube_uv = vec3_to_oct_with_border(cube_view, vec2(scene_data_block.data.radiance_border_size, 1.0 - scene_data_block.data.radiance_border_size * 2.0));
3280 | 		sky_fog_color = textureLod(sampler2D(radiance_octmap, DEFAULT_SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP), cube_uv, roughness_lod).rgb;
3281 | #endif 
3282 | 		fog_color = mix(fog_color, sky_fog_color, scene_data_block.data.fog_aerial_perspective);
3283 | 	}
3284 | 
3285 | 	if (scene_data_block.data.fog_sun_scatter > 0.001) {
3286 | 		vec4 sun_scatter = vec4(0.0);
3287 | 		float sun_total = 0.0;
3288 | 		vec3 view = normalize(vertex);
3289 | 
3290 | 		for (uint i = 0; i < scene_data_block.data.directional_light_count; i++) {
3291 | 			vec3 light_color = directional_lights.data[i].color * directional_lights.data[i].energy;
3292 | 			float light_amount = pow(max(dot(view, directional_lights.data[i].direction), 0.0), 8.0);
3293 | 			fog_color += light_color * light_amount * scene_data_block.data.fog_sun_scatter;
3294 | 		}
3295 | 	}
3296 | 
3297 | 	float fog_amount = 0.0;
3298 | 
3299 | 	if (sc_use_depth_fog()) {
3300 | 		float fog_z = smoothstep(scene_data_block.data.fog_depth_begin, scene_data_block.data.fog_depth_end, length(vertex));
3301 | 		float fog_quad_amount = pow(fog_z, scene_data_block.data.fog_depth_curve) * scene_data_block.data.fog_density;
3302 | 		fog_amount = fog_quad_amount;
3303 | 	} else {
3304 | 		fog_amount = 1 - exp(min(0.0, -length(vertex) * scene_data_block.data.fog_density));
3305 | 	}
3306 | 
3307 | 	if (abs(scene_data_block.data.fog_height_density) >= 0.0001) {
3308 | 		mat4 inv_view_matrix = transpose(mat4(scene_data_block.data.inv_view_matrix[0],
3309 | 				scene_data_block.data.inv_view_matrix[1],
3310 | 				scene_data_block.data.inv_view_matrix[2],
3311 | 				vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)));
3312 | 
3313 | 		float y = (inv_view_matrix * vec4(vertex, 1.0)).y;
3314 | 
3315 | 		float y_dist = y - scene_data_block.data.fog_height;
3316 | 
3317 | 		float vfog_amount = 1.0 - exp(min(0.0, y_dist * scene_data_block.data.fog_height_density));
3318 | 
3319 | 		fog_amount = max(vfog_amount, fog_amount);
3320 | 	}
3321 | 
3322 | 	return vec4(fog_color, fog_amount);
3323 | }

3325 | void cluster_get_item_range(uint p_offset, out uint item_min, out uint item_max, out uint item_from, out uint item_to) {
3326 | 	uint item_min_max = cluster_buffer.data[p_offset];
3327 | 	item_min = item_min_max & 0xFFFFu;
3328 | 	item_max = item_min_max >> 16;
3329 | 
3330 | 	item_from = item_min >> 5;
3331 | 	item_to = (item_max == 0) ? 0 : ((item_max - 1) >> 5) + 1; 
3332 | }
3333 | 
3334 | uint cluster_get_range_clip_mask(uint i, uint z_min, uint z_max) {
3335 | 	int local_min = clamp(int(z_min) - int(i) * 32, 0, 31);
3336 | 	int mask_width = min(int(z_max) - int(z_min), 32 - local_min);
3337 | 	return bitfieldInsert(uint(0), uint(0xFFFFFFFF), local_min, mask_width);
3338 | }

3340 | #endif

3342 | #if defined(MODE_RENDER_NORMAL_ROUGHNESS) || defined(MODE_RENDER_MATERIAL)
3343 | 
3344 | vec3 encode24(vec3 v) {
3345 | 	
3346 | 	vec3 vNormalUns = abs(v);
3347 | 	
3348 | 	float maxNAbs = max(vNormalUns.z, max(vNormalUns.x, vNormalUns.y));
3349 | 	
3350 | 	vec2 vTexCoord = vNormalUns.z < maxNAbs ? (vNormalUns.y < maxNAbs ? vNormalUns.yz : vNormalUns.xz) : vNormalUns.xy;
3351 | 	vTexCoord /= maxNAbs;
3352 | 	vTexCoord = vTexCoord.x < vTexCoord.y ? vTexCoord.yx : vTexCoord.xy;
3353 | 	
3354 | 	vTexCoord.y /= vTexCoord.x;
3355 | 	float fFittingScale = texture(sampler2D(best_fit_normal_texture, SAMPLER_NEAREST_CLAMP), vTexCoord).r;
3356 | 	
3357 | 	vec3 result = v / maxNAbs;
3358 | 	
3359 | 	result *= fFittingScale;
3360 | 	return result;
3361 | }

3624 | #if defined(NORMAL_MAP_USED)
3625 | 	normal_map.xy = normal_map.xy * 2.0 - 1.0;
3626 | 	normal_map.z = sqrt(max(0.0, 1.0 - dot(normal_map.xy, normal_map.xy))); 
3627 | 
3628 | 	
3629 | 	
3630 | 	normal = normalize(mix(normal, tangent * normal_map.x + binormal * normal_map.y + normal * normal_map.z, normal_map_depth));
3631 | #elif defined(NORMAL_USED)
3632 | 	normal = geo_normal;
3633 | #endif 
3634 | 
3635 | #ifdef BENT_NORMAL_MAP_USED
3636 | 	bent_normal_map.xy = bent_normal_map.xy * 2.0 - 1.0;
3637 | 	bent_normal_map.z = sqrt(max(0.0, 1.0 - dot(bent_normal_map.xy, bent_normal_map.xy)));
3638 | 
3639 | 	bent_normal_vector = normalize(tangent * bent_normal_map.x + binormal * bent_normal_map.y + normal * bent_normal_map.z);
3640 | #endif
3641 | 
3642 | #ifdef LIGHT_ANISOTROPY_USED
3643 | 
3644 | 	if (anisotropy > 0.01) {
3645 | 		mat3 rot = mat3(normalize(tangent), normalize(binormal), normal);
3646 | 		
3647 | 		tangent = normalize(rot * vec3(anisotropy_flow.x, anisotropy_flow.y, 0.0));
3648 | 		binormal = normalize(rot * vec3(-anisotropy_flow.y, anisotropy_flow.x, 0.0));
3649 | 	}
3650 | 
3651 | #endif
3652 | 
3653 | #ifdef ENABLE_CLIP_ALPHA
3654 | #ifdef MODE_RENDER_MATERIAL
3655 | 	if (albedo.a < 0.99) {
3656 | 		
3657 | 		albedo.a = 0.0;
3658 | 		alpha = 0.0;
3659 | 	} else {
3660 | 		albedo.a = 1.0;
3661 | 		alpha = 1.0;
3662 | 	}
3663 | #else
3664 | 	if (albedo.a < 0.99) {
3665 | 		
3666 | 		discard;
3667 | 	}
3668 | #endif 
3669 | #endif

3680 | 	if (bool(scene_data.flags & SCENE_DATA_FLAGS_USE_FOG)) {
3681 | 		fog = fog_process(vertex);
3682 | 		
3683 | 		fog.rgb *= fog.a;
3684 | 		fog.a = 1.0 - fog.a;
3685 | 	}
3686 | 
3687 | 	if (implementation_data.volumetric_fog_enabled) {
3688 | #ifdef USE_MULTIVIEW
3689 | 		vec4 volumetric_fog = volumetric_fog_process(combined_uv, -vertex.z);
3690 | #else
3691 | 		vec4 volumetric_fog = volumetric_fog_process(screen_uv, -vertex.z);
3692 | #endif

3695 | 			
3696 | 			if (sc_fog_use_legacy_blending()) {
3697 | 				res.a = mix(0.0, fog.a, volumetric_fog.a);
3698 | 				res.rgb = mix(volumetric_fog.rgb, fog.rgb, volumetric_fog.a);
3699 | 			} else {
3700 | 				res.a = fog.a * volumetric_fog.a;
3701 | 				res.rgb = fog.rgb * volumetric_fog.a + volumetric_fog.rgb;
3702 | 			}
3703 | 		} else {
3704 | 			res = volumetric_fog;
3705 | 			if (sc_fog_use_legacy_blending()) {
3706 | 				res.rgb *= 1.0 - res.a;
3707 | 			}
3708 | 		}
3709 | 		fog = res;
3710 | 	}
3711 | #else
3712 | 	
3713 | 	fog.rgb *= fog.a;
3714 | 	fog.a = 1.0 - fog.a;
3715 | #endif 
3716 |

3718 | 	uint fog_ba = packHalf2x16(fog.ba);
3719 | 
3720 | #endif 
3721 | #endif

3726 | 
3727 | #ifdef USE_MULTIVIEW
3728 | 	uvec2 cluster_pos = uvec2(combined_uv.xy / scene_data.screen_pixel_size) >> implementation_data.cluster_shift;
3729 | #else
3730 | 	uvec2 cluster_pos = uvec2(gl_FragCoord.xy) >> implementation_data.cluster_shift;
3731 | #endif
3732 | 	uint cluster_offset = (implementation_data.cluster_width * cluster_pos.y + cluster_pos.x) * (implementation_data.max_cluster_element_count_div_32 + 32);
3733 | 
3734 | 	uint cluster_z = uint(clamp((-vertex.z / scene_data.z_far) * 32.0, 0.0, 31.0));
3735 | 
3736 | 	
3737 | 	vec3 vertex_ddx = dFdx(vertex);
3738 | 	vec3 vertex_ddy = dFdy(vertex);

3741 | 
3742 | 		uint cluster_decal_offset = cluster_offset + implementation_data.cluster_type_size * 3;
3743 | 
3744 | 		uint item_min;
3745 | 		uint item_max;
3746 | 		uint item_from;
3747 | 		uint item_to;
3748 | 
3749 | 		cluster_get_item_range(cluster_decal_offset + implementation_data.max_cluster_element_count_div_32 + cluster_z, item_min, item_max, item_from, item_to);
3750 | 
3751 | 		item_from = subgroupBroadcastFirst(subgroupMin(item_from));
3752 | 		item_to = subgroupBroadcastFirst(subgroupMax(item_to));
3753 | 
3754 | 		for (uint i = item_from; i < item_to; i++) {
3755 | 			uint mask = cluster_buffer.data[cluster_decal_offset + i];
3756 | 			mask &= cluster_get_range_clip_mask(i, item_min, item_max);
3757 | 
3758 | 			uint merged_mask = subgroupBroadcastFirst(subgroupOr(mask));
3759 | 			while (merged_mask != 0) {
3760 | 				uint bit = findMSB(merged_mask);
3761 | 				merged_mask &= ~(1u << bit);
3762 | 
3763 | 				if (((1u << bit) & mask) == 0) { 
3764 | 					continue;
3765 | 				}
3766 | 
3767 | 				uint decal_index = 32 * i + bit;
3768 | 
3769 | 				if (!bool(decals.data[decal_index].mask & instances.data[instance_index].layer_mask)) {
3770 | 					continue; 
3771 | 				}

3774 | 				if (any(lessThan(uv_local, vec3(0.0, -1.0, 0.0))) || any(greaterThan(uv_local, vec3(1.0)))) {
3775 | 					continue; 
3776 | 				}
3777 | 
3778 | 				float fade = pow(1.0 - (uv_local.y > 0.0 ? uv_local.y : -uv_local.y), uv_local.y > 0.0 ? decals.data[decal_index].upper_fade : decals.data[decal_index].lower_fade);
3779 | 
3780 | 				if (decals.data[decal_index].normal_fade > 0.0) {
3781 | 					fade *= smoothstep(decals.data[decal_index].normal_fade, 1.0, dot(geo_normal, decals.data[decal_index].normal) * 0.5 + 0.5);
3782 | 				}
3783 | 
3784 | 				
3785 | 				vec2 ddx = (decals.data[decal_index].xform * vec4(vertex_ddx, 0.0)).xz;
3786 | 				vec2 ddy = (decals.data[decal_index].xform * vec4(vertex_ddy, 0.0)).xz;
3787 | 
3788 | 				if (decals.data[decal_index].albedo_rect != vec4(0.0)) {

3792 | 						decal_albedo = textureGrad(sampler2D(decal_atlas_srgb, decal_sampler), uv_local.xz * decals.data[decal_index].albedo_rect.zw + decals.data[decal_index].albedo_rect.xy, ddx * decals.data[decal_index].albedo_rect.zw, ddy * decals.data[decal_index].albedo_rect.zw);
3793 | 					} else {
3794 | 						decal_albedo = textureLod(sampler2D(decal_atlas_srgb, decal_sampler), uv_local.xz * decals.data[decal_index].albedo_rect.zw + decals.data[decal_index].albedo_rect.xy, 0.0);
3795 | 					}
3796 | 					decal_albedo *= decals.data[decal_index].modulate;
3797 | 					decal_albedo.a *= fade;

3799 | 
3800 | 					if (decals.data[decal_index].normal_rect != vec4(0.0)) {
3801 | 						vec3 decal_normal;
3802 | 						if (sc_decal_use_mipmaps()) {
3803 | 							decal_normal = textureGrad(sampler2D(decal_atlas, decal_sampler), uv_local.xz * decals.data[decal_index].normal_rect.zw + decals.data[decal_index].normal_rect.xy, ddx * decals.data[decal_index].normal_rect.zw, ddy * decals.data[decal_index].normal_rect.zw).xyz;
3804 | 						} else {
3805 | 							decal_normal = textureLod(sampler2D(decal_atlas, decal_sampler), uv_local.xz * decals.data[decal_index].normal_rect.zw + decals.data[decal_index].normal_rect.xy, 0.0).xyz;
3806 | 						}
3807 | 						decal_normal.xy = decal_normal.xy * vec2(2.0, -2.0) - vec2(1.0, -1.0); 
3808 | 						decal_normal.z = sqrt(max(0.0, 1.0 - dot(decal_normal.xy, decal_normal.xy)));
3809 | 						
3810 | 						decal_normal = (decals.data[decal_index].normal_xform * decal_normal.xzy).xyz;
3811 |

3813 | 					}

3819 | 						} else {

3821 | 						}

3823 | 						roughness = mix(roughness, decal_orm.g, decal_albedo.a);

3849 | 		vec3 dndu = dFdx(normal), dndv = dFdy(normal);

3993 | 		uint index = instances.data[instance_index].gi_offset;
3994 | 
3995 | 		
3996 | 		vec3 wnormal = mat3(inv_view_matrix) * indirect_normal;
3997 | 
3998 | 		
3999 | 		const float c[5] = float[](
4000 | 				0.886227, 
4001 | 				1.023327, 
4002 | 				0.858086, 
4003 | 				0.247708, 
4004 | 				0.429043 
4005 | 		);
4006 |

4008 | 								 c[1] * lightmap_captures.data[index].sh[1].rgb * wnormal.y +
4009 | 								 c[1] * lightmap_captures.data[index].sh[2].rgb * wnormal.z +
4010 | 								 c[1] * lightmap_captures.data[index].sh[3].rgb * wnormal.x +
4011 | 								 c[2] * lightmap_captures.data[index].sh[4].rgb * wnormal.x * wnormal.y +
4012 | 								 c[2] * lightmap_captures.data[index].sh[5].rgb * wnormal.y * wnormal.z +

4346 | 
4347 | 		if (d <= max(r1, r2) - min(r1, r2)) {
4348 |

4350 | 		} else if (d >= r1 + r2) {

4353 | 		} else {

4355 | 			float x = 1.0 - clamp((d - delta) / (r1 + r2 - delta), 0.0, 1.0);

4429 | 	
4430 | 	
4431 | 	f0 = mix(f0, f0_Clear_Coat_To_Surface(f0), clearcoat);
4432 | #endif

4439 | #else

4444 | 		energy_compensation = get_energy_compensation(f0, envBRDF.y);
4445 | 
4446 | 		
4447 | 		float f90 = clamp(50.0 * f0.g, metallic, 1.0);
4448 | 		indirect_specular_light *= energy_compensation * ((f90 - f0) * envBRDF.x + f0 * envBRDF.y);
4449 | 
4450 | #ifdef LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED

4452 |

4459 | 		indirect_specular_light *= cc_attenuation;

4469 | #endif

5099 | 
5100 | 		uint albedo16 = 0x1; 
5101 | 		albedo16 |= clamp(uint(albedo.r * 31.0), 0, 31) << 11;
5102 | 		albedo16 |= clamp(uint(albedo.g * 31.0), 0, 31) << 6;
5103 | 		albedo16 |= clamp(uint(albedo.b * 31.0), 0, 31) << 1;
5104 | 
5105 | 		imageStore(albedo_volume_grid, grid_pos, uvec4(albedo16));
5106 | 
5107 | 		uint facing_bits = 0;
5108 | 		const vec3 aniso_dir[6] = vec3[](
5109 | 				vec3(1, 0, 0),
5110 | 				vec3(0, 1, 0),
5111 | 				vec3(0, 0, 1),
5112 | 				vec3(-1, 0, 0),
5113 | 				vec3(0, -1, 0),
5114 | 				vec3(0, 0, -1));
5115 | 
5116 | 		vec3 cam_normal = mat3(inv_view_matrix) * normalize(normal_interp);
5117 | 
5118 | 		float closest_dist = -1e20;
5119 | 
5120 | 		for (uint i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
5121 | 			float d = dot(cam_normal, aniso_dir[i]);
5122 | 			if (d > closest_dist) {
5123 | 				closest_dist = d;
5124 | 				facing_bits = (1 << i);
5125 | 			}
5126 | 		}
5127 | 
5128 | #ifdef NO_IMAGE_ATOMICS
5129 | 		imageStore(geom_facing_grid, grid_pos, uvec4(imageLoad(geom_facing_grid, grid_pos).r | facing_bits)); 
5130 | #else
5131 | 		imageAtomicOr(geom_facing_grid, grid_pos, facing_bits); 
5132 | #endif
5133 | 
5134 | 		if (length(emission) > 0.001) {
5135 | 			float lumas[6];
5136 | 			vec3 light_total = vec3(0);
5137 | 
5138 | 			for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
5139 | 				float strength = max(0.0, dot(cam_normal, aniso_dir[i]));
5140 | 				vec3 light = emission * strength;
5141 | 				light_total += light;
5142 | 				lumas[i] = max(light.r, max(light.g, light.b));
5143 | 			}
5144 | 
5145 | 			float luma_total = max(light_total.r, max(light_total.g, light_total.b));
5146 | 
5147 | 			uint light_aniso = 0;
5148 | 
5149 | 			for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
5150 | 				light_aniso |= min(31, uint((lumas[i] / luma_total) * 31.0)) << (i * 5);
5151 | 			}
5152 | 
5153 | 			
5154 | 
5155 | 			const float pow2to9 = 512.0f;
5156 | 			const float B = 15.0f;
5157 | 			const float N = 9.0f;
5158 | 			const float LN2 = 0.6931471805599453094172321215;
5159 | 
5160 | 			float cRed = clamp(light_total.r, 0.0, 65408.0);
5161 | 			float cGreen = clamp(light_total.g, 0.0, 65408.0);
5162 | 			float cBlue = clamp(light_total.b, 0.0, 65408.0);
5163 | 
5164 | 			float cMax = max(cRed, max(cGreen, cBlue));
5165 | 
5166 | 			float expp = max(-B - 1.0f, floor(log(cMax) / LN2)) + 1.0f + B;
5167 | 
5168 | 			float sMax = floor((cMax / pow(2.0f, expp - B - N)) + 0.5f);
5169 | 
5170 | 			float exps = expp + 1.0f;
5171 | 
5172 | 			if (0.0 <= sMax && sMax < pow2to9) {
5173 | 				exps = expp;
5174 | 			}
5175 | 
5176 | 			float sRed = floor((cRed / pow(2.0f, exps - B - N)) + 0.5f);
5177 | 			float sGreen = floor((cGreen / pow(2.0f, exps - B - N)) + 0.5f);
5178 | 			float sBlue = floor((cBlue / pow(2.0f, exps - B - N)) + 0.5f);
5179 | 			
5180 | 			uint light_rgbe = ((uint(sRed) & 0x1FFu) >> 1) | ((uint(sGreen) & 0x1FFu) << 8) | (((uint(sBlue) & 0x1FFu) >> 1) << 17) | ((uint(exps) & 0x1Fu) << 25);
5181 | 
5182 | 			imageStore(emission_grid, grid_pos, uvec4(light_rgbe));
5183 | 			imageStore(emission_aniso_grid, grid_pos, uvec4(light_aniso));
5184 | 		}
5185 | 	}

  68 | #define hmat4 f16mat4
  69 | #define saturateHalf(x) min(float16_t(x), HALF_FLT_MAX)
  70 | 
  71 | #else
  72 | 
  73 | #define HALF_FLT_MIN float(1.175494351e-38F)
  74 | #define HALF_FLT_MAX float(3.402823466e+38F)
  75 |

  79 | #define hvec4 vec4
  80 | #define hmat2 mat2
  81 | #define hmat3 mat3
  82 | #define hmat4 mat4
  83 | #define saturateHalf(x) (x)
  84 | 
  85 | #endif

  87 | #endif

 120 | vec3 oct_to_vec3(vec2 e) {
 121 | 	vec3 v = vec3(e.xy, 1.0 - abs(e.x) - abs(e.y));
 122 | 	float t = max(-v.z, 0.0);
 123 | 	v.xy += t * -sign(v.xy);
 124 | 	return normalize(v);
 125 | }
 126 | 
 127 | 
 128 | vec3 oct_to_vec3_with_border(vec2 uv, float border_size) {
 129 | 	
 130 | 	uv = (uv - 0.5) * (2.0 / border_size);
 131 | 	
 132 | 	
 133 | 	vec2 mask = step(vec2(1.0), abs(uv));
 134 | 	uv = 2.0 * clamp(uv, -1.0, 1.0) - uv;
 135 | 	uv = mix(uv, -uv, mask.yx);
 136 | 	return oct_to_vec3(uv);
 137 | }
 138 | 
 139 | vec2 oct_wrap(vec2 v) {
 140 | 	vec2 signVal;
 141 | 	signVal.x = v.x >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : -1.0;
 142 | 	signVal.y = v.y >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : -1.0;
 143 | 	return (1.0 - abs(v.yx)) * signVal;
 144 | }
 145 | 
 146 | vec2 vec3_to_oct(vec3 n) {
 147 | 	
 148 | 	n /= (abs(n.x) + abs(n.y) + abs(n.z));
 149 | 	n.xy = (n.z >= 0.0) ? n.xy : oct_wrap(n.xy);
 150 | 	n.xy = n.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;
 151 | 	return n.xy;
 152 | }
 153 | 
 154 | 
 155 | 
 156 | vec2 vec3_to_oct_with_border(vec3 n, vec2 border_size) {
 157 | 	vec2 uv = vec3_to_oct(n);
 158 | 	return uv * border_size.y + border_size.x;
 159 | }
 160 | 
 161 | float vec3_to_oct_lod(vec3 n_ddx, vec3 n_ddy, float pixel_size) {
 162 | 	
 163 | 	
 164 | 	float pixel_size_sqr = 4.0 * pixel_size * pixel_size;
 165 | 	float ddx = dot(n_ddx, n_ddx) / pixel_size_sqr;
 166 | 	float ddy = dot(n_ddy, n_ddy) / pixel_size_sqr;
 167 | 	float dd_sqr = max(ddx, ddy);
 168 | 	return 0.25 * log2(dd_sqr + 1e-6f);
 169 | }

 815 | vec4 normal_roughness_compatibility(vec4 p_normal_roughness) {
 816 | 	float roughness = p_normal_roughness.w;
 817 | 	if (roughness > 0.5) {
 818 | 		roughness = 1.0 - roughness;
 819 | 	}
 820 | 	roughness /= (127.0 / 255.0);
 821 | 	return vec4(normalize(p_normal_roughness.xyz * 2.0 - 1.0) * 0.5 + 0.5, roughness);
 822 | }

 883 | #ifdef USE_LIGHTMAP
 884 | 
 885 | float w0(float a) {
 886 | 	return (1.0 / 6.0) * (a * (a * (-a + 3.0) - 3.0) + 1.0);
 887 | }
 888 | 
 889 | float w1(float a) {
 890 | 	return (1.0 / 6.0) * (a * a * (3.0 * a - 6.0) + 4.0);
 891 | }
 892 | 
 893 | float w2(float a) {
 894 | 	return (1.0 / 6.0) * (a * (a * (-3.0 * a + 3.0) + 3.0) + 1.0);
 895 | }
 896 | 
 897 | float w3(float a) {
 898 | 	return (1.0 / 6.0) * (a * a * a);
 899 | }
 900 | 
 901 | 
 902 | float g0(float a) {
 903 | 	return w0(a) + w1(a);
 904 | }
 905 | 
 906 | float g1(float a) {
 907 | 	return w2(a) + w3(a);
 908 | }
 909 | 
 910 | 
 911 | float h0(float a) {
 912 | 	return -1.0 + w1(a) / (w0(a) + w1(a));
 913 | }
 914 | 
 915 | float h1(float a) {
 916 | 	return 1.0 + w3(a) / (w2(a) + w3(a));
 917 | }
 918 | 
 919 | vec4 textureArray_bicubic(texture2DArray tex, vec3 uv, vec2 texture_size) {
 920 | 	vec2 texel_size = vec2(1.0) / texture_size;
 921 | 
 922 | 	uv.xy = uv.xy * texture_size + vec2(0.5);
 923 | 
 924 | 	vec2 iuv = floor(uv.xy);
 925 | 	vec2 fuv = fract(uv.xy);
 926 | 
 927 | 	float g0x = g0(fuv.x);
 928 | 	float g1x = g1(fuv.x);
 929 | 	float h0x = h0(fuv.x);
 930 | 	float h1x = h1(fuv.x);
 931 | 	float h0y = h0(fuv.y);
 932 | 	float h1y = h1(fuv.y);
 933 | 
 934 | 	vec2 p0 = (vec2(iuv.x + h0x, iuv.y + h0y) - vec2(0.5)) * texel_size;
 935 | 	vec2 p1 = (vec2(iuv.x + h1x, iuv.y + h0y) - vec2(0.5)) * texel_size;
 936 | 	vec2 p2 = (vec2(iuv.x + h0x, iuv.y + h1y) - vec2(0.5)) * texel_size;
 937 | 	vec2 p3 = (vec2(iuv.x + h1x, iuv.y + h1y) - vec2(0.5)) * texel_size;
 938 | 
 939 | 	return (g0(fuv.y) * (g0x * texture(sampler2DArray(tex, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), vec3(p0, uv.z)) + g1x * texture(sampler2DArray(tex, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), vec3(p1, uv.z)))) +
 940 | 			(g1(fuv.y) * (g0x * texture(sampler2DArray(tex, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), vec3(p2, uv.z)) + g1x * texture(sampler2DArray(tex, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), vec3(p3, uv.z))));
 941 | }
 942 | #endif

 989 | #ifdef MATERIAL_UNIFORMS_USED
 990 | /* clang-format off */
 991 | layout(set = MATERIAL_UNIFORM_SET, binding = 0, std140) uniform MaterialUniforms {
 992 | highp float m_surface_detail_strength;
 993 | highp float m_surface_detail_fade_m;
 994 | highp vec4 m_grass_tint;
 995 | highp float m_grass_scale;
 996 | highp float m_turf_patch_strength;
 997 | highp float m_turf_patch_freq;
 998 | highp vec4 m_turf_dry_tint;
 999 | highp vec4 m_turf_lush_tint;
1000 | highp vec2 m_turf_dry_band;
1001 | highp vec2 m_turf_lush_band;
1002 | highp vec4 m_tint;
1003 | } material;

1007 | layout(set = 3, binding = 1) uniform highp texture2D m_dirt_texture;
1008 | layout(set = 3, binding = 2) uniform highp texture2D m_grass_texture;
1009 | layout(location=15) in highp vec3 m_world_position;
1010 | 
1011 | vec3 m_cloudreach_detail_normal(vec3 m_at, vec3 m_normal_world, float m_height_value)
1012 | 	{
1013 | 		vec3 m_n=normalize(m_normal_world);
1014 | 		vec3 m_dx=dFdx(m_at);
1015 | 		vec3 m_dy=dFdy(m_at);
1016 | 		vec3 m_rx=cross(m_dy, m_n);
1017 | 		vec3 m_ry=cross(m_n, m_dx);
1018 | 		float m_determinant=dot(m_dx, m_rx);
1019 | 		vec3 m_gradient=((sign(m_determinant) * ((dFdx(m_height_value) * m_rx) + (dFdy(m_height_value) * m_ry))) / max(abs(m_determinant), 0.000001));
1020 | 		float m_fade=(1.0 - smoothstep((material.m_surface_detail_fade_m * 0.25), material.m_surface_detail_fade_m, max(length(m_dx), length(m_dy))));
1021 | return normalize((m_n - ((m_gradient * material.m_surface_detail_strength) * m_fade)));	}
1022 | 
1023 | float m_cloudreach_turf_patch(vec2 m_xz)
1024 | 	{
1025 | 		vec2 m_p=(m_xz * material.m_turf_patch_freq);
1026 | 		float m_a=(sin((m_p.x + (sin((m_p.y * 1.37000000476837)) * 1.70000004768372))) * sin(((m_p.y * 0.91000002622604) + (sin((m_p.x * 1.12999999523163)) * 1.29999995231628))));
1027 | 		float m_b=(sin((((m_p.x * 2.30999994277954) + 1.89999997615814) + (sin((m_p.y * 2.70000004768372)) * 1.10000002384186))) * sin((((m_p.y * 2.0699999332428) + 0.69999998807907) + (sin((m_p.x * 1.89999997615814)) * 1.20000004768372))));
1028 | return ((m_a * 0.72000002861023) + (m_b * 0.28000000119209));	}
1029 | 
1030 | vec3 m_cloudreach_turf(vec3 m_at)
1031 | 	{
1032 | 		vec3 m_fine=texture(sampler2D(m_grass_texture, SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_ANISOTROPIC_REPEAT), (m_at.xz * material.m_grass_scale)).rgb;
1033 | 		vec3 m_turned=texture(sampler2D(m_grass_texture, SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_ANISOTROPIC_REPEAT), (((mat2(0.80000001192093,0.60000002384186,-0.60000002384186,0.80000001192093) * m_at.xz) * material.m_grass_scale) * 0.70999997854233)).rgb;
1034 | 		vec3 m_texture_value=mix(m_fine, m_turned, 0.34999999403954);
1035 | 		m_texture_value=mix(vec3(0.50999999046326,0.50999999046326,0.50999999046326), m_texture_value, 0.51999998092651);
1036 | 		float m_broad=(sin(((m_at.x * 0.02899999916553) + sin((m_at.z * 0.04100000113249)))) * 0.04500000178814);
1037 | 		vec3 m_tint=material.m_grass_tint.rgb;
1038 | 		if ((material.m_turf_patch_strength > 0.0))
1039 | 		{
1040 | 					{
1041 | 			float m_field=m_cloudreach_turf_patch(m_at.xz);
1042 | 			float m_dry=smoothstep(material.m_turf_dry_band.x, material.m_turf_dry_band.y, m_field);
1043 | 			float m_lush=smoothstep(material.m_turf_lush_band.x, material.m_turf_lush_band.y, -m_field);
1044 | 			material.m_tint=mix(material.m_tint, material.m_turf_dry_tint.rgb, (m_dry * material.m_turf_patch_strength));
1045 | 			material.m_tint=mix(material.m_tint, material.m_turf_lush_tint.rgb, (m_lush * material.m_turf_patch_strength));
1046 | 		}
1047 | ;
1048 | 		}
1049 | return ((m_texture_value * material.m_tint) * (1.0 + m_broad));	}

1088 | #ifdef ALPHA_HASH_USED
1089 | 
1090 | float hash_2d(vec2 p) {
1091 | 	return fract(1.0e4 * sin(17.0 * p.x + 0.1 * p.y) *
1092 | 			(0.1 + abs(sin(13.0 * p.y + p.x))));
1093 | }
1094 | 
1095 | float hash_3d(vec3 p) {
1096 | 	return hash_2d(vec2(hash_2d(p.xy), p.z));
1097 | }
1098 | 
1099 | half compute_alpha_hash_threshold(vec3 pos, float hash_scale) {
1100 | 	vec3 dx = dFdx(pos);
1101 | 	vec3 dy = dFdy(pos);
1102 | 
1103 | 	float delta_max_sqr = max(length(dx), length(dy));
1104 | 	float pix_scale = 1.0 / (hash_scale * delta_max_sqr);
1105 | 
1106 | 	vec2 pix_scales =
1107 | 			vec2(exp2(floor(log2(pix_scale))), exp2(ceil(log2(pix_scale))));
1108 | 
1109 | 	vec2 a_thresh = vec2(hash_3d(floor(pix_scales.x * pos.xyz)),
1110 | 			hash_3d(floor(pix_scales.y * pos.xyz)));
1111 | 
1112 | 	float lerp_factor = fract(log2(pix_scale));
1113 | 
1114 | 	float a_interp = (1.0 - lerp_factor) * a_thresh.x + lerp_factor * a_thresh.y;
1115 | 
1116 | 	float min_lerp = min(lerp_factor, 1.0 - lerp_factor);
1117 | 
1118 | 	vec3 cases = vec3(a_interp * a_interp / (2.0 * min_lerp * (1.0 - min_lerp)),
1119 | 			(a_interp - 0.5 * min_lerp) / (1.0 - min_lerp),
1120 | 			1.0 - ((1.0 - a_interp) * (1.0 - a_interp) / (2.0 * min_lerp * (1.0 - min_lerp))));
1121 | 
1122 | 	float alpha_hash_threshold =
1123 | 			(a_interp < (1.0 - min_lerp)) ? ((a_interp < min_lerp) ? cases.x : cases.y) : cases.z;
1124 | 
1125 | 	return half(clamp(alpha_hash_threshold, 0.00001, 1.0));
1126 | }
1127 | 
1128 | #endif 
1129 | 
1130 | #ifdef ALPHA_ANTIALIASING_EDGE_USED
1131 | 
1132 | half calc_mip_level(vec2 texture_coord) {
1133 | 	vec2 dx = dFdx(texture_coord);
1134 | 	vec2 dy = dFdy(texture_coord);
1135 | 	float delta_max_sqr = max(dot(dx, dx), dot(dy, dy));
1136 | 	return max(half(0.0), half(0.5) * half(log2(delta_max_sqr)));
1137 | }
1138 | 
1139 | half compute_alpha_antialiasing_edge(half input_alpha, vec2 texture_coord, half alpha_edge) {
1140 | 	input_alpha *= half(1.0) + calc_mip_level(texture_coord) * half(0.25);
1141 | 	input_alpha = (input_alpha - alpha_edge) / max(fwidth(input_alpha), half(0.0001)) + half(0.5);
1142 | 	return clamp(input_alpha, half(0.0), half(1.0));
1143 | }
1144 | 
1145 | #endif

1159 | 
1160 | #define M_PI 3.14159265359
1161 | #define M_TAU 6.28318530718
1162 | 
1163 | float acos_approx(float p_x) {
1164 | 	float x = abs(p_x);
1165 | 	float res = -0.156583f * x + (M_PI / 2.0);
1166 | 	res *= sqrt(1.0f - x);
1167 | 	return (p_x >= 0) ? res : M_PI - res;
1168 | }
1169 | 
1170 | vec3 fetch_ltc_lod(vec2 uv, vec4 texture_rect, float lod, float max_mipmap, texture2D area_light_atlas, sampler texture_sampler) {
1171 | 	float low = min(max(floor(lod), 0.0), max_mipmap - 1.0);
1172 | 	float high = min(max(floor(lod + 1.0), 1.0), max_mipmap);
1173 | 	vec2 sample_pos = texture_rect.xy + clamp(uv, 0.0, 1.0) * texture_rect.zw; 
1174 | 	vec4 sample_col_low = textureLod(sampler2D(area_light_atlas, texture_sampler), sample_pos, low);
1175 | 	vec4 sample_col_high = textureLod(sampler2D(area_light_atlas, texture_sampler), sample_pos, high);
1176 | 
1177 | 	float blend = high - clamp(lod, high - 1.0, high);
1178 | 	vec4 sample_col = mix(sample_col_high, sample_col_low, blend);
1179 | 	return sample_col.rgb * sample_col.a; 
1180 | }
1181 | 
1182 | vec3 integrate_edge_hill(vec3 p0, vec3 p1) {
1183 | 	
1184 | 	
1185 | 	float cosTheta = dot(p0, p1);
1186 | 
1187 | 	float x = cosTheta;
1188 | 	float y = abs(x);
1189 | 	float a = 5.42031 + (3.12829 + 0.0902326 * y) * y;
1190 | 	float b = 3.45068 + (4.18814 + y) * y;
1191 | 	float theta_sintheta = a / b;
1192 | 
1193 | 	if (x < 0.0) {
1194 | 		theta_sintheta = M_PI * inversesqrt(1.0 - x * x) - theta_sintheta; 
1195 | 	}
1196 | 	return theta_sintheta * cross(p0, p1);
1197 | }
1198 | 
1199 | float integrate_edge(vec3 p_proj0, vec3 p_proj1, vec3 p0, vec3 p1) {
1200 | 	float epsilon = 0.00001;
1201 | 	bool opposite_sides = dot(p_proj0, p_proj1) < -1.0 + epsilon;
1202 | 	if (opposite_sides) {
1203 | 		
1204 | 		vec3 half_point_t = p0 + normalize(p1 - p0) * dot(p0, normalize(p0 - p1));
1205 | 		vec3 half_point = normalize(half_point_t);
1206 | 
1207 | 		
1208 | 		float theta_sintheta = 0.0;
1209 | 		{
1210 | 			float cosTheta = dot(p_proj0, half_point);
1211 | 
1212 | 			float x = cosTheta;
1213 | 			float y = abs(x);
1214 | 			float a = 5.42031 + (3.12829 + 0.0902326 * y) * y;
1215 | 			float b = 3.45068 + (4.18814 + y) * y;
1216 | 			theta_sintheta = a / b;
1217 | 			if (x < 0.0) {
1218 | 				theta_sintheta = M_PI * inversesqrt(1.0 - x * x) - theta_sintheta; 
1219 | 			}
1220 | 		}
1221 | 		float aa = (theta_sintheta * cross(p_proj0, half_point)).y;
1222 | 		{
1223 | 			float cosTheta = dot(half_point, p_proj1);
1224 | 
1225 | 			float x = cosTheta;
1226 | 			float y = abs(x);
1227 | 			float a = 5.42031 + (3.12829 + 0.0902326 * y) * y;
1228 | 			float b = 3.45068 + (4.18814 + y) * y;
1229 | 			theta_sintheta = a / b;
1230 | 			if (x < 0.0) {
1231 | 				theta_sintheta = M_PI * inversesqrt(1.0 - x * x) - theta_sintheta; 
1232 | 			}
1233 | 		}
1234 | 		float bb = (theta_sintheta * cross(half_point, p_proj1)).y;
1235 | 		return aa + bb;
1236 | 	} else {
1237 | 		
1238 | 		float cosTheta = dot(p_proj0, p_proj1);
1239 | 
1240 | 		float x = cosTheta;
1241 | 		float y = abs(x);
1242 | 		float a = 5.42031 + (3.12829 + 0.0902326 * y) * y;
1243 | 		float b = 3.45068 + (4.18814 + y) * y;
1244 | 		float theta_sintheta = a / b;
1245 | 
1246 | 		if (x < 0.0) {
1247 | 			theta_sintheta = M_PI * inversesqrt(1.0 - x * x) - theta_sintheta; 
1248 | 		}
1249 | 		return (theta_sintheta * cross(p_proj0, p_proj1)).y;
1250 | 	}
1251 | }
1252 | 
1253 | vec3 fetch_ltc_filtered_texture_with_form_factor(vec4 texture_rect, vec3 L[4], float max_mipmap, texture2D area_light_atlas, sampler texture_sampler) {
1254 | 	vec3 L0 = normalize(L[0]);
1255 | 	vec3 L1 = normalize(L[1]);
1256 | 	vec3 L2 = normalize(L[2]);
1257 | 	vec3 L3 = normalize(L[3]);
1258 | 
1259 | 	vec3 F = vec3(0.0); 
1260 | 	F += integrate_edge_hill(L0, L1);
1261 | 	F += integrate_edge_hill(L1, L2);
1262 | 	F += integrate_edge_hill(L2, L3);
1263 | 	F += integrate_edge_hill(L3, L0);
1264 | 
1265 | 	vec2 uv;
1266 | 	float lod = 0.0;
1267 | 
1268 | 	if (dot(F, F) < 1e-16) {
1269 | 		uv = vec2(0.5);
1270 | 		lod = max_mipmap;
1271 | 	} else {
1272 | 		vec3 lx = L[1] - L[0];
1273 | 		vec3 ly = L[3] - L[0];
1274 | 		vec3 ln = cross(lx, ly);
1275 | 
1276 | 		float dist_x_area = dot(L[0], ln);
1277 | 		float d = dist_x_area / dot(F, ln);
1278 | 		vec3 isec = d * F;
1279 | 		vec3 li = isec - L[0]; 
1280 | 
1281 | 		float dot_lxy = dot(lx, ly);
1282 | 		float inv_dot_lxlx = 1.0 / dot(lx, lx);
1283 | 		vec3 ly_ = vec3(ly - lx * dot_lxy * inv_dot_lxlx); 
1284 | 		uv.y = dot(vec3(li), ly_) / dot(ly_, ly_);
1285 | 		uv.x = dot(vec3(li), lx) * inv_dot_lxlx - dot_lxy * inv_dot_lxlx * uv.y;
1286 | 
1287 | 		lod = abs(dist_x_area) / pow(dot(ln, ln), 0.75);
1288 | 		lod = log(2048.0 * lod) / log(3.0);
1289 | 	}
1290 | 	return fetch_ltc_lod(vec2(1.0) - uv, texture_rect, lod, max_mipmap, area_light_atlas, texture_sampler);
1291 | }
1292 | 
1293 | 
1294 | float quad_solid_angle(vec3 L[4]) {
1295 | 	
1296 | 	
1297 | 	vec3 c1 = cross(L[0], L[1]);
1298 | 	vec3 c2 = cross(L[1], L[2]);
1299 | 	vec3 c3 = cross(L[2], L[3]);
1300 | 	vec3 c4 = cross(L[3], L[0]);
1301 | 	vec3 n0 = normalize(c1);
1302 | 	vec3 n1 = normalize(c2);
1303 | 	vec3 n2 = normalize(c3);
1304 | 	vec3 n3 = normalize(c4);
1305 | 	float g0 = acos(clamp(dot(-n0, n1), -1.0, 1.0));
1306 | 	float g1 = acos(clamp(dot(-n1, n2), -1.0, 1.0));
1307 | 	float g2 = acos(clamp(dot(-n2, n3), -1.0, 1.0));
1308 | 	float g3 = acos(clamp(dot(-n3, n0), -1.0, 1.0));
1309 | 
1310 | 	float angle_sum = g0 + g1 + g2 + g3;
1311 | 
1312 | 	return clamp(angle_sum - M_TAU, 0.0, M_TAU);
1313 | }
1314 | 
1315 | void clip_quad_to_horizon(inout vec3 L[5], out int vertex_count) {
1316 | 	
1317 | 	int config = 0;
1318 | 	if (L[0].y > 0.0) {
1319 | 		config += 1;
1320 | 	}
1321 | 	if (L[1].y > 0.0) {
1322 | 		config += 2;
1323 | 	}
1324 | 	if (L[2].y > 0.0) {
1325 | 		config += 4;
1326 | 	}
1327 | 	if (L[3].y > 0.0) {
1328 | 		config += 8;
1329 | 	}
1330 | 
1331 | 	
1332 | 	vertex_count = 0;
1333 | 
1334 | 	if (config == 0) {
1335 | 		
1336 | 	} else if (config == 1) { 
1337 | 		vertex_count = 3;
1338 | 		L[1] = -L[1].y * L[0] + L[0].y * L[1];
1339 | 		L[2] = -L[3].y * L[0] + L[0].y * L[3];
1340 | 	} else if (config == 2) { 
1341 | 		vertex_count = 3;
1342 | 		L[0] = -L[0].y * L[1] + L[1].y * L[0];
1343 | 		L[2] = -L[2].y * L[1] + L[1].y * L[2];
1344 | 	} else if (config == 3) { 
1345 | 		vertex_count = 4;
1346 | 		L[2] = -L[2].y * L[1] + L[1].y * L[2];
1347 | 		L[3] = -L[3].y * L[0] + L[0].y * L[3];
1348 | 	} else if (config == 4) { 
1349 | 		vertex_count = 3;
1350 | 		L[0] = -L[3].y * L[2] + L[2].y * L[3];
1351 | 		L[1] = -L[1].y * L[2] + L[2].y * L[1];
1352 | 	} else if (config == 5) { 
1353 | 		vertex_count = 0;
1354 | 	} else if (config == 6) { 
1355 | 		vertex_count = 4;
1356 | 		L[0] = -L[0].y * L[1] + L[1].y * L[0];
1357 | 		L[3] = -L[3].y * L[2] + L[2].y * L[3];
1358 | 	} else if (config == 7) { 
1359 | 		vertex_count = 5;
1360 | 		L[4] = -L[3].y * L[0] + L[0].y * L[3];
1361 | 		L[3] = -L[3].y * L[2] + L[2].y * L[3];
1362 | 	} else if (config == 8) { 
1363 | 		vertex_count = 3;
1364 | 		L[0] = -L[0].y * L[3] + L[3].y * L[0];
1365 | 		L[1] = -L[2].y * L[3] + L[3].y * L[2];
1366 | 		L[2] = L[3];
1367 | 	} else if (config == 9) { 
1368 | 		vertex_count = 4;
1369 | 		L[1] = -L[1].y * L[0] + L[0].y * L[1];
1370 | 		L[2] = -L[2].y * L[3] + L[3].y * L[2];
1371 | 	} else if (config == 10) { 
1372 | 		vertex_count = 0;
1373 | 	} else if (config == 11) { 
1374 | 		vertex_count = 5;
1375 | 		L[4] = L[3];
1376 | 		L[3] = -L[2].y * L[3] + L[3].y * L[2];
1377 | 		L[2] = -L[2].y * L[1] + L[1].y * L[2];
1378 | 	} else if (config == 12) { 
1379 | 		vertex_count = 4;
1380 | 		L[1] = -L[1].y * L[2] + L[2].y * L[1];
1381 | 		L[0] = -L[0].y * L[3] + L[3].y * L[0];
1382 | 	} else if (config == 13) { 
1383 | 		vertex_count = 5;
1384 | 		L[4] = L[3];
1385 | 		L[3] = L[2];
1386 | 		L[2] = -L[1].y * L[2] + L[2].y * L[1];
1387 | 		L[1] = -L[1].y * L[0] + L[0].y * L[1];
1388 | 	} else if (config == 14) { 
1389 | 		vertex_count = 5;
1390 | 		L[4] = -L[0].y * L[3] + L[3].y * L[0];
1391 | 		L[0] = -L[0].y * L[1] + L[1].y * L[0];
1392 | 	} else if (config == 15) { 
1393 | 		vertex_count = 4;
1394 | 	}
1395 | 
1396 | 	if (vertex_count == 3) {
1397 | 		L[3] = L[0];
1398 | 	}
1399 | 	if (vertex_count == 4) {
1400 | 		L[4] = L[0];
1401 | 	}
1402 | }
1403 | 
1404 | float ltc_integrate_clipped_quad(vec3 L[5], vec3 L_proj[5], int vertices_above_horizon) {
1405 | 	float I;
1406 | 	I = integrate_edge(L_proj[0], L_proj[1], L[0], L[1]);
1407 | 	I += integrate_edge(L_proj[1], L_proj[2], L[1], L[2]);
1408 | 	I += integrate_edge(L_proj[2], L_proj[3], L[2], L[3]);
1409 | 	if (vertices_above_horizon >= 4) {
1410 | 		I += integrate_edge(L_proj[3], L_proj[4], L[3], L[4]);
1411 | 	}
1412 | 	if (vertices_above_horizon == 5) {
1413 | 		I += integrate_edge(L_proj[4], L_proj[0], L[4], L[0]);
1414 | 	}
1415 | 	return abs(I);
1416 | }
1417 | 
1418 | void ltc_evaluate(vec3 normal, vec3 eye_vec, mat3 M_inv, vec3 points[4], vec4 texture_rect, float max_mipmap, texture2D area_light_atlas, sampler texture_sampler, out float integral, out vec3 tex_color) {
1419 | 	
1420 | 	tex_color = vec3(1.0);
1421 | 	
1422 | 	vec3 x, z;
1423 | 	z = -normalize(eye_vec - normal * dot(eye_vec, normal)); 
1424 | 	x = cross(normal, z);
1425 | 
1426 | 	
1427 | 	M_inv = M_inv * transpose(mat3(x, normal, z));
1428 | 
1429 | 	vec3 L[5];
1430 | 	L[0] = M_inv * points[0];
1431 | 	L[1] = M_inv * points[1];
1432 | 	L[2] = M_inv * points[2];
1433 | 	L[3] = M_inv * points[3];
1434 | 
1435 | 	vec3 L_unclipped[4];
1436 | 	L_unclipped[0] = L[0];
1437 | 	L_unclipped[1] = L[1];
1438 | 	L_unclipped[2] = L[2];
1439 | 	L_unclipped[3] = L[3];
1440 | 
1441 | 	int n;
1442 | 	clip_quad_to_horizon(L, n);
1443 | 	if (n == 0) {
1444 | 		integral = 0.0;
1445 | 		return;
1446 | 	}
1447 | 
1448 | 	
1449 | 	vec3 L_proj[5];
1450 | 	L_proj[0] = normalize(L[0]);
1451 | 	L_proj[1] = normalize(L[1]);
1452 | 	L_proj[2] = normalize(L[2]);
1453 | 	L_proj[3] = normalize(L[3]);
1454 | 	L_proj[4] = normalize(L[4]);
1455 | 
1456 | 	if (texture_rect != vec4(0.0)) {
1457 | 		tex_color = vec3(fetch_ltc_filtered_texture_with_form_factor(texture_rect, L_unclipped, max_mipmap, area_light_atlas, texture_sampler));
1458 | 	}
1459 | 
1460 | 	
1461 | 	vec3 pnorm = normalize(cross(L_proj[0] - L_proj[1], L_proj[2] - L_proj[1]));
1462 | 	if (abs(dot(pnorm, L_proj[0])) < 1e-10) {
1463 | 		
1464 | 		
1465 | 		vec3 r10 = points[0] - points[1];
1466 | 		vec3 r12 = points[2] - points[1];
1467 | 		float alpha = -dot(points[1], r10) / dot(r10, r10);
1468 | 		float beta = -dot(points[1], r12) / dot(r12, r12);
1469 | 		if (0.0 < alpha && alpha < 1.0 && 0.0 < beta && beta < 1.0) { 
1470 | 			integral = 1.0;
1471 | 			return;
1472 | 		} else {
1473 | 			integral = 0.0;
1474 | 			return;
1475 | 		}
1476 | 	}
1477 | 
1478 | 	float I = ltc_integrate_clipped_quad(L, L_proj, n);
1479 | 	integral = I / (2.0 * M_PI);
1480 | }
1481 | 
1482 | void ltc_evaluate_specular(vec3 normal, vec3 eye_vec, float roughness, vec3 points[4], vec4 texture_rect, float max_mipmap, texture2D area_light_atlas, sampler texture_sampler, sampler2D ltc_lut1, sampler2D ltc_lut2, out float ltc_specular, out vec2 fresnel, out vec3 ltc_specular_tex_color) {
1483 | 	float theta = acos_approx(dot(normal, eye_vec));
1484 | 	const float LTC_LUT_SIZE = float(64.0);
1485 | 	vec2 lut_pos = vec2(max(roughness, float(0.02)), theta / float(0.5 * M_PI));
1486 | 	vec2 lut_uv = vec2(lut_pos * (float(63.0) / LTC_LUT_SIZE) + vec2(float(0.5) / LTC_LUT_SIZE)); 
1487 | 	vec4 M_brdf_abcd = texture(ltc_lut1, lut_uv);
1488 | 	vec3 M_brdf_e_mag_fres = texture(ltc_lut2, lut_uv).xyz;
1489 | 	float scale = 1.0 / (M_brdf_abcd.x * M_brdf_e_mag_fres.x - M_brdf_abcd.y * M_brdf_abcd.w);
1490 | 
1491 | 	mat3 M_inv = mat3(
1492 | 			vec3(0, 0, 1.0 / M_brdf_abcd.z),
1493 | 			vec3(-M_brdf_abcd.w * scale, M_brdf_abcd.x * scale, 0),
1494 | 			vec3(-M_brdf_e_mag_fres.x * scale, M_brdf_abcd.y * scale, 0));
1495 | 
1496 | 	ltc_evaluate(normal, eye_vec, M_inv, points, texture_rect, max_mipmap, area_light_atlas, texture_sampler, ltc_specular, ltc_specular_tex_color);
1497 | 	fresnel = vec2(M_brdf_e_mag_fres.yz);
1498 | }
1499 | 
1500 | void ltc_evaluate_diff(vec3 normal, vec3 points[4], vec4 texture_rect, float max_mipmap, texture2D area_light_atlas, sampler texture_sampler, out float integral, out vec3 tex_color) {
1501 | 	
1502 | 	tex_color = vec3(1.0);
1503 | 	
1504 | 	vec3 x, z;
1505 | 	vec3 eye_vec = abs(normal.z) < 0.7 ? vec3(0.0, 0.0, -1.0) : vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0);
1506 | 	z = -normalize(eye_vec - normal * dot(eye_vec, normal)); 
1507 | 	x = cross(normal, z);
1508 | 
1509 | 	
1510 | 	mat3 M_inv = transpose(mat3(x, normal, z));
1511 | 
1512 | 	vec3 L[5];
1513 | 	L[0] = M_inv * points[0];
1514 | 	L[1] = M_inv * points[1];
1515 | 	L[2] = M_inv * points[2];
1516 | 	L[3] = M_inv * points[3];
1517 | 
1518 | 	vec3 L_unclipped[4];
1519 | 	L_unclipped[0] = L[0];
1520 | 	L_unclipped[1] = L[1];
1521 | 	L_unclipped[2] = L[2];
1522 | 	L_unclipped[3] = L[3];
1523 | 
1524 | 	int n;
1525 | 	clip_quad_to_horizon(L, n);
1526 | 	if (n == 0) {
1527 | 		integral = 0.0;
1528 | 		return;
1529 | 	}
1530 | 
1531 | 	
1532 | 	vec3 L_proj[5];
1533 | 	L_proj[0] = normalize(L[0]);
1534 | 	L_proj[1] = normalize(L[1]);
1535 | 	L_proj[2] = normalize(L[2]);
1536 | 	L_proj[3] = normalize(L[3]);
1537 | 	L_proj[4] = normalize(L[4]);
1538 | 
1539 | 	
1540 | 	vec3 pnorm = normalize(cross(L_proj[0] - L_proj[1], L_proj[2] - L_proj[1]));
1541 | 	if (abs(dot(pnorm, L_proj[0])) < 1e-10) {
1542 | 		
1543 | 		
1544 | 		integral = 0.0;
1545 | 		return;
1546 | 	}
1547 | 
1548 | 	if (texture_rect != vec4(0.0)) {
1549 | 		tex_color = fetch_ltc_filtered_texture_with_form_factor(texture_rect, L_unclipped, max_mipmap, area_light_atlas, texture_sampler);
1550 | 	}
1551 | 
1552 | 	float I = ltc_integrate_clipped_quad(L, L_proj, n);
1553 | 	integral = I; 
1554 | }

1567 | half D_GGX(half NoH, half roughness, hvec3 n, hvec3 h) {
1568 | 	half a = NoH * roughness;
1569 | #ifdef EXPLICIT_FP16
1570 | 	hvec3 NxH = cross(n, h);
1571 | 	half k = roughness / (dot(NxH, NxH) + a * a);
1572 | #else
1573 | 	float k = roughness / (1.0 - NoH * NoH + a * a);
1574 | #endif
1575 | 	half d = k * k * half(1.0 / M_PI);
1576 | 	return saturateHalf(d);
1577 | }
1578 | 
1579 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
1580 | #ifdef SSS_MODE_SKIN
1581 | hvec3 SSS_skin(half NdotL, half transmittance_depth, half transmittance_z, half transmittance_boost, hvec4 transmittance_color, hvec3 light_color) {
1582 | 	half scale = half(8.25) / transmittance_depth;
1583 | 	half d = scale * abs(transmittance_z);
1584 | 	float dd = float(-d * d);
1585 | 	hvec3 profile = hvec3(vec3(0.233, 0.455, 0.649) * exp(dd / 0.0064) +
1586 | 			vec3(0.1, 0.336, 0.344) * exp(dd / 0.0484) +
1587 | 			vec3(0.118, 0.198, 0.0) * exp(dd / 0.187) +
1588 | 			vec3(0.113, 0.007, 0.007) * exp(dd / 0.567) +
1589 | 			vec3(0.358, 0.004, 0.0) * exp(dd / 1.99) +
1590 | 			vec3(0.078, 0.0, 0.0) * exp(dd / 7.41));
1591 | 
1592 | 	return profile * transmittance_color.a * light_color * clamp(transmittance_boost - NdotL, half(0.0), half(1.0)) * half(1.0 / M_PI);
1593 | }
1594 | #else
1595 | hvec3 SSS(half NdotL, half transmittance_depth, half transmittance_z, half transmittance_boost, hvec4 transmittance_color, hvec3 light_color) {
1596 | 	half scale = half(8.25) / transmittance_depth;
1597 | 	half d = scale * abs(transmittance_z);
1598 | 	half dd = -d * d;
1599 | 	return exp(dd) * transmittance_color.rgb * transmittance_color.a * light_color * clamp(transmittance_boost - NdotL, half(0.0), half(1.0)) * half(1.0 / M_PI);
1600 | }
1601 | #endif
1602 | #endif

1604 | 
1605 | half V_GGX(half NdotL, half NdotV, half alpha) {
1606 | 	half v = half(0.5) / mix(half(2.0) * NdotL * NdotV, NdotL + NdotV, alpha);
1607 | 	return saturateHalf(v);
1608 | }
1609 | 
1610 | half D_GGX_anisotropic(half cos_theta_m, half alpha_x, half alpha_y, half cos_phi, half sin_phi) {
1611 | 	half alpha2 = alpha_x * alpha_y;
1612 | 	vec3 v = vec3(alpha_y * cos_phi, alpha_x * sin_phi, alpha2 * cos_theta_m);
1613 | 	float v2 = dot(v, v);
1614 | 	half w2 = half(float(alpha2) / v2);
1615 | 	return alpha2 * w2 * w2 * half(1.0 / M_PI);
1616 | }
1617 | 
1618 | half V_GGX_anisotropic(half alpha_x, half alpha_y, half TdotV, half TdotL, half BdotV, half BdotL, half NdotV, half NdotL) {
1619 | 	half Lambda_V = NdotL * length(hvec3(alpha_x * TdotV, alpha_y * BdotV, NdotV));
1620 | 	half Lambda_L = NdotV * length(hvec3(alpha_x * TdotL, alpha_y * BdotL, NdotL));
1621 | 	half v = half(0.5) / (Lambda_V + Lambda_L);
1622 | 	return saturateHalf(v);
1623 | }
1624 | 
1625 | half SchlickFresnel(half u) {
1626 | 	half m = half(1.0) - u;
1627 | 	half m2 = m * m;
1628 | 	return m2 * m2 * m; 
1629 | }
1630 | 
1631 | hvec3 F0(half metallic, half specular, hvec3 albedo) {
1632 | 	half dielectric = half(0.16) * specular * specular;
1633 | 	
1634 | 	
1635 | 	return mix(hvec3(dielectric), albedo, hvec3(metallic));
1636 | }
1637 | 
1638 | half V_Kelemen(half LdotH) {
1639 | 	
1640 | 	return saturateHalf(half(0.25) / (LdotH * LdotH + half(1e-4)));
1641 | }
1642 | 
1643 | hvec3 f0_Clear_Coat_To_Surface(hvec3 f0) {
1644 | 	
1645 | 	
1646 | 	
1647 | 	return clamp(f0 * (f0 * (half(0.941892) - half(0.263008) * f0) + half(0.346479)) - half(0.0285998), half(0.0), half(1.0));
1648 | }
1649 | 
1650 | void light_compute(hvec3 N, hvec3 L, hvec3 V, half A, hvec3 light_color, bool is_directional, half attenuation, hvec3 f0, half roughness, half metallic, half specular_amount, hvec3 albedo, inout half alpha, vec2 screen_uv, hvec3 energy_compensation,
1651 | #ifdef LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED
1652 | 		hvec3 backlight,
1653 | #endif
1654 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
1655 | 		hvec4 transmittance_color,
1656 | 		half transmittance_depth,
1657 | 		half transmittance_boost,
1658 | 		half transmittance_z,
1659 | #endif
1660 | #ifdef LIGHT_RIM_USED
1661 | 		half rim, half rim_tint,
1662 | #endif
1663 | #ifdef LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED
1664 | 		half clearcoat, half clearcoat_roughness, hvec3 vertex_normal,
1665 | #endif
1666 | #ifdef LIGHT_ANISOTROPY_USED
1667 | 		hvec3 B, hvec3 T, half anisotropy,
1668 | #endif
1669 | 		inout hvec3 diffuse_light, inout hvec3 specular_light) {
1670 | #if defined(LIGHT_CODE_USED)
1671 | 	
1672 | 	mat4 inv_view_matrix = transpose(mat4(scene_data_block.data.inv_view_matrix[0],
1673 | 			scene_data_block.data.inv_view_matrix[1],
1674 | 			scene_data_block.data.inv_view_matrix[2],
1675 | 			vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)));
1676 | 	mat4 read_view_matrix = transpose(mat4(scene_data_block.data.view_matrix[0],
1677 | 			scene_data_block.data.view_matrix[1],
1678 | 			scene_data_block.data.view_matrix[2],
1679 | 			vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)));
1680 | 
1681 | #ifdef USING_MOBILE_RENDERER
1682 | 	uint instance_index = draw_call.instance_index;
1683 | #else
1684 | 	uint instance_index = instance_index_interp;
1685 | #endif
1686 | 
1687 | 	mat4 read_model_matrix = transpose(mat4(instances.data[instance_index].transform[0],
1688 | 			instances.data[instance_index].transform[1],
1689 | 			instances.data[instance_index].transform[2],
1690 | 			vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)));
1691 | 
1692 | #undef projection_matrix
1693 | #define projection_matrix scene_data_block.data.projection_matrix
1694 | #undef inv_projection_matrix
1695 | #define inv_projection_matrix scene_data_block.data.inv_projection_matrix
1696 | 
1697 | 	vec2 read_viewport_size = scene_data_block.data.viewport_size;
1698 | 
1699 | #ifdef LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED
1700 | 	vec3 backlight_highp = vec3(backlight);
1701 | #endif
1702 | 	float roughness_highp = float(roughness);
1703 | 	float metallic_highp = float(metallic);
1704 | 	vec3 albedo_highp = vec3(albedo);
1705 | 	float alpha_highp = float(alpha);
1706 | 	vec3 normal_highp = vec3(N);
1707 | 	vec3 light_highp = vec3(L);
1708 | 	vec3 view_highp = vec3(V);
1709 | 	float specular_amount_highp = float(specular_amount);
1710 | 	vec3 light_color_highp = vec3(light_color);
1711 | 	float attenuation_highp = float(attenuation);
1712 | 	vec3 diffuse_light_highp = vec3(diffuse_light);
1713 | 	vec3 specular_light_highp = vec3(specular_light);
1714 | 	bool is_area = false;
1715 | 	float area_diffuse = 1.0;
1716 | 	float area_specular = 1.0;
1717 | 	vec3 area_diffuse_tex_color = vec3(1.0);
1718 | 	vec3 area_specular_tex_color = vec3(1.0);
1719 | 
1720 | 
1721 | 	alpha = half(alpha_highp);
1722 | 	diffuse_light = hvec3(diffuse_light_highp);
1723 | 	specular_light = hvec3(specular_light_highp);
1724 | #else 
1725 | 	half NdotL = min(A + dot(N, L), half(1.0));
1726 | 	half cNdotV = max(dot(N, V), half(1e-4));
1727 | 
1728 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
1729 | 	{
1730 | #ifdef SSS_MODE_SKIN
1731 | 		diffuse_light += SSS_skin(NdotL, transmittance_depth, transmittance_z, transmittance_boost, transmittance_color, light_color);
1732 | #else
1733 | 		diffuse_light += SSS(NdotL, transmittance_depth, transmittance_z, transmittance_boost, transmittance_color, light_color);
1734 | #endif
1735 | 	}
1736 | #endif 
1737 | 
1738 | #if defined(LIGHT_RIM_USED)
1739 | 	
1740 | 	half rim_light = pow(max(half(1e-4), half(1.0) - cNdotV), max(half(0.0), (half(1.0) - roughness) * half(16.0)));
1741 | 	diffuse_light += rim_light * rim * mix(hvec3(1.0), albedo, rim_tint) * light_color;
1742 | #endif
1743 | 
1744 | 	half cc_attenuation = half(1.0);
1745 | 
1746 | 	
1747 | 	if (is_directional || attenuation > HALF_FLT_MIN) {
1748 | 		half cNdotL = max(NdotL, half(0.0));
1749 | #if defined(DIFFUSE_BURLEY) || defined(SPECULAR_SCHLICK_GGX) || defined(LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED)
1750 | 		hvec3 H = normalize(V + L);
1751 | 		half cLdotH = clamp(A + dot(L, H), half(0.0), half(1.0));
1752 | #endif
1753 | 
1754 | #if defined(LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED)
1755 | 		
1756 | 		half ccNdotL = clamp(A + dot(vertex_normal, L), half(0.0), half(1.0));
1757 | 		half ccNdotH = clamp(A + dot(vertex_normal, H), half(0.0), half(1.0));
1758 | 		half cLdotH5 = SchlickFresnel(cLdotH);
1759 | 
1760 | 		half Dr = D_GGX(ccNdotH, half(mix(half(0.001), half(0.1), clearcoat_roughness)), vertex_normal, H);
1761 | 		half Gr = V_Kelemen(cLdotH);
1762 | 		half Fr = mix(half(0.04), half(1.0), cLdotH5) * clearcoat;
1763 | 		cc_attenuation = half(1.0) - Fr;
1764 | 		half clearcoat_specular_brdf_NL = Dr * Gr * Fr * ccNdotL;
1765 | 		specular_light += clearcoat_specular_brdf_NL * light_color * attenuation * specular_amount;
1766 | #endif 
1767 | 
1768 | 		if (metallic < half(1.0)) {
1769 | 			half diffuse_brdf_NL; 
1770 | 
1771 | #if defined(DIFFUSE_LAMBERT_WRAP)
1772 | 			
1773 | 			
1774 | 			half op_roughness = half(1.0) + roughness;
1775 | 			diffuse_brdf_NL = max(half(0.0), (NdotL + roughness) / (op_roughness * op_roughness)) * half(1.0 / M_PI);
1776 | #elif defined(DIFFUSE_TOON)
1777 | 
1778 | 			diffuse_brdf_NL = smoothstep(-roughness, max(roughness, half(0.01)), NdotL) * half(1.0 / M_PI);
1779 | 
1780 | #elif defined(DIFFUSE_BURLEY)
1781 | 			{
1782 | 				half FD90_minus_1 = half(2.0) * cLdotH * cLdotH * roughness - half(0.5);
1783 | 				half FdV = half(1.0) + FD90_minus_1 * SchlickFresnel(cNdotV);
1784 | 				half FdL = half(1.0) + FD90_minus_1 * SchlickFresnel(cNdotL);
1785 | 				diffuse_brdf_NL = half(1.0 / M_PI) * FdV * FdL * cNdotL;
1786 | 			}
1787 | #else
1788 | 			
1789 | 			diffuse_brdf_NL = cNdotL * half(1.0 / M_PI);
1790 | #endif
1791 | 
1792 | 			diffuse_light += light_color * diffuse_brdf_NL * attenuation * cc_attenuation;
1793 | 
1794 | #if defined(LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED)
1795 | 			diffuse_light += light_color * (hvec3(1.0 / M_PI) - diffuse_brdf_NL) * backlight * attenuation;
1796 | #endif
1797 | 		}
1798 | 
1799 | 		if (roughness > half(0.0)) {
1800 | #if defined(SPECULAR_SCHLICK_GGX)
1801 | 			half cNdotH = clamp(A + dot(N, H), half(0.0), half(1.0));
1802 | #endif
1803 | 			
1804 | 			
1805 | #if defined(SPECULAR_TOON)
1806 | 			hvec3 R = normalize(-reflect(L, N));
1807 | 			half RdotV = dot(R, V);
1808 | 			half mid = half(1.0) - roughness;
1809 | 			mid *= mid;
1810 | 			half intensity = smoothstep(mid - roughness * half(0.5), mid + roughness * half(0.5), RdotV) * mid;
1811 | 			diffuse_light += light_color * intensity * attenuation * specular_amount; 
1812 | 
1813 | #elif defined(SPECULAR_DISABLED)
1814 | 			
1815 | 
1816 | #elif defined(SPECULAR_SCHLICK_GGX)
1817 | 			
1818 | 			half alpha_ggx = roughness * roughness;
1819 | #if defined(LIGHT_ANISOTROPY_USED)
1820 | 			half aspect = sqrt(half(1.0) - anisotropy * half(0.9));
1821 | 			half ax = alpha_ggx / aspect;
1822 | 			half ay = alpha_ggx * aspect;
1823 | 			half XdotH = dot(T, H);
1824 | 			half YdotH = dot(B, H);
1825 | 			half D = D_GGX_anisotropic(cNdotH, ax, ay, XdotH, YdotH);
1826 | 			half G = V_GGX_anisotropic(ax, ay, dot(T, V), dot(T, L), dot(B, V), dot(B, L), cNdotV, cNdotL);
1827 | #else 
1828 | 			half D = D_GGX(cNdotH, alpha_ggx, N, H);
1829 | 			half G = V_GGX(cNdotL, cNdotV, alpha_ggx);
1830 | #endif 
1831 | 	   
1832 | #if !defined(LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED)
1833 | 			half cLdotH5 = SchlickFresnel(cLdotH);
1834 | #endif
1835 | 			
1836 | 			
1837 | 			half f90 = clamp(dot(f0, hvec3(50.0 * 0.33)), metallic, half(1.0));
1838 | 			hvec3 F = f0 + (f90 - f0) * cLdotH5;
1839 | 			hvec3 specular_brdf_NL = energy_compensation * cNdotL * D * F * G;
1840 | 			specular_light += specular_brdf_NL * light_color * attenuation * cc_attenuation * specular_amount;
1841 | #endif
1842 | 		}
1843 | 
1844 | #ifdef USE_SHADOW_TO_OPACITY
1845 | 		alpha = min(alpha, clamp(half(1.0 - attenuation), half(0.0), half(1.0)));
1846 | #endif
1847 | 	}
1848 | #endif 
1849 | }
1850 | 
1851 | #ifndef SHADOWS_DISABLED
1852 | 
1853 | 
1854 | 
1855 | float quick_hash(vec2 pos) {
1856 | 	const vec3 magic = vec3(0.06711056f, 0.00583715f, 52.9829189f);
1857 | 	return fract(magic.z * fract(dot(pos, magic.xy)));
1858 | }

1860 | half sample_directional_pcf_shadow(texture2D shadow, vec2 shadow_pixel_size, vec4 coord, float taa_frame_count) {
1861 | 	vec2 pos = coord.xy;
1862 | 	float depth = coord.z;
1863 | 
1864 | 	
1865 | 	if (sc_directional_soft_shadow_samples() == 0) {
1866 | 		return half(textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow, shadow_sampler), vec4(pos, depth, 1.0)));
1867 | 	}
1868 | 
1869 | 	mat2 disk_rotation;
1870 | 	{
1871 | 		float r = quick_hash(gl_FragCoord.xy + vec2(taa_frame_count * 5.588238)) * 2.0 * M_PI;
1872 | 		float sr = sin(r);
1873 | 		float cr = cos(r);
1874 | 		disk_rotation = mat2(vec2(cr, -sr), vec2(sr, cr));
1875 | 	}
1876 | 
1877 | 	float avg = 0.0;
1878 | 
1879 | 	SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
1880 | 	for (uint i = 0; i < sc_directional_soft_shadow_samples(); i++) {
1881 | 		avg += textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow, shadow_sampler), vec4(pos + shadow_pixel_size * (disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.directional_soft_shadow_kernel[i].xy), depth, 1.0));
1882 | 	}
1883 | 
1884 | 	return half(avg * (1.0 / float(sc_directional_soft_shadow_samples())));
1885 | }
1886 | 
1887 | half sample_pcf_shadow(texture2D shadow, vec2 shadow_pixel_size, vec3 coord, float taa_frame_count) {
1888 | 	vec2 pos = coord.xy;
1889 | 	float depth = coord.z;
1890 | 
1891 | 	
1892 | 	if (sc_soft_shadow_samples() == 0) {
1893 | 		return half(textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow, shadow_sampler), vec4(pos, depth, 1.0)));
1894 | 	}
1895 | 
1896 | 	mat2 disk_rotation;
1897 | 	{
1898 | 		float r = quick_hash(gl_FragCoord.xy + vec2(taa_frame_count * 5.588238)) * 2.0 * M_PI;
1899 | 		float sr = sin(r);
1900 | 		float cr = cos(r);
1901 | 		disk_rotation = mat2(vec2(cr, -sr), vec2(sr, cr));
1902 | 	}
1903 | 
1904 | 	float avg = 0.0;
1905 | 
1906 | 	SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
1907 | 	for (uint i = 0; i < sc_soft_shadow_samples(); i++) {
1908 | 		avg += textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow, shadow_sampler), vec4(pos + shadow_pixel_size * (disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.soft_shadow_kernel[i].xy), depth, 1.0));
1909 | 	}
1910 |

1912 | }
1913 | 
1914 | half sample_omni_pcf_shadow(texture2D shadow, float blur_scale, vec2 coord, vec4 uv_rect, vec2 flip_offset, float depth, float taa_frame_count) {
1915 | 	
1916 | 	if (sc_soft_shadow_samples() == 0) {
1917 | 		vec2 pos = coord * 0.5 + 0.5;
1918 | 		pos = uv_rect.xy + pos * uv_rect.zw;
1919 | 		return half(textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow, shadow_sampler), vec4(pos, depth, 1.0)));
1920 | 	}
1921 | 
1922 | 	mat2 disk_rotation;
1923 | 	{
1924 | 		float r = quick_hash(gl_FragCoord.xy + vec2(taa_frame_count * 5.588238)) * 2.0 * M_PI;
1925 | 		float sr = sin(r);
1926 | 		float cr = cos(r);
1927 | 		disk_rotation = mat2(vec2(cr, -sr), vec2(sr, cr));
1928 | 	}
1929 | 
1930 | 	float avg = 0.0;
1931 | 	vec2 offset_scale = blur_scale * 2.0 * scene_data_block.data.shadow_atlas_pixel_size / uv_rect.zw;
1932 | 
1933 | 	SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
1934 | 	for (uint i = 0; i < sc_soft_shadow_samples(); i++) {
1935 | 		vec2 offset = offset_scale * (disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.soft_shadow_kernel[i].xy);
1936 | 		vec2 sample_coord = coord + offset;
1937 | 
1938 | 		float sample_coord_length_squared = dot(sample_coord, sample_coord);
1939 | 		bool do_flip = sample_coord_length_squared > 1.0;
1940 | 
1941 | 		if (do_flip) {
1942 | 			float len = sqrt(sample_coord_length_squared);
1943 | 			sample_coord = sample_coord * (2.0 / len - 1.0);
1944 | 		}
1945 | 
1946 | 		sample_coord = sample_coord * 0.5 + 0.5;
1947 | 		sample_coord = uv_rect.xy + sample_coord * uv_rect.zw;
1948 | 
1949 | 		if (do_flip) {
1950 | 			sample_coord += flip_offset;
1951 | 		}
1952 | 		avg += textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow, shadow_sampler), vec4(sample_coord, depth, 1.0));
1953 | 	}
1954 | 
1955 | 	return half(avg * (1.0 / float(sc_soft_shadow_samples())));
1956 | }
1957 | 
1958 | half sample_directional_soft_shadow(texture2D shadow, vec3 pssm_coord, vec2 tex_scale, float taa_frame_count) {
1959 | 	
1960 | 	float blocker_count = 0.0;
1961 | 	float blocker_average = 0.0;
1962 | 
1963 | 	mat2 disk_rotation;
1964 | 	{
1965 | 		float r = quick_hash(gl_FragCoord.xy + vec2(taa_frame_count * 5.588238)) * 2.0 * M_PI;
1966 | 		float sr = sin(r);
1967 | 		float cr = cos(r);
1968 | 		disk_rotation = mat2(vec2(cr, -sr), vec2(sr, cr));
1969 | 	}
1970 | 
1971 | 	SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
1972 | 	for (uint i = 0; i < sc_directional_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
1973 | 		vec2 suv = pssm_coord.xy + (disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.directional_penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy) * tex_scale;
1974 | 		float d = textureLod(sampler2D(shadow, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), suv, 0.0).r;
1975 | 		if (d > pssm_coord.z) {
1976 | 			blocker_average += d;
1977 | 			blocker_count += 1.0;
1978 | 		}
1979 | 	}
1980 | 
1981 | 	if (blocker_count > 0.0) {
1982 | 		
1983 | 		blocker_average /= blocker_count;
1984 | 		float penumbra = (-pssm_coord.z + blocker_average) / (1.0 - blocker_average);
1985 | 		tex_scale *= penumbra;
1986 | 
1987 | 		float s = 0.0;
1988 | 
1989 | 		SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
1990 | 		for (uint i = 0; i < sc_directional_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
1991 | 			vec2 suv = pssm_coord.xy + (disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.directional_penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy) * tex_scale;
1992 | 			s += textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow, shadow_sampler), vec4(suv, pssm_coord.z, 1.0));
1993 | 		}
1994 | 
1995 | 		return half(s / float(sc_directional_penumbra_shadow_samples()));
1996 | 
1997 | 	} else {
1998 | 		
1999 | 		return half(1.0);
2000 | 	}
2001 | }
2002 | 
2003 | #endif 
2004 | 
2005 | half get_omni_attenuation(float distance, float inv_range, float decay) {
2006 | 	float nd = distance * inv_range;
2007 | 	nd *= nd;
2008 | 	nd *= nd; 
2009 | 	nd = max(1.0 - nd, 0.0);
2010 | 	nd *= nd; 
2011 | 	return half(nd * pow(max(distance, 0.0001), -decay));
2012 | }

2014 | void light_process_omni(uint idx, vec3 vertex, hvec3 eye_vec, hvec3 normal, vec3 vertex_ddx, vec3 vertex_ddy, hvec3 f0, half roughness, half metallic, float taa_frame_count, hvec3 albedo, inout half alpha, vec2 screen_uv, hvec3 energy_compensation,
2015 | #ifdef LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED
2016 | 		hvec3 backlight,
2017 | #endif
2018 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
2019 | 		hvec4 transmittance_color,
2020 | 		half transmittance_depth,
2021 | 		half transmittance_boost,
2022 | #endif
2023 | #ifdef LIGHT_RIM_USED
2024 | 		half rim, half rim_tint,
2025 | #endif
2026 | #ifdef LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED
2027 | 		half clearcoat, half clearcoat_roughness, hvec3 vertex_normal,
2028 | #endif
2029 | #ifdef LIGHT_ANISOTROPY_USED
2030 | 		hvec3 binormal, hvec3 tangent, half anisotropy,
2031 | #endif
2032 | 		inout hvec3 diffuse_light, inout hvec3 specular_light) {
2033 | 
2034 | 	
2035 | 	vec3 light_rel_vec = omni_lights.data[idx].position - vertex;
2036 | 	float light_length = length(light_rel_vec);
2037 | 	half omni_attenuation = get_omni_attenuation(light_length, omni_lights.data[idx].inv_radius, omni_lights.data[idx].attenuation);
2038 | 
2039 | 	
2040 | 	half size = half(0.0);
2041 | 	if (sc_use_light_soft_shadows() && omni_lights.data[idx].size > 0.0) {
2042 | 		half t = half(omni_lights.data[idx].size / max(0.001, light_length));
2043 | 		size = half(1.0) / sqrt(half(1.0) + t * t);
2044 | 		size = max(half(1.0) - size, half(0.0));
2045 | 	}
2046 | 
2047 | 	half shadow = half(1.0);
2048 | #ifndef SHADOWS_DISABLED
2049 | 	
2050 | 	if (omni_attenuation > HALF_FLT_MIN && omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity > 0.001) {
2051 | 		
2052 | 		vec2 texel_size = scene_data_block.data.shadow_atlas_pixel_size;
2053 | 		vec4 base_uv_rect = omni_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect;
2054 | 		base_uv_rect.xy += texel_size;
2055 | 		base_uv_rect.zw -= texel_size * 2.0;
2056 | 
2057 | 		
2058 | 		vec2 flip_offset = omni_lights.data[idx].direction.xy;
2059 | 
2060 | 		vec3 local_vert = (omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex, 1.0)).xyz;
2061 | 
2062 | 		float shadow_len = length(local_vert); 
2063 | 		vec3 shadow_dir = normalize(local_vert);
2064 | 
2065 | 		vec3 local_normal = normalize(mat3(omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix) * vec3(normal));
2066 | 		vec3 normal_bias = local_normal * omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_normal_bias * (1.0 - abs(dot(local_normal, shadow_dir)));
2067 | 
2068 | 		if (sc_use_light_soft_shadows() && omni_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size > 0.0) {
2069 | 			
2070 | 
2071 | 			
2072 | 
2073 | 			float blocker_count = 0.0;
2074 | 			float blocker_average = 0.0;
2075 | 
2076 | 			mat2 disk_rotation;
2077 | 			{
2078 | 				float r = quick_hash(gl_FragCoord.xy + vec2(taa_frame_count * 5.588238)) * 2.0 * M_PI;
2079 | 				float sr = sin(r);
2080 | 				float cr = cos(r);
2081 | 				disk_rotation = mat2(vec2(cr, -sr), vec2(sr, cr));
2082 | 			}
2083 | 
2084 | 			vec3 basis_normal = shadow_dir;
2085 | 			vec3 v0 = abs(basis_normal.z) < 0.999 ? vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0) : vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);
2086 | 			vec3 tangent = normalize(cross(v0, basis_normal));
2087 | 			vec3 bitangent = normalize(cross(tangent, basis_normal));
2088 | 			float z_norm = 1.0 - shadow_len * omni_lights.data[idx].inv_radius;
2089 | 
2090 | 			tangent *= omni_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size * omni_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_scale;
2091 | 			bitangent *= omni_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size * omni_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_scale;
2092 | 
2093 | 			SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
2094 | 			for (uint i = 0; i < sc_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
2095 | 				vec2 disk = disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy;
2096 | 
2097 | 				vec3 pos = local_vert + tangent * disk.x + bitangent * disk.y;
2098 | 
2099 | 				pos = normalize(pos);
2100 | 
2101 | 				vec4 uv_rect = base_uv_rect;
2102 | 
2103 | 				if (pos.z >= 0.0) {
2104 | 					uv_rect.xy += flip_offset;
2105 | 				}
2106 | 
2107 | 				pos.z = 1.0 + abs(pos.z);
2108 | 				pos.xy /= pos.z;
2109 | 
2110 | 				pos.xy = pos.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;
2111 | 				pos.xy = uv_rect.xy + pos.xy * uv_rect.zw;
2112 |

2114 | 				if (d > z_norm) {
2115 | 					blocker_average += d;
2116 | 					blocker_count += 1.0;
2117 | 				}
2118 | 			}
2119 | 
2120 | 			if (blocker_count > 0.0) {
2121 | 				
2122 | 				blocker_average /= blocker_count;
2123 | 				float penumbra = (-z_norm + blocker_average) / (1.0 - blocker_average);
2124 | 				tangent *= penumbra;
2125 | 				bitangent *= penumbra;
2126 | 
2127 | 				z_norm += omni_lights.data[idx].inv_radius * omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_bias;
2128 | 
2129 | 				shadow = half(0.0);
2130 | 
2131 | 				SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
2132 | 				for (uint i = 0; i < sc_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
2133 | 					vec2 disk = disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy;
2134 | 					vec3 pos = local_vert + tangent * disk.x + bitangent * disk.y;
2135 | 
2136 | 					pos = normalize(pos);
2137 | 					pos = normalize(pos + normal_bias);
2138 | 
2139 | 					vec4 uv_rect = base_uv_rect;
2140 | 
2141 | 					if (pos.z >= 0.0) {
2142 | 						uv_rect.xy += flip_offset;
2143 | 					}
2144 | 
2145 | 					pos.z = 1.0 + abs(pos.z);
2146 | 					pos.xy /= pos.z;
2147 | 
2148 | 					pos.xy = pos.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;
2149 | 					pos.xy = uv_rect.xy + pos.xy * uv_rect.zw;
2150 | 					shadow += half(textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow_atlas, shadow_sampler), vec4(pos.xy, z_norm, 1.0)));
2151 | 				}
2152 | 
2153 | 				shadow /= half(sc_penumbra_shadow_samples());
2154 | 				shadow = mix(half(1.0), shadow, half(omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity));
2155 | 
2156 | 			} else {
2157 | 				
2158 | 				shadow = half(1.0);
2159 | 			}
2160 | 		} else {
2161 | 			vec4 uv_rect = base_uv_rect;
2162 | 
2163 | 			vec3 shadow_sample = normalize(shadow_dir + normal_bias);
2164 | 			if (shadow_sample.z >= 0.0) {
2165 | 				uv_rect.xy += flip_offset;
2166 | 				flip_offset *= -1.0;
2167 | 			}
2168 | 
2169 | 			shadow_sample.z = 1.0 + abs(shadow_sample.z);
2170 | 			vec2 pos = shadow_sample.xy / shadow_sample.z;
2171 | 			float depth = shadow_len - omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_bias;
2172 | 			depth *= omni_lights.data[idx].inv_radius;
2173 | 			depth = 1.0 - depth;
2174 | 			shadow = mix(half(1.0), sample_omni_pcf_shadow(shadow_atlas, omni_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_scale / shadow_sample.z, pos, uv_rect, flip_offset, depth, taa_frame_count), half(omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity));
2175 | 		}
2176 | 	}
2177 | #endif
2178 | 
2179 | 	vec3 color = omni_lights.data[idx].color;
2180 | 
2181 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
2182 | 	half transmittance_z = transmittance_depth; 
2183 | 	transmittance_color.a *= omni_attenuation;
2184 | #ifndef SHADOWS_DISABLED
2185 | 	if (omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity > 0.001) {
2186 | 		
2187 | 		vec2 texel_size = scene_data_block.data.shadow_atlas_pixel_size;
2188 | 		vec4 uv_rect = omni_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect;
2189 | 		uv_rect.xy += texel_size;
2190 | 		uv_rect.zw -= texel_size * 2.0;
2191 | 
2192 | 		
2193 | 		vec2 flip_offset = omni_lights.data[idx].direction.xy;
2194 | 
2195 | 		vec3 local_vert = (omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex - normal * omni_lights.data[idx].transmittance_bias, 1.0)).xyz;
2196 | 
2197 | 		float shadow_len = length(local_vert); 
2198 | 		vec3 shadow_sample = normalize(local_vert);
2199 | 
2200 | 		if (shadow_sample.z >= 0.0) {
2201 | 			uv_rect.xy += flip_offset;
2202 | 			flip_offset *= -1.0;
2203 | 		}
2204 | 
2205 | 		shadow_sample.z = 1.0 + abs(shadow_sample.z);
2206 | 		vec2 pos = shadow_sample.xy / shadow_sample.z;
2207 | 		float depth = shadow_len * omni_lights.data[idx].inv_radius;
2208 | 		depth = 1.0 - depth;
2209 | 
2210 | 		pos = pos * 0.5 + 0.5;
2211 | 		pos = uv_rect.xy + pos * uv_rect.zw;
2212 | 		float shadow_z = textureLod(sampler2D(shadow_atlas, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), pos, 0.0).r;
2213 | 		transmittance_z = half((depth - shadow_z) / omni_lights.data[idx].inv_radius);
2214 | 	}
2215 | #endif 
2216 | #endif 
2217 | 
2218 | 	if (sc_use_light_projector() && omni_lights.data[idx].projector_rect != vec4(0.0)) {
2219 | 		vec3 local_v = (omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex, 1.0)).xyz;
2220 | 		local_v = normalize(local_v);
2221 | 
2222 | 		vec4 atlas_rect = omni_lights.data[idx].projector_rect;
2223 | 
2224 | 		if (local_v.z >= 0.0) {
2225 | 			atlas_rect.y += atlas_rect.w;
2226 | 		}
2227 | 
2228 | 		local_v.z = 1.0 + abs(local_v.z);
2229 | 
2230 | 		local_v.xy /= local_v.z;
2231 | 		local_v.xy = local_v.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;
2232 | 		vec2 proj_uv = local_v.xy * atlas_rect.zw;
2233 | 
2234 | 		
2235 | 		vec2 texel_size = 1.0 / vec2(textureSize(sampler2D(decal_atlas_srgb, light_projector_sampler), 0));
2236 | 		proj_uv = clamp(proj_uv, texel_size * 0.5, atlas_rect.zw - texel_size * 0.5);
2237 | 
2238 | 		if (sc_projector_use_mipmaps()) {
2239 | 			vec2 proj_uv_ddx;
2240 | 			vec2 proj_uv_ddy;
2241 | 			{
2242 | 				vec3 local_v_ddx = (omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex + vertex_ddx, 1.0)).xyz;
2243 | 				local_v_ddx = normalize(local_v_ddx);
2244 | 
2245 | 				if (local_v_ddx.z >= 0.0) {
2246 | 					local_v_ddx.z += 1.0;
2247 | 				} else {
2248 | 					local_v_ddx.z = 1.0 - local_v_ddx.z;
2249 | 				}
2250 | 
2251 | 				local_v_ddx.xy /= local_v_ddx.z;

2255 | 
2256 | 				vec3 local_v_ddy = (omni_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex + vertex_ddy, 1.0)).xyz;
2257 | 				local_v_ddy = normalize(local_v_ddy);
2258 | 
2259 | 				if (local_v_ddy.z >= 0.0) {
2260 | 					local_v_ddy.z += 1.0;
2261 | 				} else {
2262 | 					local_v_ddy.z = 1.0 - local_v_ddy.z;
2263 | 				}

2275 | 			color *= proj.rgb * proj.a;
2276 | 		}
2277 | 	}
2278 | 
2279 | 	vec3 light_rel_vec_norm = light_rel_vec / light_length;
2280 | 	light_compute(normal, hvec3(light_rel_vec_norm), eye_vec, size, hvec3(color), false, omni_attenuation * shadow, f0, roughness, metallic, half(omni_lights.data[idx].specular_amount), albedo, alpha, screen_uv, energy_compensation,
2281 | #ifdef LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED
2282 | 			backlight,
2283 | #endif
2284 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED

2299 | 			diffuse_light,
2300 | 			specular_light);

2303 | vec2 normal_to_panorama(vec3 n) {
2304 | 	n = normalize(n);
2305 | 	vec2 panorama_coords = vec2(atan(n.x, n.z), acos(-n.y));
2306 | 
2307 | 	if (panorama_coords.x < 0.0) {
2308 | 		panorama_coords.x += M_PI * 2.0;
2309 | 	}
2310 | 
2311 | 	panorama_coords /= vec2(M_PI * 2.0, M_PI);
2312 | 	return panorama_coords;
2313 | }

2315 | void light_process_spot(uint idx, vec3 vertex, hvec3 eye_vec, hvec3 normal, vec3 vertex_ddx, vec3 vertex_ddy, hvec3 f0, half roughness, half metallic, float taa_frame_count, hvec3 albedo, inout half alpha, vec2 screen_uv, hvec3 energy_compensation,
2316 | #ifdef LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED
2317 | 		hvec3 backlight,
2318 | #endif
2319 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
2320 | 		hvec4 transmittance_color,
2321 | 		half transmittance_depth,
2322 | 		half transmittance_boost,
2323 | #endif
2324 | #ifdef LIGHT_RIM_USED
2325 | 		half rim, half rim_tint,
2326 | #endif
2327 | #ifdef LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED
2328 | 		half clearcoat, half clearcoat_roughness, hvec3 vertex_normal,
2329 | #endif
2330 | #ifdef LIGHT_ANISOTROPY_USED
2331 | 		hvec3 binormal, hvec3 tangent, half anisotropy,
2332 | #endif
2333 | 		inout hvec3 diffuse_light,
2334 | 		inout hvec3 specular_light) {
2335 | 
2336 | 	
2337 | 	vec3 light_rel_vec = spot_lights.data[idx].position - vertex;
2338 | 	float light_length = length(light_rel_vec);
2339 | 	hvec3 light_rel_vec_norm = hvec3(light_rel_vec / light_length);
2340 | 	half spot_attenuation = get_omni_attenuation(light_length, spot_lights.data[idx].inv_radius, spot_lights.data[idx].attenuation);
2341 | 	vec3 spot_dir = spot_lights.data[idx].direction;
2342 | 	float cone_angle = spot_lights.data[idx].cone_angle;
2343 | 	float scos = max(dot(-vec3(light_rel_vec_norm), spot_dir), cone_angle);
2344 | 
2345 | 	
2346 | 	float spot_rim = max(1e-4, (1.0 - scos) / (1.0 - cone_angle));
2347 | 	spot_attenuation *= half(1.0 - pow(spot_rim, spot_lights.data[idx].cone_attenuation));
2348 | 
2349 | 	
2350 | 	half size = half(0.0);
2351 | 	if (sc_use_light_soft_shadows() && spot_lights.data[idx].size > 0.0) {
2352 | 		half t = half(spot_lights.data[idx].size / max(0.001, light_length));
2353 | 		size = half(1.0) / sqrt(half(1.0) + t * t);
2354 | 		size = max(half(1.0) - size, half(0.0));
2355 | 	}
2356 | 
2357 | 	half shadow = half(1.0);
2358 | #ifndef SHADOWS_DISABLED
2359 | 	
2360 | 	if (spot_attenuation > HALF_FLT_MIN && spot_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity > 0.001) {
2361 | 		vec3 normal_bias = vec3(normal) * light_length * spot_lights.data[idx].shadow_normal_bias * (1.0 - abs(dot(normal, light_rel_vec_norm)));
2362 | 
2363 | 		
2364 | 		vec4 v = vec4(vertex + normal_bias, 1.0);
2365 | 
2366 | 		vec4 splane = (spot_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * v);
2367 | 		splane.z += spot_lights.data[idx].shadow_bias;
2368 | 		splane /= splane.w;
2369 | 
2370 | 		if (sc_use_light_soft_shadows() && spot_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size > 0.0) {
2371 | 			
2372 | 
2373 | 			
2374 | 			float z_norm = dot(spot_dir, -light_rel_vec) * spot_lights.data[idx].inv_radius;
2375 | 
2376 | 			vec2 shadow_uv = splane.xy * spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.zw + spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.xy;
2377 | 
2378 | 			float blocker_count = 0.0;
2379 | 			float blocker_average = 0.0;
2380 | 
2381 | 			mat2 disk_rotation;
2382 | 			{
2383 | 				float r = quick_hash(gl_FragCoord.xy + vec2(taa_frame_count * 5.588238)) * 2.0 * M_PI;
2384 | 				float sr = sin(r);
2385 | 				float cr = cos(r);
2386 | 				disk_rotation = mat2(vec2(cr, -sr), vec2(sr, cr));
2387 | 			}
2388 | 
2389 | 			float uv_size = spot_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size * z_norm * spot_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_scale;
2390 | 			vec2 clamp_max = spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.xy + spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.zw;
2391 | 
2392 | 			SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
2393 | 			for (uint i = 0; i < sc_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
2394 | 				vec2 suv = shadow_uv + (disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy) * uv_size;
2395 | 				suv = clamp(suv, spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.xy, clamp_max);
2396 | 				float d = textureLod(sampler2D(shadow_atlas, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), suv, 0.0).r;
2397 | 				if (d > splane.z) {
2398 | 					blocker_average += d;
2399 | 					blocker_count += 1.0;
2400 | 				}
2401 | 			}
2402 | 
2403 | 			if (blocker_count > 0.0) {
2404 | 				
2405 | 				blocker_average /= blocker_count;
2406 | 				float penumbra = (-z_norm + blocker_average) / (1.0 - blocker_average);
2407 | 				uv_size *= penumbra;
2408 | 
2409 | 				shadow = half(0.0);
2410 | 
2411 | 				SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
2412 | 				for (uint i = 0; i < sc_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
2413 | 					vec2 suv = shadow_uv + (disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy) * uv_size;
2414 | 					suv = clamp(suv, spot_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect.xy, clamp_max);

2416 | 				}

2424 | 			}

2429 | 		}

2456 | #endif

2458 | 	if (sc_use_light_projector() && spot_lights.data[idx].projector_rect != vec4(0.0)) {

2460 | 		splane /= splane.w;
2461 | 
2462 | 		vec2 proj_uv = splane.xy * spot_lights.data[idx].projector_rect.zw;

2485 | #endif
