2500 | #endif
2501 | 
2502 | 			diffuse_light, specular_light);
2503 | }
2504 | 
2505 | 
2506 | void light_process_area(uint idx, vec3 vertex, hvec3 eye_vec, hvec3 normal, vec3 vertex_ddx, vec3 vertex_ddy, hvec3 f0, half roughness, half metallic, float taa_frame_count, hvec3 albedo, inout half alpha, vec2 screen_uv, hvec3 energy_compensation,
2507 | #ifdef LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED
2508 | 		hvec3 backlight,
2509 | #endif
2510 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
2511 | 		hvec4 transmittance_color,
2512 | 		half transmittance_depth,
2513 | 		half transmittance_boost,
2514 | #endif
2515 | #ifdef LIGHT_RIM_USED
2516 | 		half rim, half rim_tint,
2517 | #endif
2518 | #ifdef LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED
2519 | 		half clearcoat, half clearcoat_roughness, hvec3 vertex_normal,
2520 | #endif
2521 | #ifdef LIGHT_ANISOTROPY_USED
2522 | 		hvec3 binormal, hvec3 tangent, half anisotropy,
2523 | #endif
2524 | 		inout hvec3 diffuse_light, inout hvec3 specular_light) {
2525 | 	half EPSILON = half(1e-7);
2526 | 	hvec3 area_width = hvec3(area_lights.data[idx].area_width);
2527 | 	hvec3 area_height = hvec3(area_lights.data[idx].area_height);
2528 | 
2529 | 	if (dot(area_width, area_width) < EPSILON || dot(area_height, area_height) < EPSILON) { 
2530 | 		return;
2531 | 	}
2532 | 
2533 | 	if (dot(area_lights.data[idx].direction, vertex - area_lights.data[idx].position) <= 0) {
2534 | 		return; 
2535 | 	}
2536 | 
2537 | 	half a_len = length(area_width);
2538 | 	half b_len = length(area_height);
2539 | 	half a_half_len = a_len / half(2.0);
2540 | 	half b_half_len = b_len / half(2.0);
2541 | 	hvec3 light_center = hvec3(area_lights.data[idx].position);
2542 | 	hvec3 light_to_vert = hvec3(vertex) - light_center;
2543 | 	hvec3 area_a_dir = normalize(area_width);
2544 | 	hvec3 area_b_dir = normalize(area_height);
2545 | 	hvec3 area_direction = hvec3(area_lights.data[idx].direction);
2546 | 	hvec3 pos_local_to_light = hvec3(dot(light_to_vert, area_a_dir), dot(light_to_vert, area_b_dir), dot(light_to_vert, -area_direction));
2547 | 
2548 | 	hvec3 closest_point_local_to_light = hvec3(clamp(pos_local_to_light.x, -a_half_len, a_half_len), clamp(pos_local_to_light.y, -b_half_len, b_half_len), 0);
2549 | 	half dist = length(closest_point_local_to_light - pos_local_to_light);
2550 | 
2551 | 	half light_length = max(half(0.0), dist);
2552 | 	half light_attenuation_raw = get_omni_attenuation(float(light_length), area_lights.data[idx].inv_radius, area_lights.data[idx].attenuation);
2553 | 	half light_attenuation_ltc = light_attenuation_raw * half(light_length * light_length); 
2554 | 	half shadow = half(1.0);
2555 | 
2556 | #ifndef SHADOWS_DISABLED
2557 | 	
2558 | 	if (light_attenuation_raw > HALF_FLT_MIN && area_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity > 0.001) {
2559 | 		
2560 | 		vec2 texel_size = scene_data_block.data.shadow_atlas_pixel_size;
2561 | 		vec4 base_uv_rect = area_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect;
2562 | 		base_uv_rect.xy += texel_size;
2563 | 		base_uv_rect.zw -= texel_size * 2.0;
2564 | 
2565 | 		vec3 local_vert = (area_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex, 1.0)).xyz;
2566 | 
2567 | 		float shadow_len = length(local_vert); 
2568 | 		vec3 shadow_dir = normalize(local_vert);
2569 | 
2570 | 		vec3 local_normal = normalize(mat3(area_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix) * vec3(normal));
2571 | 		vec3 normal_bias = local_normal * area_lights.data[idx].shadow_normal_bias * (1.0 - abs(dot(local_normal, shadow_dir)));
2572 | 
2573 | 		float inv_center_range = area_lights.data[idx].cone_attenuation;
2574 | 
2575 | 		if (sc_use_light_soft_shadows() && area_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size > 0.0) {
2576 | 			
2577 | 
2578 | 			
2579 | 
2580 | 			float blocker_count = 0.0;
2581 | 			float blocker_average = 0.0;
2582 | 
2583 | 			mat2 disk_rotation;
2584 | 			{
2585 | 				float r = quick_hash(gl_FragCoord.xy + vec2(taa_frame_count * 5.588238)) * 2.0 * M_PI;
2586 | 				float sr = sin(r);
2587 | 				float cr = cos(r);
2588 | 				disk_rotation = mat2(vec2(cr, -sr), vec2(sr, cr));
2589 | 			}
2590 | 
2591 | 			vec3 basis_normal = shadow_dir;
2592 | 			vec3 v0 = abs(basis_normal.z) < 0.999 ? vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0) : vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);
2593 | 			vec3 tangent = normalize(cross(v0, basis_normal));
2594 | 			vec3 bitangent = normalize(cross(tangent, basis_normal));
2595 | 			float z_norm = 1.0 - shadow_len * inv_center_range;
2596 | 
2597 | 			tangent *= area_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size * area_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_scale;
2598 | 			bitangent *= area_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_size * area_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_scale;
2599 | 
2600 | 			SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
2601 | 			for (uint i = 0; i < sc_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
2602 | 				vec2 disk = disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy;
2603 | 
2604 | 				vec3 pos = local_vert + tangent * disk.x + bitangent * disk.y;
2605 | 
2606 | 				pos = normalize(pos);
2607 | 
2608 | 				vec4 uv_rect = base_uv_rect;
2609 | 
2610 | 				pos.z = 1.0 + abs(pos.z);
2611 | 				pos.xy /= pos.z;
2612 | 
2613 | 				pos.xy = pos.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;
2614 | 				pos.xy = uv_rect.xy + pos.xy * uv_rect.zw;
2615 | 
2616 | 				float d = textureLod(sampler2D(shadow_atlas, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), pos.xy, 0.0).r;
2617 | 				if (d > z_norm) {
2618 | 					blocker_average += d;
2619 | 					blocker_count += 1.0;
2620 | 				}
2621 | 			}
2622 | 
2623 | 			if (blocker_count > 0.0) {
2624 | 				
2625 | 				blocker_average /= blocker_count;
2626 | 				float penumbra = (-z_norm + blocker_average) / (1.0 - blocker_average);
2627 | 				tangent *= penumbra;
2628 | 				bitangent *= penumbra;
2629 | 
2630 | 				z_norm += inv_center_range * area_lights.data[idx].shadow_bias;
2631 | 
2632 | 				shadow = half(0.0);
2633 | 
2634 | 				SPEC_CONSTANT_LOOP_ANNOTATION
2635 | 				for (uint i = 0; i < sc_penumbra_shadow_samples(); i++) {
2636 | 					vec2 disk = disk_rotation * scene_data_block.data.penumbra_shadow_kernel[i].xy;
2637 | 					vec3 pos = local_vert + tangent * disk.x + bitangent * disk.y;
2638 | 
2639 | 					pos = normalize(pos);
2640 | 					pos = normalize(pos + normal_bias);
2641 | 
2642 | 					vec4 uv_rect = base_uv_rect;
2643 | 
2644 | 					pos.z = 1.0 + abs(pos.z);
2645 | 					pos.xy /= pos.z;
2646 | 
2647 | 					pos.xy = pos.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;
2648 | 					pos.xy = uv_rect.xy + pos.xy * uv_rect.zw;
2649 | 					shadow += half(textureProj(sampler2DShadow(shadow_atlas, shadow_sampler), vec4(pos.xy, z_norm, 1.0)));
2650 | 				}
2651 | 
2652 | 				shadow /= half(sc_penumbra_shadow_samples());
2653 | 				shadow = mix(half(1.0), shadow, half(area_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity));
2654 | 
2655 | 			} else {
2656 | 				
2657 | 				shadow = half(1.0);
2658 | 			}
2659 | 		} else {
2660 | 			vec4 uv_rect = base_uv_rect;
2661 | 
2662 | 			vec3 shadow_sample = normalize(shadow_dir + normal_bias);
2663 | 
2664 | 			shadow_sample.z = 1.0 + abs(shadow_sample.z);
2665 | 			vec2 pos = shadow_sample.xy / shadow_sample.z;
2666 | 			float depth = shadow_len - area_lights.data[idx].shadow_bias;
2667 | 			depth *= inv_center_range;
2668 | 			depth = 1.0 - depth;
2669 | 			shadow = mix(half(1.0), sample_omni_pcf_shadow(shadow_atlas, area_lights.data[idx].soft_shadow_scale / shadow_sample.z, pos, uv_rect, vec2(0), depth, taa_frame_count), half(area_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity));
2670 | 		}
2671 | 	}
2672 | #endif
2673 | 	light_attenuation_ltc = light_attenuation_ltc * shadow;
2674 | 	half light_attenuation = light_attenuation_raw * shadow;
2675 | 	hvec3 color = hvec3(area_lights.data[idx].color);
2676 | 	float max_mipmap = area_lights.data[idx].cone_angle;
2677 | 
2678 | 	vec3 points[4];
2679 | 	hvec3 h_area_width = area_width / half(2.0);
2680 | 	hvec3 h_area_height = area_height / half(2.0);
2681 | 	points[0] = area_lights.data[idx].position - h_area_width - h_area_height - vertex;
2682 | 	points[1] = area_lights.data[idx].position + h_area_width - h_area_height - vertex;
2683 | 	points[2] = area_lights.data[idx].position + h_area_width + h_area_height - vertex;
2684 | 	points[3] = area_lights.data[idx].position - h_area_width + h_area_height - vertex;
2685 | 
2686 | 	float ltc_diffuse = 0.0;
2687 | 	vec3 ltc_diffuse_tex_color = vec3(1.0);
2688 | 	float ltc_specular = 0.0;
2689 | 	vec3 ltc_specular_tex_color = vec3(1.0);
2690 | 	vec2 ltc_fresnel = vec2(0.0);
2691 | 	hvec3 fresnel_color = hvec3(0.0);
2692 | 	ltc_evaluate(vec3(normal), vec3(eye_vec), mat3(1), points, area_lights.data[idx].projector_rect, max_mipmap, area_light_atlas, SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP, ltc_diffuse, ltc_diffuse_tex_color);
2693 | 
2694 | #if !defined(SPECULAR_DISABLED) || (defined(LIGHT_CODE_USED) && defined(AREA_LIGHT_CODE_USED))
2695 | 	ltc_evaluate_specular(vec3(normal), vec3(eye_vec), roughness, points, area_lights.data[idx].projector_rect, max_mipmap, area_light_atlas, SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP, ltc_lut1, ltc_lut2, ltc_specular, ltc_fresnel, ltc_specular_tex_color);
2696 | 	half f90 = clamp(dot(f0, hvec3(50.0 * 0.33)), metallic, half(1.0));
2697 | 	fresnel_color = f0 * max(half(ltc_fresnel.x), half(0.0)) + (f90 - f0) * max(half(ltc_fresnel.y), half(0.0));
2698 | #endif
2699 | 
2700 | #if defined(LIGHT_CODE_USED) && defined(AREA_LIGHT_CODE_USED)
2701 | 	
2702 | 	mat4 inv_view_matrix = transpose(mat4(scene_data_block.data.inv_view_matrix[0],
2703 | 			scene_data_block.data.inv_view_matrix[1],
2704 | 			scene_data_block.data.inv_view_matrix[2],
2705 | 			vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)));
2706 | 	mat4 read_view_matrix = transpose(mat4(scene_data_block.data.view_matrix[0],
2707 | 			scene_data_block.data.view_matrix[1],
2708 | 			scene_data_block.data.view_matrix[2],
2709 | 			vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)));
2710 | 
2711 | #ifdef USING_MOBILE_RENDERER
2712 | 	uint instance_index = draw_call.instance_index;
2713 | #else
2714 | 	uint instance_index = instance_index_interp;
2715 | #endif
2716 | 
2717 | 	mat4 read_model_matrix = transpose(mat4(instances.data[instance_index].transform[0],
2718 | 			instances.data[instance_index].transform[1],
2719 | 			instances.data[instance_index].transform[2],
2720 | 			vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)));
2721 | 
2722 | #undef projection_matrix
2723 | #define projection_matrix scene_data_block.data.projection_matrix
2724 | #undef inv_projection_matrix
2725 | #define inv_projection_matrix scene_data_block.data.inv_projection_matrix
2726 | 
2727 | 	vec2 read_viewport_size = scene_data_block.data.viewport_size;
2728 | 
2729 | #ifdef LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED
2730 | 	vec3 backlight_highp = vec3(backlight);
2731 | #endif
2732 | 	float roughness_highp = float(roughness);
2733 | 	float metallic_highp = float(metallic);
2734 | 	vec3 albedo_highp = vec3(albedo);
2735 | 	float alpha_highp = float(alpha);
2736 | 	vec3 normal_highp = vec3(normal);
2737 | 	vec3 light_highp = (light_center - vertex) / light_length;
2738 | 	vec3 view_highp = vec3(eye_vec);
2739 | 	float specular_amount_highp = float(area_lights.data[idx].specular_amount);
2740 | 	vec3 light_color_highp = vec3(color);
2741 | 	float attenuation_highp = float(light_attenuation_ltc);
2742 | 	vec3 diffuse_light_highp = vec3(diffuse_light);
2743 | 	vec3 specular_light_highp = vec3(specular_light);
2744 | 	bool is_directional = false;
2745 | 	bool is_area = true;
2746 | 	vec3 area_diffuse = ltc_diffuse * ltc_diffuse_tex_color;
2747 | 	vec3 area_specular = ltc_specular * vec3(fresnel_color) * ltc_specular_tex_color;
2748 | 
2749 | 
2750 | 	alpha = half(alpha_highp);
2751 | 	diffuse_light = hvec3(diffuse_light_highp);
2752 | 	specular_light = hvec3(specular_light_highp);
2753 | 
2754 | #else
2755 | 	half specular_amount = half(area_lights.data[idx].specular_amount);
2756 | 	half area = a_len * b_len;
2757 | 	half cc_attenuation = half(1.0);
2758 | 
2759 | #if defined(LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED) || defined(LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED) || defined(LIGHT_RIM_USED) || defined(DIFFUSE_TOON)
2760 | 	hvec3 isotropic_light_color = hvec3(1.0); 
2761 | 	if (area_lights.data[idx].projector_rect != vec4(0.0)) {
2762 | 		half lod = dist / sqrt(area);
2763 | 		lod = log(half(2048.0) * lod) / log(half(3.0));
2764 | 
2765 | 		hvec2 uv = (closest_point_local_to_light.xy + hvec2(a_half_len, b_half_len)) / hvec2(a_len, b_len);
2766 | 		isotropic_light_color = hvec3(fetch_ltc_lod(vec2(hvec2(1.0) - uv), area_lights.data[idx].projector_rect, float(lod), max_mipmap, area_light_atlas, SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP));
2767 | 	}
2768 | #endif
2769 | #ifdef LIGHT_TRANSMITTANCE_USED
2770 | 	{
2771 | 		half transmittance_z = transmittance_depth;
2772 | 		transmittance_color.a *= light_attenuation_raw; 
2773 | #ifndef SHADOWS_DISABLED
2774 | 		if (area_lights.data[idx].shadow_opacity > 0.001) {
2775 | 			
2776 | 			vec2 texel_size = scene_data_block.data.shadow_atlas_pixel_size;
2777 | 			vec4 uv_rect = area_lights.data[idx].atlas_rect;
2778 | 			uv_rect.xy += texel_size;
2779 | 			uv_rect.zw -= texel_size * 2.0;
2780 | 			vec3 local_vert = (area_lights.data[idx].shadow_matrix * vec4(vertex - vec3(normal) * area_lights.data[idx].transmittance_bias, 1.0)).xyz;
2781 | 
2782 | 			float shadow_len = length(local_vert); 
2783 | 			vec3 shadow_sample = normalize(local_vert);
2784 | 
2785 | 			shadow_sample.z = 1.0 + abs(shadow_sample.z);
2786 | 			vec2 pos = shadow_sample.xy / shadow_sample.z;
2787 | 			float inv_center_range = area_lights.data[idx].cone_attenuation;
2788 | 			float depth = shadow_len * inv_center_range;
2789 | 			depth = 1.0 - depth;
2790 | 
2791 | 			pos = pos * 0.5 + 0.5;
2792 | 			pos = uv_rect.xy + pos * uv_rect.zw;
2793 | 			float shadow_z = textureLod(sampler2D(shadow_atlas, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), pos, 0.0).r;
2794 | 			transmittance_z = half((depth - shadow_z) / inv_center_range);
2795 | 		}
2796 | #endif
2797 | 		
2798 | #ifdef SSS_MODE_SKIN
2799 | 		diffuse_light += SSS_skin(half(ltc_diffuse), transmittance_depth, transmittance_z, transmittance_boost, transmittance_color, color * isotropic_light_color * area);
2800 | #else
2801 | 		diffuse_light += SSS(half(ltc_diffuse), transmittance_depth, transmittance_z, transmittance_boost, transmittance_color, color * isotropic_light_color * area);
2802 | #endif
2803 | 	}
2804 | #endif 
2805 | 
2806 | #if defined(LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED) || defined(LIGHT_RIM_USED) || defined(DIFFUSE_TOON) || defined(SPECULAR_TOON)
2807 | 	float solid_angle = quad_solid_angle(points);
2808 | #endif
2809 | 
2810 | #if defined(LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED)
2811 | 	vec3 cc_specular_tex_color = vec3(1.0);
2812 | 	float cc_specular_ltc = 0.0;
2813 | 	vec2 cc_fresnel;
2814 | 	ltc_evaluate_specular(vec3(vertex_normal), vec3(eye_vec), sqrt(mix(0.001, 0.1, float(clearcoat_roughness))), points, area_lights.data[idx].projector_rect, max_mipmap, area_light_atlas, SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP, ltc_lut1, ltc_lut2, cc_specular_ltc, cc_fresnel, cc_specular_tex_color);
2815 | 	half Fr = (half(0.04) * max(half(cc_fresnel.x), half(0.0)) + half(1.0 - 0.04) * max(half(cc_fresnel.y), half(0.0))) * clearcoat;
2816 | 	cc_attenuation = half(1.0) - Fr;
2817 | 	specular_light += half(cc_specular_ltc) * hvec3(cc_specular_tex_color) * Fr * color * light_attenuation_ltc * specular_amount;
2818 | #endif 
2819 | 
2820 | 	if (metallic < half(1.0)) {
2821 | #if defined(DIFFUSE_TOON)
2822 | 		float backface_ltc_diffuse = 0.0;
2823 | 		vec3 backface_ltc_tex_color_discard = vec3(1.0);
2824 | 		ltc_evaluate(vec3(-normal), vec3(eye_vec), mat3(1), points, vec4(0.0), max_mipmap, area_light_atlas, SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP, backface_ltc_diffuse, backface_ltc_tex_color_discard);
2825 | 		half NdotL = half((ltc_diffuse - backface_ltc_diffuse) / (max(solid_angle, 0.001) / M_PI));
2826 | 		half diffuse_brdf_NL = smoothstep(-roughness, max(roughness, half(0.01)), NdotL) * half(1.0 / M_PI);
2827 | 		diffuse_light += diffuse_brdf_NL * isotropic_light_color * color * area * light_attenuation * cc_attenuation;
2828 | #else
2829 | 		diffuse_light += half(ltc_diffuse) * hvec3(ltc_diffuse_tex_color) * color * light_attenuation_ltc * cc_attenuation;
2830 | #endif 
2831 | 
2832 | #if defined(LIGHT_BACKLIGHT_USED)
2833 | 		
2834 | 		diffuse_light += color * max(half(solid_angle / M_PI) - half(ltc_diffuse), half(0.0)) * backlight * isotropic_light_color * light_attenuation_ltc;
2835 | #endif
2836 | 	}
2837 | 
2838 | #if defined(LIGHT_RIM_USED) 
2839 | 	half cNdotV = max(dot(normal, eye_vec), half(1e-4));
2840 | 	half rim_light = pow(max(half(1e-4), half(1.0) - cNdotV), max(half(0.0), (half(1.0) - roughness) * half(16.0)));
2841 | 	diffuse_light += rim_light * rim * mix(hvec3(1.0), albedo, rim_tint) * isotropic_light_color * color * half(solid_angle) * light_attenuation_ltc;
2842 | #endif
2843 | 
2844 | #if defined(SPECULAR_TOON)
2845 | 	
2846 | 	half mid = half(1.0) - roughness;
2847 | 	mid *= mid;
2848 | 
2849 | 	half RdotV = half(ltc_specular / (max(solid_angle, 0.001) / (M_PI)));
2850 | 	half intensity = smoothstep(mid - roughness * half(0.5), mid + roughness * half(0.5), RdotV) * mid; 
2851 | 	diffuse_light += intensity * hvec3(ltc_specular_tex_color) * color * area * light_attenuation * specular_amount; 
2852 | #elif defined(SPECULAR_DISABLED)
2853 | 	
2854 | #else
2855 | 	hvec3 spec = half(ltc_specular) * hvec3(ltc_specular_tex_color) * color * fresnel_color;
2856 | 	specular_light += spec * specular_amount * light_attenuation_ltc * cc_attenuation;
2857 | #endif 
2858 | 
2859 | #endif 
2860 | }
2861 | 
2862 | void reflection_process(uint ref_index, vec3 vertex, hvec3 ref_vec, hvec3 normal, half roughness, hvec3 ambient_light,
2863 | #ifdef LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED
2864 | 		hvec3 cc_ref_vec, half cc_roughness, inout hvec4 cc_reflection_accum,
2865 | #endif
2866 | 		inout hvec4 ambient_accum, inout hvec4 reflection_accum) {
2867 | 	vec3 box_extents = reflections.data[ref_index].box_extents;
2868 | 	vec3 local_pos = (reflections.data[ref_index].local_matrix * vec4(vertex, 1.0)).xyz;
2869 | 
2870 | 	if (any(greaterThan(abs(local_pos), box_extents))) { 
2871 | 		return;
2872 | 	}
2873 | 
2874 | 	half blend = half(1.0);
2875 | 	if (reflections.data[ref_index].blend_distance != 0.0) {
2876 | 		vec3 axis_blend_distance = min(vec3(reflections.data[ref_index].blend_distance), box_extents);
2877 | 		vec3 blend_axes_highp = abs(local_pos) - box_extents + axis_blend_distance;
2878 | 		hvec3 blend_axes = hvec3(blend_axes_highp / axis_blend_distance);
2879 | 		blend_axes = clamp(half(1.0) - blend_axes, hvec3(0.0), hvec3(1.0));
2880 | 		blend = pow(blend_axes.x * blend_axes.y * blend_axes.z, half(2.0));
2881 | 	}
2882 |

3005 | vec4 voxel_cone_trace(texture3D probe, vec3 cell_size, vec3 pos, vec3 direction, float tan_half_angle, float max_distance, float p_bias) {
3006 | 	float dist = p_bias;
3007 | 	vec4 color = vec4(0.0);
3008 | 
3009 | 	while (dist < max_distance && color.a < 0.95) {
3010 | 		float diameter = max(1.0, 2.0 * tan_half_angle * dist);
3011 | 		vec3 uvw_pos = (pos + dist * direction) * cell_size;
3012 | 		float half_diameter = diameter * 0.5;
3013 | 		
3014 | 		if (any(greaterThan(abs(uvw_pos - 0.5), vec3(0.5f + half_diameter * cell_size)))) {
3015 | 			break;
3016 | 		}
3017 | 		vec4 scolor = textureLod(sampler3D(probe, DEFAULT_SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP), uvw_pos, log2(diameter));
3018 | 		float a = (1.0 - color.a);
3019 | 		color += a * scolor;
3020 | 		dist += half_diameter;
3021 | 	}
3022 | 
3023 | 	return color;
3024 | }
3025 | 
3026 | vec4 voxel_cone_trace_45_degrees(texture3D probe, vec3 cell_size, vec3 pos, vec3 direction, float tan_half_angle, float max_distance, float p_bias) {
3027 | 	float dist = p_bias;
3028 | 	vec4 color = vec4(0.0);
3029 | 	float radius = max(0.5, tan_half_angle * dist);
3030 | 	float lod_level = log2(radius * 2.0);
3031 | 
3032 | 	while (dist < max_distance && color.a < 0.95) {
3033 | 		vec3 uvw_pos = (pos + dist * direction) * cell_size;
3034 | 
3035 | 		
3036 | 		if (any(greaterThan(abs(uvw_pos - 0.5), vec3(0.5f + radius * cell_size)))) {
3037 | 			break;
3038 | 		}
3039 | 		vec4 scolor = textureLod(sampler3D(probe, DEFAULT_SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP), uvw_pos, lod_level);
3040 | 		lod_level += 1.0;
3041 | 
3042 | 		float a = (1.0 - color.a);
3043 | 		scolor *= a;
3044 | 		color += scolor;
3045 | 		dist += radius;
3046 | 		radius = max(0.5, tan_half_angle * dist);
3047 | 	}
3048 | 
3049 | 	return color;
3050 | }

3052 | void voxel_gi_compute(uint index, vec3 position, vec3 normal, vec3 ref_vec, mat3 normal_xform, float roughness, vec3 ambient, vec3 environment, inout vec4 out_spec, inout vec4 out_diff) {
3053 | 	position = (voxel_gi_instances.data[index].xform * vec4(position, 1.0)).xyz;
3054 | 	ref_vec = normalize((voxel_gi_instances.data[index].xform * vec4(ref_vec, 0.0)).xyz);
3055 | 	normal = normalize((voxel_gi_instances.data[index].xform * vec4(normal, 0.0)).xyz);
3056 | 
3057 | 	position += normal * voxel_gi_instances.data[index].normal_bias;
3058 | 
3059 | 	
3060 | 	if (any(bvec2(any(lessThan(position, vec3(0.0))), any(greaterThan(position, voxel_gi_instances.data[index].bounds))))) {
3061 | 		return;
3062 | 	}
3063 | 
3064 | 	vec3 blendv = abs(position / voxel_gi_instances.data[index].bounds * 2.0 - 1.0);
3065 | 	float blend = clamp(1.0 - max(blendv.x, max(blendv.y, blendv.z)), 0.0, 1.0);
3066 | 	
3067 | 
3068 | 	float max_distance = length(voxel_gi_instances.data[index].bounds);
3069 | 	vec3 cell_size = 1.0 / voxel_gi_instances.data[index].bounds;
3070 | 
3071 | 	
3072 | 
3073 | #define MAX_CONE_DIRS 4
3074 | 
3075 | 	vec3 cone_dirs[MAX_CONE_DIRS] = vec3[](
3076 | 			vec3(0.707107, 0.0, 0.707107),
3077 | 			vec3(0.0, 0.707107, 0.707107),
3078 | 			vec3(-0.707107, 0.0, 0.707107),
3079 | 			vec3(0.0, -0.707107, 0.707107));
3080 | 
3081 | 	float cone_weights[MAX_CONE_DIRS] = float[](0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25);
3082 | 	float cone_angle_tan = 0.98269;
3083 | 
3084 | 	vec3 light = vec3(0.0);
3085 | 
3086 | 	for (int i = 0; i < MAX_CONE_DIRS; i++) {
3087 | 		vec3 dir = normalize((voxel_gi_instances.data[index].xform * vec4(normal_xform * cone_dirs[i], 0.0)).xyz);
3088 | 
3089 | 		vec4 cone_light = voxel_cone_trace_45_degrees(voxel_gi_textures[index], cell_size, position, dir, cone_angle_tan, max_distance, voxel_gi_instances.data[index].bias);
3090 | 
3091 | 		if (voxel_gi_instances.data[index].blend_ambient) {
3092 | 			cone_light.rgb = mix(ambient, cone_light.rgb, min(1.0, cone_light.a / 0.95));
3093 | 		}
3094 | 
3095 | 		light += cone_weights[i] * cone_light.rgb;
3096 | 	}
3097 | 
3098 | 	light *= voxel_gi_instances.data[index].dynamic_range * voxel_gi_instances.data[index].exposure_normalization;
3099 | 	out_diff += vec4(light * blend, blend);
3100 | 
3101 | 	
3102 | 	vec4 irr_light = voxel_cone_trace(voxel_gi_textures[index], cell_size, position, ref_vec, tan(roughness * 0.5 * M_PI * 0.99), max_distance, voxel_gi_instances.data[index].bias);
3103 | 	if (voxel_gi_instances.data[index].blend_ambient) {
3104 | 		irr_light.rgb = mix(environment, irr_light.rgb, min(1.0, irr_light.a / 0.95));
3105 | 	}
3106 | 	irr_light.rgb *= voxel_gi_instances.data[index].dynamic_range * voxel_gi_instances.data[index].exposure_normalization;
3107 | 	
3108 | 
3109 | 	out_spec += vec4(irr_light.rgb * blend, blend);
3110 | }

3112 | vec2 octahedron_wrap(vec2 v) {
3113 | 	vec2 signVal;
3114 | 	signVal.x = v.x >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : -1.0;
3115 | 	signVal.y = v.y >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : -1.0;
3116 | 	return (1.0 - abs(v.yx)) * signVal;
3117 | }
3118 | 
3119 | vec2 octahedron_encode(vec3 n) {
3120 | 	
3121 | 	n /= (abs(n.x) + abs(n.y) + abs(n.z));
3122 | 	n.xy = n.z >= 0.0 ? n.xy : octahedron_wrap(n.xy);
3123 | 	n.xy = n.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;
3124 | 	return n.xy;
3125 | }

3127 | void sdfgi_process(uint cascade, vec3 cascade_pos, vec3 cam_pos, vec3 cam_normal, vec3 cam_specular_normal, bool use_specular, float roughness, out vec3 diffuse_light, out vec3 specular_light, out float blend) {
3128 | 	cascade_pos += cam_normal * sdfgi.normal_bias;
3129 | 
3130 | 	vec3 base_pos = floor(cascade_pos);
3131 | 	
3132 | 	ivec3 probe_base_pos = ivec3(base_pos);
3133 | 
3134 | 	vec4 diffuse_accum = vec4(0.0);
3135 | 	vec3 specular_accum;
3136 | 
3137 | 	ivec3 tex_pos = ivec3(probe_base_pos.xy, int(cascade));
3138 | 	tex_pos.x += probe_base_pos.z * sdfgi.probe_axis_size;
3139 | 	tex_pos.xy = tex_pos.xy * (SDFGI_OCT_SIZE + 2) + ivec2(1);
3140 | 
3141 | 	vec3 diffuse_posf = (vec3(tex_pos) + vec3(octahedron_encode(cam_normal) * float(SDFGI_OCT_SIZE), 0.0)) * sdfgi.lightprobe_tex_pixel_size;
3142 | 
3143 | 	vec3 specular_posf;
3144 | 
3145 | 	if (use_specular) {
3146 | 		specular_accum = vec3(0.0);
3147 | 		specular_posf = (vec3(tex_pos) + vec3(octahedron_encode(cam_specular_normal) * float(SDFGI_OCT_SIZE), 0.0)) * sdfgi.lightprobe_tex_pixel_size;
3148 | 	}
3149 | 
3150 | 	vec4 light_accum = vec4(0.0);
3151 | 	float weight_accum = 0.0;
3152 | 
3153 | 	for (uint j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
3154 | 		ivec3 offset = (ivec3(j) >> ivec3(0, 1, 2)) & ivec3(1, 1, 1);
3155 | 		ivec3 probe_posi = probe_base_pos;
3156 | 		probe_posi += offset;
3157 | 
3158 | 		
3159 | 
3160 | 		vec3 probe_pos = vec3(probe_posi);
3161 | 		vec3 probe_to_pos = cascade_pos - probe_pos;
3162 | 		vec3 probe_dir = normalize(-probe_to_pos);
3163 | 
3164 | 		vec3 trilinear = vec3(1.0) - abs(probe_to_pos);
3165 | 		float weight = trilinear.x * trilinear.y * trilinear.z * max(0.005, dot(cam_normal, probe_dir));
3166 | 
3167 | 		
3168 | 
3169 | 		if (sdfgi.use_occlusion) {
3170 | 			ivec3 occ_indexv = abs((sdfgi.cascades[cascade].probe_world_offset + probe_posi) & ivec3(1, 1, 1)) * ivec3(1, 2, 4);
3171 | 			vec4 occ_mask = mix(vec4(0.0), vec4(1.0), equal(ivec4(occ_indexv.x | occ_indexv.y), ivec4(0, 1, 2, 3)));
3172 | 
3173 | 			vec3 occ_pos = clamp(cascade_pos, probe_pos - sdfgi.occlusion_clamp, probe_pos + sdfgi.occlusion_clamp) * sdfgi.probe_to_uvw;
3174 | 			occ_pos.z += float(cascade);
3175 | 			if (occ_indexv.z != 0) { 
3176 | 				occ_pos.x += 1.0;
3177 | 			}
3178 | 
3179 | 			occ_pos *= sdfgi.occlusion_renormalize;
3180 | 			float occlusion = dot(textureLod(sampler3D(sdfgi_occlusion_cascades, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), occ_pos, 0.0), occ_mask);
3181 | 
3182 | 			weight *= max(occlusion, 0.01);
3183 | 		}
3184 | 
3185 | 		
3186 | 
3187 | 		vec3 diffuse;
3188 | 		vec3 pos_uvw = diffuse_posf;
3189 | 		pos_uvw.xy += vec2(offset.xy) * sdfgi.lightprobe_uv_offset.xy;
3190 | 		pos_uvw.x += float(offset.z) * sdfgi.lightprobe_uv_offset.z;
3191 | 		diffuse = textureLod(sampler2DArray(sdfgi_lightprobe_texture, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), pos_uvw, 0.0).rgb;
3192 | 
3193 | 		diffuse_accum += vec4(diffuse * weight * sdfgi.cascades[cascade].exposure_normalization, weight);
3194 | 
3195 | 		if (use_specular) {
3196 | 			vec3 specular = vec3(0.0);
3197 | 			vec3 pos_uvw = specular_posf;
3198 | 			pos_uvw.xy += vec2(offset.xy) * sdfgi.lightprobe_uv_offset.xy;
3199 | 			pos_uvw.x += float(offset.z) * sdfgi.lightprobe_uv_offset.z;
3200 | 			if (roughness < 0.99) {
3201 | 				specular = textureLod(sampler2DArray(sdfgi_lightprobe_texture, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), pos_uvw + vec3(0, 0, float(sdfgi.max_cascades)), 0.0).rgb;
3202 | 			}
3203 | 			if (roughness > 0.5) {
3204 | 				specular = mix(specular, textureLod(sampler2DArray(sdfgi_lightprobe_texture, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), pos_uvw, 0.0).rgb, (roughness - 0.5) * 2.0);
3205 | 			}
3206 | 
3207 | 			specular_accum += specular * weight * sdfgi.cascades[cascade].exposure_normalization;
3208 | 		}
3209 | 	}
3210 | 
3211 | 	if (diffuse_accum.a > 0.0) {
3212 | 		diffuse_accum.rgb /= diffuse_accum.a;
3213 | 	}
3214 | 
3215 | 	diffuse_light = diffuse_accum.rgb;
3216 | 
3217 | 	if (use_specular) {
3218 | 		if (diffuse_accum.a > 0.0) {
3219 | 			specular_accum /= diffuse_accum.a;
3220 | 		}
3221 | 
3222 | 		specular_light = specular_accum;
3223 | 	}
3224 | 
3225 | 	{
3226 | 		
3227 | 		float blend_from = (float(sdfgi.probe_axis_size - 1) / 2.0) - 2.5;
3228 | 		float blend_to = blend_from + 2.0;
3229 | 
3230 | 		vec3 inner_pos = cam_pos * sdfgi.cascades[cascade].to_probe;
3231 | 
3232 | 		float len = length(inner_pos);
3233 | 
3234 | 		inner_pos = abs(normalize(inner_pos));
3235 | 		len *= max(inner_pos.x, max(inner_pos.y, inner_pos.z));
3236 | 
3237 | 		if (len >= blend_from) {
3238 | 			blend = smoothstep(blend_from, blend_to, len);
3239 | 		} else {
3240 | 			blend = 0.0;
3241 | 		}
3242 | 	}
3243 | }
3244 |

3249 | vec4 volumetric_fog_process(vec2 screen_uv, float z) {
3250 | 	vec3 fog_pos = vec3(screen_uv, z * implementation_data.volumetric_fog_inv_length);
3251 | 	if (fog_pos.z < 0.0) {
3252 | 		return vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
3253 | 	} else if (fog_pos.z < 1.0) {
3254 | 		fog_pos.z = pow(fog_pos.z, implementation_data.volumetric_fog_detail_spread);
3255 | 	}
3256 | 
3257 | 	return texture(sampler3D(volumetric_fog_texture, SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP), fog_pos);

3260 | vec4 fog_process(vec3 vertex) {
3261 | 	vec3 fog_color = scene_data_block.data.fog_light_color;
3262 | 
3263 | 	if (scene_data_block.data.fog_aerial_perspective > 0.0) {
3264 | 		vec3 sky_fog_color = vec3(0.0);
3265 | 		vec3 cube_view = scene_data_block.data.radiance_inverse_xform * vertex;
3266 | 		
3267 | 		float mip_level = mix(1.0 / MAX_ROUGHNESS_LOD, 1.0, 1.0 - (abs(vertex.z) - scene_data_block.data.z_near) / (scene_data_block.data.z_far - scene_data_block.data.z_near));
3268 | #ifdef USE_RADIANCE_OCTMAP_ARRAY
3269 | 		float roughness_lod, blend;
3270 | 		blend = modf(mip_level * MAX_ROUGHNESS_LOD, roughness_lod);
3271 | 		float cube_lod = vec3_to_oct_lod(dFdx(cube_view), dFdy(cube_view), scene_data_block.data.radiance_pixel_size);
3272 | 		vec2 cube_uv = vec3_to_oct_with_border(cube_view, vec2(scene_data_block.data.radiance_border_size, 1.0 - scene_data_block.data.radiance_border_size * 2.0));
3273 | 		vec3 sky_sample_a = textureLod(sampler2DArray(radiance_octmap, DEFAULT_SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP), vec3(cube_uv, roughness_lod), cube_lod).rgb;
3274 | 		vec3 sky_sample_b = textureLod(sampler2DArray(radiance_octmap, DEFAULT_SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP), vec3(cube_uv, roughness_lod + 1), cube_lod).rgb;
3275 | 		sky_fog_color = mix(sky_sample_a, sky_sample_b, blend);
3276 | #else
3277 | 		float roughness_lod = mip_level * MAX_ROUGHNESS_LOD;
3278 | 		vec2 cube_uv = vec3_to_oct_with_border(cube_view, vec2(scene_data_block.data.radiance_border_size, 1.0 - scene_data_block.data.radiance_border_size * 2.0));
3279 | 		sky_fog_color = textureLod(sampler2D(radiance_octmap, DEFAULT_SAMPLER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_CLAMP), cube_uv, roughness_lod).rgb;
3280 | #endif

3284 | 	if (scene_data_block.data.fog_sun_scatter > 0.001) {

3287 | 		vec3 view = normalize(vertex);
3288 | 
3289 | 		for (uint i = 0; i < scene_data_block.data.directional_light_count; i++) {
3290 | 			vec3 light_color = directional_lights.data[i].color * directional_lights.data[i].energy;
3291 | 			float light_amount = pow(max(dot(view, directional_lights.data[i].direction), 0.0), 8.0);
3292 | 			fog_color += light_color * light_amount * scene_data_block.data.fog_sun_scatter;
3293 | 		}
3294 | 	}
3295 | 
3296 | 	float fog_amount = 0.0;
3297 | 
3298 | 	if (sc_use_depth_fog()) {
3299 | 		float fog_z = smoothstep(scene_data_block.data.fog_depth_begin, scene_data_block.data.fog_depth_end, length(vertex));
3300 | 		float fog_quad_amount = pow(fog_z, scene_data_block.data.fog_depth_curve) * scene_data_block.data.fog_density;
3301 | 		fog_amount = fog_quad_amount;
3302 | 	} else {
3303 | 		fog_amount = 1 - exp(min(0.0, -length(vertex) * scene_data_block.data.fog_density));
3304 | 	}
3305 | 
3306 | 	if (abs(scene_data_block.data.fog_height_density) >= 0.0001) {
3307 | 		mat4 inv_view_matrix = transpose(mat4(scene_data_block.data.inv_view_matrix[0],
3308 | 				scene_data_block.data.inv_view_matrix[1],
3309 | 				scene_data_block.data.inv_view_matrix[2],
3310 | 				vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)));
3311 | 
3312 | 		float y = (inv_view_matrix * vec4(vertex, 1.0)).y;
3313 | 
3314 | 		float y_dist = y - scene_data_block.data.fog_height;
3315 | 
3316 | 		float vfog_amount = 1.0 - exp(min(0.0, y_dist * scene_data_block.data.fog_height_density));
3317 | 
3318 | 		fog_amount = max(vfog_amount, fog_amount);
3319 | 	}
3320 | 
3321 | 	return vec4(fog_color, fog_amount);
3322 | }
3323 | 
3324 | void cluster_get_item_range(uint p_offset, out uint item_min, out uint item_max, out uint item_from, out uint item_to) {
3325 | 	uint item_min_max = cluster_buffer.data[p_offset];
3326 | 	item_min = item_min_max & 0xFFFFu;
3327 | 	item_max = item_min_max >> 16;
3328 | 
3329 | 	item_from = item_min >> 5;
3330 | 	item_to = (item_max == 0) ? 0 : ((item_max - 1) >> 5) + 1; 
3331 | }
3332 | 
3333 | uint cluster_get_range_clip_mask(uint i, uint z_min, uint z_max) {
3334 | 	int local_min = clamp(int(z_min) - int(i) * 32, 0, 31);
3335 | 	int mask_width = min(int(z_max) - int(z_min), 32 - local_min);
3336 | 	return bitfieldInsert(uint(0), uint(0xFFFFFFFF), local_min, mask_width);
3337 | }
3338 | 
3339 | #endif 
3340 | 
3341 | #if defined(MODE_RENDER_NORMAL_ROUGHNESS) || defined(MODE_RENDER_MATERIAL)
3342 | 
3343 | vec3 encode24(vec3 v) {
3344 | 	
3345 | 	vec3 vNormalUns = abs(v);
3346 | 	
3347 | 	float maxNAbs = max(vNormalUns.z, max(vNormalUns.x, vNormalUns.y));
3348 | 	
3349 | 	vec2 vTexCoord = vNormalUns.z < maxNAbs ? (vNormalUns.y < maxNAbs ? vNormalUns.yz : vNormalUns.xz) : vNormalUns.xy;
3350 | 	vTexCoord /= maxNAbs;
3351 | 	vTexCoord = vTexCoord.x < vTexCoord.y ? vTexCoord.yx : vTexCoord.xy;
3352 | 	
3353 | 	vTexCoord.y /= vTexCoord.x;
3354 | 	float fFittingScale = texture(sampler2D(best_fit_normal_texture, SAMPLER_NEAREST_CLAMP), vTexCoord).r;
3355 | 	
3356 | 	vec3 result = v / maxNAbs;
3357 | 	
3358 | 	result *= fFittingScale;
3359 | 	return result;
3360 | }
3361 | #endif

3749 |

3758 | 			while (merged_mask != 0) {

3760 | 				merged_mask &= ~(1u << bit);
3761 | 
3762 | 				if (((1u << bit) & mask) == 0) { 
3763 | 					continue;
3764 | 				}

3767 | 
3768 | 				if (!bool(decals.data[decal_index].mask & instances.data[instance_index].layer_mask)) {

3778 | 
3779 | 				if (decals.data[decal_index].normal_fade > 0.0) {
3780 | 					fade *= smoothstep(decals.data[decal_index].normal_fade, 1.0, dot(geo_normal, decals.data[decal_index].normal) * 0.5 + 0.5);
3781 | 				}
3782 |

4130 | 			}
4131 | 		}
4132 | 	}
4133 | 
4134 | 	if (sc_use_forward_gi() && bool(instances.data[instance_index].flags & INSTANCE_FLAGS_USE_VOXEL_GI)) {

4136 | 		
4137 | 		vec3 cam_pos = mat3(inv_view_matrix) * vertex;

4155 | 		}

4189 | #endif

4225 | 	{

4233 | 		uint cluster_reflection_offset = cluster_offset + implementation_data.cluster_type_size * 4;

4241 | 
4242 | 		item_from = subgroupBroadcastFirst(subgroupMin(item_from));
4243 | 		item_to = subgroupBroadcastFirst(subgroupMax(item_to));
4244 | 
4245 | #ifdef LIGHT_ANISOTROPY_USED
4246 | 		
4247 | 		vec3 anisotropic_direction = anisotropy >= 0.0 ? binormal : tangent;

4253 | #endif
4254 | 		vec3 ref_vec = normalize(reflect(-view, bent_normal));

4260 | 			mask &= cluster_get_range_clip_mask(i, item_min, item_max);
4261 | 
4262 | 			uint merged_mask = subgroupBroadcastFirst(subgroupOr(mask));
4263 | 			while (merged_mask != 0) {
4264 | 				uint bit = findMSB(merged_mask);
4265 | 				merged_mask &= ~(1u << bit);
4266 | 
4267 | 				if (((1u << bit) & mask) == 0) { 
4268 | 					continue;
4269 | 				}

4271 | 				uint reflection_index = 32 * i + bit;
4272 | 
4273 | 				if (!bool(reflections.data[reflection_index].mask & instances.data[instance_index].layer_mask)) {
4274 | 					continue; 
4275 | 				}

4277 | #ifndef LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED
4278 | 				if (reflection_accum.a >= 1.0 && ambient_accum.a >= 1.0) {

4280 | 				}

4283 | 					break;
4284 | 				}

4297 | 		}

4300 | 			reflection_accum.rgb = indirect_specular_light * (1.0 - reflection_accum.a) + reflection_accum.rgb;

4307 | #endif
4308 | 
4309 | 		if (reflection_accum.a > 0.0) {

4313 | #ifdef LIGHT_CLEARCOAT_USED
4314 | 		if (cc_reflection_accum.a > 0.0) {

4326 | 	
4327 | 	{
4328 | 		ambient_light *= ao;
4329 | #ifndef SPECULAR_OCCLUSION_DISABLED
4330 | #ifdef BENT_NORMAL_MAP_USED
4331 | 		
4332 | 		
4333 | 		float cos_a_v = sqrt(1.0 - ao);
4334 | 		float limited_roughness = max(roughness, 0.01); 
4335 | 		float cos_a_s = exp2((-log(10.0) / log(2.0)) * limited_roughness * limited_roughness);
4336 | 		float cos_b = dot(bent_normal_vector, reflect(-view, normal));
4337 | 
4338 | 		
4339 | 		
4340 | 		
4341 | 		float r1 = acos(cos_a_v);
4342 | 		float r2 = acos(cos_a_s);
4343 | 		float d = acos(cos_b);
4344 | 		float area = 0.0;
4345 | 
4346 | 		if (d <= max(r1, r2) - min(r1, r2)) {
4347 | 			
4348 | 			area = M_TAU - M_TAU * max(cos_a_v, cos_a_s);
4349 | 		} else if (d >= r1 + r2) {
4350 | 			
4351 | 			area = 0.0;
4352 | 		} else {
4353 | 			float delta = abs(r1 - r2);
4354 | 			float x = 1.0 - clamp((d - delta) / (r1 + r2 - delta), 0.0, 1.0);
4355 | 			area = smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, x);
4356 | 			area *= M_TAU - M_TAU * max(cos_a_v, cos_a_s);
4357 | 		}
4358 | 
4359 | 		float specular_occlusion = area / (M_TAU * (1.0 - cos_a_s));
4360 | 		indirect_specular_light *= specular_occlusion;
4361 | #else 
4362 | 		float specular_occlusion = (ambient_light.r * 0.3 + ambient_light.g * 0.59 + ambient_light.b * 0.11) * 2.0; 
4363 | 		specular_occlusion = min(specular_occlusion * 4.0, 1.0); 
4364 | 
4365 | 		float reflective_f = (1.0 - roughness) * metallic;
4366 | 		
4367 | 		
4368 | 		specular_occlusion = max(min(reflective_f * specular_occlusion * 10.0, 1.0), specular_occlusion);
4369 | 		indirect_specular_light *= specular_occlusion;
4370 | #endif 
4371 | #endif

4578 | 								shadow = s;

4580 | 								
4581 | 								float blend = smoothstep(0.0, directional_lights.data[i].shadow_split_offsets.x, depth_z);
4582 | 								shadow = mix(shadow, s, blend);
4583 | 							}
4584 | 
4585 | 							blend_count++;
4586 | 						}
4587 | 
4588 | 						if (blend_count < blend_max && depth_z < directional_lights.data[i].shadow_split_offsets.z) {
4589 | 							vec4 v = vec4(vertex, 1.0);
4590 | 
4591 | 							BIAS_FUNC(v, 2)
4592 | 
4593 | 							vec4 pssm_coord = (directional_lights.data[i].shadow_matrix3 * v);
4594 | 							pssm_coord /= pssm_coord.w;

4600 | 							float s = sample_directional_soft_shadow(directional_shadow_atlas, pssm_coord.xyz, tex_scale * directional_lights.data[i].soft_shadow_scale, scene_data.taa_frame_count);
4601 | 
4602 | 							if (blend_count == 0) {
4603 | 								shadow = s;
4604 | 							} else {
4605 | 								
4606 | 								float blend = smoothstep(directional_lights.data[i].shadow_split_offsets.x, directional_lights.data[i].shadow_split_offsets.y, depth_z);
4607 | 								shadow = mix(shadow, s, blend);
4608 | 							}
4609 |

4611 | 						}
4612 |

4617 |

4619 | 							pssm_coord /= pssm_coord.w;

4629 | 							} else {

4645 | 
4646 | 							pssm_coord = (directional_lights.data[i].shadow_matrix1 * v);

4694 |

5107 | 		const vec3 aniso_dir[6] = vec3[](
5108 | 				vec3(1, 0, 0),
5109 | 				vec3(0, 1, 0),
5110 | 				vec3(0, 0, 1),
5111 | 				vec3(-1, 0, 0),
5112 | 				vec3(0, -1, 0),

5114 | 
5115 | 		vec3 cam_normal = mat3(inv_view_matrix) * normalize(normal_interp);
5116 | 
5117 | 		float closest_dist = -1e20;
5118 | 
5119 | 		for (uint i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
5120 | 			float d = dot(cam_normal, aniso_dir[i]);
5121 | 			if (d > closest_dist) {
5122 | 				closest_dist = d;
5123 | 				facing_bits = (1 << i);
5124 | 			}
5125 | 		}

5131 | #endif

5133 | 		if (length(emission) > 0.001) {

5137 | 			for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
5138 | 				float strength = max(0.0, dot(cam_normal, aniso_dir[i]));
5139 | 				vec3 light = emission * strength;

5142 | 			}

5154 | 			const float pow2to9 = 512.0f;

5162 | 
5163 | 			float cMax = max(cRed, max(cGreen, cBlue));

5167 | 			float sMax = floor((cMax / pow(2.0f, expp - B - N)) + 0.5f);

5169 | 			float exps = expp + 1.0f;

5177 | 			float sBlue = floor((cBlue / pow(2.0f, exps - B - N)) + 0.5f);
5178 |
